The owner merges it · **Token:** `HOMEBREW_TAP_TOKEN` is a fine-grained PAT for the tap repo only (contents + pull requests: write, nothing else), stored in a GitHub Environment `tap` that needs the owner's approval for each run; the tap's `main` is protected (pull requests only, no force push). Creating the tap, the token, publishing a release and every merge is **publishing**, so it is human-gated (§22.1)
- **The formula has no `service do` block** (no `brew services`); its caveats say `wraith-node service install` and, after `brew upgrade wraith-node`, `wraith-node service restart`. A restart leaves every pane running in herdr-core (§7.6)
- **Sprite path across upgrades:** the node starts the sprite at `<bin-dir>/wraith-sprite`, where `<bin-dir>` is a **stable folder** passed as `wraith-node start --bin-dir <dir>` (`process.execPath` is already symlink-resolved, so it would point into a versioned folder that `brew upgrade` deletes): Homebrew: `$(brew --prefix)/opt/wraith-node/bin`, which `service install` derives when the node runs from `<prefix>/Cellar/wraith-node/<v>/bin`; Wraith.app: the bundle's `Contents/MacOS` (passed by the Rust shell); tarball: the unpacked `bin/` (unpack each version into the **same** folder, or re-run `service install` after unpacking elsewhere). **Default without the flag:** a single-file build uses the folder of `process.execPath`; a JS run (development, `pnpm link --global`, the Q24b folder fallback) uses the `bin/` folder of its own package (`realpath(process.argv[1])`). pane-manager (§4.3) and `doctor` use this same rule. **Check before every spawn:** `<bin-dir>` and `wraith-sprite` must be owned by the user or root and not writable by group or others; otherwise the node refuses to spawn (bot `failed`, reason shown) and `doctor` names the path. So a respawn after an upgrade starts the new sprite even after the old version's folder is removed. Old and new builds meet only across one release, which §6.5 keeps compatible
- `brew uninstall wraith-node` removes the binaries only; `~/.wraith` and the service unit stay (`wraith-node service uninstall` first, documented in the caveats)

### 4.7 Mobile & Wearables 💡 ✅ DECISION-14

#### 4.7.1 Mobile

| Level | Method | Use when |
|---|---|---|
| **Now (P1–P4)** | **PWA** (installed to Home Screen) + Web Push | Approvals and chat from the phone, no store needed |
| Later (P9) | **Capacitor** wraps the same PWA build as an iOS/Android app (plugins: push, biometric, keychain) | A store app, **Apple Watch**, or more reliable push than PWA is needed |
| Alternative | Tauri v2 mobile | If its ecosystem is more mature at decision time |

#### 4.7.2 Smart Watch: Amazfit Active 2 (Square) + iPhone

**Starting fact (confirmed by the user):** the watch is paired with an **iPhone**, so the Zepp app and Side Service run on iOS, which restricts background work more than Android → the whole design must **work even if the Side Service never runs in the background**; background mode is a bonus if Q27a passes

**No mobile app of our own is needed.** A Zepp OS Mini Program has 3 parts ✅:
- **Device App** (on the watch, JavaScript): approval cards, Allow/Reject buttons, bot status, quick messages from a list
- **Side Service** (runs inside the Zepp app on the iPhone, JavaScript): **the only part with internet access**, via `fetch` ✅ (supports only `url`, `method`, `headers`, `body` ✅; nothing documented about timeouts or WebSocket) · Talks to the watch over BLE (ZML ✅)
- **Settings App** (settings page in the Zepp app): enter the relay URL and pair

```
⌚ Device App ⇄ BLE/ZML ⇄ Zepp app on iPhone (Side Service: fetch + command signing) ⇄ HTTPS ⇄ relay (Cloudflare / Self-hosted)
```

| Topic | Design |
|---|---|
| **Channel to the relay** | HTTP API in `relay-core` (§6.7): `GET /api/v1/inbox` (long-poll ≤ 25s 🧪 Q25b) + `POST /api/v1/cmd` + `GET /api/v1/roster` |
| **Identity** | The watch is a **device** in the roster with **cap `watch`** · Pairing: the Settings App shows the **text pairing code** in the common `WRP1-` format (§3.4: kind=watch + the full compressed `signPub` + 128-bit secret), because a QR code on the Settings page cannot be scanned by the same phone → the owner pastes it into Add device (same phone: use Share sheet/clipboard) · the owner takes `signPub` from the code (an offer with a different key aborts) and fixes `caps=[watch]` from the code, never from the offer · HMAC steps as in §3.4 item 3 · The Side Service pins the owner public key |
| **Permissions of cap `watch`** | Allowed: `approval.respond` with `allow_once`/`reject_once` only, `user.message` from the quick messages list (≤ 140 characters) **or** a confirmed voice transcript (`via:"voice"`, ≤ 280, P9.18, DECISION-55), `voice.transcribe`, `room.freeze{frozen:true}` · **Not allowed:** `allow_always`, `reject_always`, unfreeze, take control, spawn/kill, resurrect, `cloud.move`, roster · Checked both at the relay (for commands the relay handles) and in the node `verifier` |
| **Signing** | Key in Side Service storage · If WebCrypto is unavailable, use `@noble/curves` (ECDSA P-256) 🧪 Q26 · Accepted trade-off: the key lives in the Zepp app on the iPhone, so it is only as secure as that phone; permissions are therefore limited by cap `watch` |
| **Request verification** | The Side Service verifies the node signature on `approval.request` with the roster from `GET /api/v1/roster` (owner-signed, owner key pinned) before passing it to the watch, and computes `requestHash` itself (does not trust the relay's value) |
| **Approval on the watch** | Title, bot name, kind, shortened command (≤ 120 characters) · If the detail is long or is a diff → show "Open on phone" instead of the Allow button (§14.4) |
| **Notifications (3 layers)** | **(1) Guaranteed layer:** Web Push/notifications on the iPhone are already mirrored to the watch by Zepp (cannot approve from there) → the user opens the Mini Program and the Home page fetches the inbox immediately · **(2) 🧪 Q27a:** the Side Service polls the inbox in the background on iOS (likely only while the Zepp app is open/just closed) · **(3) 🧪 Q27b:** `@zos/notification.notify()` ✅ (API_LEVEL ≥ 3.0, permission `device:os.notification`) creates a watch notification with `actions:[{text:"Allow", file:"app-service/approve", param:"<approvalId>"}]` → the action **starts an App Service in single-execution mode (≤ 600 ms, no timers)** ✅, which must send a message to the Side Service within that time |
| **Continuous App Service** | Requires permission `device:os.bg_service` + user consent ✅; keeps running after the page closes · Use only if Q27b needs it (e.g. to receive messages from the Side Service in order to `notify()`) · Only one per device ✅ |
| **Mode requirement** | Requires **Cloudflare or Self-hosted with HTTPS** (the Side Service cannot reach the Mac's `localhost`) |
| **Installation** | Development: Zepp Simulator (usable by agents) + developer mode/QR on the real device (human) · Distribution: Zepp App Store (later) |

**Target device: Amazfit Active 2 (Square)** ✅ (Zepp device list)
- **API_LEVEL 4.2 / Zepp OS 5.0** · Square screen **390 × 450**, corner radius 86 · 2 physical buttons · deviceSource `10223873`, `10223875` (`10223872` = mainland China model) · **Do not confuse with Active 2 (Round)** 466 × 466
- `app.json`: `targets` screen 390 × 450, `minVersion` API_LEVEL 3.0 (enables App Service + notifications), permissions `device:os.notification` (+ `device:os.bg_service` only if layer 3 is used)
- `createSysTimer` (background timer): community reports say it requires API_LEVEL 4.0 🧪, not yet confirmed by official docs

#### 4.7.3 Other watches (P9)

| Watch | Method | Needs our mobile app |
|---|---|---|
| Garmin | Connect IQ (Monkey C); internet via the Garmin Connect app | ❌ |
| Wear OS | Kotlin app; connects to the internet directly or via the phone | Not required |
| Apple Watch | Swift app (watchOS) | ✅ Requires a parent iOS app (Capacitor) |

All of them use the same HTTP API + cap `watch` (§6.7)

### 4.8 Workspaces: one folder, one bot 💡 DECISION-24

**Why:** chat-first tools (Kiro Crew, Claude Desktop) treat the folder as an afterthought: several sessions end up editing the same folder, and when a human wants "the AI that works on `~/code/app`", nothing points to it. Claude Code CLI gets this right by keying sessions on the project path. Wraith makes the folder the identity.

| Rule | Design |
|---|---|
| **Identity** | `workspaceKey = <nodeId>:<realpath>` (symlinks resolved; trailing slash removed). The `botId` is a ULID created at first spawn and stored with the workspace (`workspaces` table, unique on `workspace_key`); lookups go by `workspaceKey`, so the same folder always yields the same bot, and the bot keeps its id if it is later moved to another folder. Cloud bots: `workspaceKey = cloud:<owner>/<repo>@<branch>` |
| **One bot per workspace** | `bot.spawn` for a workspace that already has a bot does **not** create a second one: if it is alive the node answers `E_WORKSPACE_TAKEN{botId}` and the UI opens it; if it is in the Crypt the node resurrects it (same session) |
| **Session continuity** | The node stores `workspaceKey → {agentKind, sessionId}`; opening the folder again calls ACP `session/load` with that id. If the store is lost, the sprite asks the agent for the latest session in that cwd (Kiro: 🧪 Q30) |
| **Hierarchy** | Parent = nearest ancestor workspace **on the same node** that has a bot. Computed by the node from paths; shown as a tree in the UI. `wraith_list_bots` returns `parent` and `children`. A parent's context includes its children (name, state, last summary). Delegation down and returns up use the normal handoff + loop-guard (§7.3) |
| **Bot kinds** (DECISION-25) | **Project bot**: owns any folder under a root, including a root itself; whether it *leads* is not a kind but a fact of the tree (it is the parent of every bot in its sub-folders, also ones created before it, which are re-parented and notified) · **Project assistant** (reviewer, docs, …): lives in `<project>/.wraith/assistants/<name>/`, so its parent is that project's bot · **Standalone assistant**: lives in `~/.wraith/scratch/<name>/`, no parent, no children (research, drafting) |
| **Read anywhere, write at home** (DECISION-25) | Every bot may **read** any folder under the workspace roots. A bot **writes** only inside its own workspace; a parent may also write anywhere below its own folder. Enforcement: (1) where the agent supports it, the sprite passes the workspace as the write allowlist (Kiro: 🧪 Q31); (2) every write/edit/delete/move approval card shows the target path, and a path outside the bot's write area gets a red **"Outside workspace"** badge and Allow always/Reject always are hidden for it |
| **Assistant folders** | Created by the node on demand. `.wraith/` is added (idempotently) to the file given by `git rev-parse --git-path info/exclude` (works for worktrees/submodules; skipped if the folder is not in a git repo; never a tracked `.gitignore`), so the repo is unchanged. Assistant folders show in the folder browser as assistant entries even though `.wraith` is hidden |
| **Move to folder** | A standalone assistant (or any bot) can be moved to a folder that has no bot: same `botId` and room history; the agent session restarts in the new cwd with a summary of the old session as its first context; the old session goes to the Crypt |
| **Agent + instructions** (DECISION-26) | There are **no preset roles/profiles**. Creating a bot picks only the **agent** (Kiro, Claude Code, local LLM, Shell — only those detected on that machine by `doctor`) and optional **instructions** (empty by default, editable later in the bot's detail page). Wraith adds only factual context it needs: the bot's workspace, its parent and children, and the read-anywhere/write-at-home rule. Changing instructions restarts the agent with the same session; changing the agent kind starts a new session in the same workspace (old one goes to the Crypt). Two roles in one repo = two folders or two machines |
| **Instructions delivery** | Kiro: Wraith writes a per-bot custom agent `~/.kiro/agents/wraith-<botId>.json` (prompt = Wraith context + user instructions; only read-only tools are trusted, every other tool asks for permission, SI-2; file mode 0600; MCP tokens are **per session** and passed to the agent via env, rotated on every epoch) and starts `kiro-cli acp --agent wraith-<botId>` 🧪 Q32 (or selects `wraith-<botId>` after `initialize` if Q43 shows V3 rejects `--agent`; the sprite must then confirm the active agent, SI-2). Wraith only ever creates/updates/deletes files named `wraith-*.json` there. Fallback: send the instructions as the first context block of the session. Claude Code (`--mode sdk`): `systemPrompt.append` (preset `claude_code`) 🧪 Q36 |
| **Chats and groups** (DECISION-28) | Every bot gets its **own chat** when it is created; creating a bot never asks for a room. **Groups** are created separately (name + pick existing bots from the bot tree) and can be edited later; a bot may be in any number of groups. A bot's **main session** serves its own chat and handoffs from other bots; each group it belongs to is served by a **group seat** (next rows, DECISION-31). Every prompt carries `meta.room`; replies, tool cards and approvals go back to that chat. Handoff cards appear in the chat of the root prompt. Freeze is per chat (a group or a bot chat). The context for a prompt = that chat's recent signed history (seats: the unseen events since the watermark, DECISION-53) (+ the bot's workspace facts) |
| **Group seats** (DECISION-31, 32) | When a bot is added to a group, Wraith starts a **group seat**: its own session bound to that group, in the **same workspace**, hosted per the agent's `seats` mode (DECISION-54): `process` (default) = a separate agent process in its own herdr pane (`wraith-sprite --bot <id> --seat <roomId>`); `swap` = the bot's one process switching sessions (fallback row below). **Seed (default):** a summary the main session writes on request (`PromptKind "summary"`, redacted, ≤ 2 KB), shown to the user in the add-to-group dialog where it can be edited or cleared. **Native fork** (not in ACP's published schema: the Claude ACP adapter's draft `session/fork` or Claude Agent SDK `forkSession`/`--fork-session` 🧪 Q33d; none known for Kiro) is an opt-in per group, because it copies the whole private chat history into the group. If a Crypt entry `(botId, seat, room)` exists, it is resumed instead of seeding. A seat only sees its group's signed history. **Context watermark** (DECISION-53): the node keeps `bot_sessions.delivered_seq` per seat session. **When the node turns a queued seat prompt into `bot.prompt`** (§6.5) it builds a block "Since your last turn in # <group>" from the allowlisted, signed group events (SI-6) with `delivered_seq < relay.seq < seq(trigger)` in order — the trigger (the `user.message`, `room.handoff` or `room.return` that made the prompt) is the prompt text, never part of its own block; for node-made prompts (nudge, `summary`) the block ends at `upToId` or the newest event, inclusive · other bots' text is labelled untrusted (SI-10) · `room.handoff`/`room.return` render as one line `@from → @to: <task ≤ 200 chars>` without attachments or nested context · each event is cut at 4 KB ("… use `wraith_room_context`") · the block is ≤ 24 KB: when more is waiting, the oldest part is replaced by the group `context` (context-builder, ≤ 30 lines) and the line "N earlier messages (seq < S): use `wraith_room_context{beforeSeq:S}`"; if the node got a `snapshot` instead of a replay for that range, the block says "N events could not be replayed". The block is stored in `prompts_local.payload` and `delivered_seq` moves to the trigger (or the last event included) **at that moment**; a resend after a sprite restart or an `E_PROMPT_PARTIAL` retry reuses the stored payload and never rebuilds it, so two queued prompts never share an event and nothing is skipped silently. **`summary` prompts** (idle and close) carry the block and move the watermark too; their `bot.summary` sets `untrustedInputs` when the block held another bot's, cloud or `pane-unverified` text, and they never count as seat activity for the idle rule. The seat's `bot_sessions` row is kept when the seat closes (`state='closed'`), so a Crypt resume of the same session id continues from its `delivered_seq`; the watermark is read from the **live** row for `(bot, room)` (a closed and a live row for one group can coexist after a new seed or fork); a closed row is deleted when its Crypt entry is deleted or the bot is removed, and `bot.updated.seats[]` lists live rows only; a seat that starts a **new** session (seed or fork) starts at 0 and so gets the newest 24 KB of the group. Respond (nudge) uses the same rule. In the UI it is the same bot (same name, sprite, tree position); in that group, "Open console" opens the seat's pane (`swap`: the bot's one pane, with a `# <group>` chip). Removing the bot from the group asks the seat for a final summary (wait ≤ 60 s), then closes it (session → Crypt). At most `maxSeatsPerBot` (default 4) live seats (`state ≠ 'closed'`) per bot (`E_SEAT_LIMIT`) |
| **Allow always with seats** (DECISION-33; for `--mode sdk` the grant lives only in the sprite's memory for that epoch, never written to Claude settings) | The lock is enforced at `request_permission`/hook points only. While a bot has **≥ 2 live sessions** (main + any seat), `allow_always` is **not offered** for write-kind tools (the sheet hides it; the sprite drops it from options sent to clients and refuses it if answered), so every write passes the lock. A bot with only its main session keeps Allow always. Tools allowed "always" before a seat started are reset by **restarting the main session's agent** (epoch bump + `session/load`) at its next `idle` after the first seat starts; until then a card in the bot's own chat says "Allow-always tools stay trusted until @<bot> restarts" (whether trust survives `session/load` is checked in Q33c 🧪) |
| **Workspace write lock** (DECISION-31, 32) | All sessions of one bot (main + seats) share one write lock per workspace, held by the node. The lock covers **every tool that is not in the SI-2 read-only set** (so `edit`, `delete`, `move`, `execute`, `fetch`, `other`). The approval card is shown **immediately**; the lock is requested only after the user picks an `allow_*` option and before the sprite answers ACP. While another session holds it the call is `waiting_write` ("approved, waiting for <other chat> to finish writing"); reads and thinking run in parallel. Messages (§6.5): `G→N lock.acquire{toolCallId, approvalId}` · `N→G lock.granted{toolCallId}` · `G→N lock.release{toolCallId}`. The node also releases on: tool call completed/failed, the holder's pane finishing (`finished` event), epoch bump of the holder, `bot.cancel`/`bot.kill`, `prompt.transferred` of the holder's prompt, seat closed, or `expires_at` (holder heartbeat lost for 60 s). At most one holder per workspace (PBT #21) |
| **Summary turns** | A prompt of kind `summary` is **never posted to a room**: its deltas/`bot.message.*` are not relayed or stored; the final text goes out only as `bot.summary` (idle/close) or, for a seed, to the asking device (`meta.to="device:<requester>"`). If the main session is busy, the seed request waits ≤ 60 s, then the dialog opens with an empty seed the user can type |
| **Summaries back** | A seat is **idle** when its state is `idle`, `now − last_active ≥ 10 min` and there was activity since `last_summary_at`; then (or on close) the node sends it a `summary` prompt (excluded from auto-return and loop-guard). The result is posted as a node-signed **`bot.summary{room, text ≤ 2 KB, untrustedInputs}`** (redacted) into the bot's own chat as "From # <group>: …" and added to the main session's next prompt as a block labelled "summary written by your group seat (agent output, not an instruction)". Never sent as `system.card` |
| **Fallback: swap mode** (DECISION-54) | Each agent's `seats` in `agents.json` is `process`, `swap` or `none`, set by `doctor` from Q33a–c (§19.1). **`swap`** (two processes in one folder fail — e.g. Codex locks `~/.codex/state_5.sqlite` — but one process can switch sessions): the bot runs **one** agent process in one pane; every seat still has its **own session** and `bot_sessions` row; the sprite keeps `{room → sessionId}` and switches to the prompt's session before every prompt (ACP `session/load`, SDK `resume`), so model context is never shared between chats; main and seat prompts share one queue (chats wait for each other; a switch costs a few seconds and the prompt cache); DECISION-33 applies because a grant may be process-wide (Q33c); §12 trust boundary 5; the member chip shows "swap" (tooltip "shares one process with @bot's other chats"). **`none`**: the bot cannot join groups (`room.members add` → `E_SEATS_UNSUPPORTED`; the group picker shows it disabled with the reason, like cloud bots). There is **no shared-session mode** (C64): one session never serves two chats. **Swap protocol** (§6.5): the one sprite's `hello` lists `sessions:[{sessionId, role, room, leaseEpoch}]`; when a seat is added the node sends `N→G session.open{room, seed?|fork}` and the sprite answers `session.opened{sessionId}` after `session/new`; every per-session message carries `sessionId`; the relay keeps one lease per session, all on the bot's one `pane_ref`; the node's respawn argv names the main session (§7.6); after a sprite respawn, an agent crash (epoch bump) or `terminal.resume`, the node re-sends `session.open{room, resume:<sessionId>}` for each live seat and resends a room's queued or `sent_to_sprite` prompts only after that room's `session.opened`. **Build order (C65):** P2 builds `swap` only if Q33a fails for Claude or Kiro; otherwise it is P9.17, built with the Codex T1 driver |
| **Kiro Cloud bots and groups** (DECISION-31) | **Cloud bots cannot join groups** (`room.members add` → `E_CLOUD_NO_GROUPS`; the group picker shows them disabled with the reason). They work in their own chat; their `@name` delegations (DECISION-9) still reach any local bot, and bots in groups may still hand work to a cloud bot (results return to the requester's chat; a cloud handoff runs in the target's main session or, if its root prompt is from a group the target belongs to, in that seat) |
| **Mentions vs handoffs** (DECISION-32) | In a bot chat every **user** message goes to that bot (for `user.message` the client sets `meta.mentions=[botId]` and the relay enforces it for `rooms.kind='bot'`; node-signed bot events and handoffs from any bot are accepted in that room). In a group, a **user** `@mention` must name a member (`E_NOT_MEMBER`). A **bot handoff** (`wraith_mention`, cloud `@`) may target any bot; it inherits `meta.room` of its root prompt and runs in the target's seat for that room if it has one, **otherwise in the target's main session** — like a colleague, the target remembers work done for others. To keep that visible, the target's own chat gets a card "Worked for @<requester> (from # <room>): <task>"; only the handoff packet (task, refs, ≤ 30 lines of verified context) crosses over, never whole histories |
| **New-bot defaults** | Kind picker first. For a project assistant the default project is the project of the bot the user last chatted with in the current room, else the last project the user created a bot in. The `.wraith/assistants/` folder is created if missing |
| **Bots creating bots** (DECISION-20 + 25) | `wraith_spawn_request.workspace` must be inside the requester's own workspace (a sub-folder or its `.wraith/assistants/<name>`); otherwise `E_PATH_OUTSIDE_WORKSPACE`. Leads should create bots: if the requester has a parent, the approval card says "api is not the lead of app" and the node posts a note to the parent bot's context. A bot at a root may request anywhere under that root |
| **Workspace roots** (DECISION-29) | `node.json` `workspaceRoots` is a **list**; only folders under a root can become workspaces (assistant and scratch folders are managed by Wraith). **No default of `~`**: on first start the node proposes the common folders that exist (`~/code`, `~/Projects`, `~/dev`, `~/src`, `~/work`) for the user to tick; if none exist the user picks one. **A project that lives elsewhere** (e.g. `~/Desktop/AI_Workspace/Wraith`) is added as its own root: from the phone/desktop "Add folder by path" (`node.roots`), from the desktop app's native folder picker, or on the computer with `wraith-node open [path]` (adds the folder as a root if needed, then opens or creates its bot and prints the chat link). Removing a root never deletes bots; bots outside every root keep working but their folder can't be browsed |
| **Folder browsing** | `fs.list` (signed, device cap only) returns **sub-folder names only** (no files, no hidden folders, max 200 entries) for a path under a root, after `realpath` (roots are realpath'd at load; the prefix check uses `root + "/"`; blocks `..`/symlink escape; roots may not be `/`, `~` or an ancestor of `~`). Requests and listings are sent to the asking device only and are not stored by the relay. Names pass through the relay (no E2E), so roots should not be broader than needed |
| **Name** (DECISION-27) | Every bot has a **name** used for `@mention` and display, separate from its identity. Prefilled at creation with the folder name (project bot) or the typed assistant name; editable then and later (`bot.configure`). Unique in the Space, `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,31}$`; on a clash the UI suggests `<name>-<node>` or `<name>-2` (`E_NAME_TAKEN`). Renaming never moves the folder or changes the session. The UI always shows the workspace path under the name · **Long names:** headers, list rows and chips show names on **one line with an ellipsis** (full name in the tooltip / bot card / members sheet); counts and state chips never shrink |

### 4.9 Files and Changes (per bot workspace) 💡 DECISION-35

A **bot chat** (never a group) gets two small icon buttons next to the workspace path in its header: **Files** (folder icon) and **Changes** (branch-arrow icon + count of changed files, shown only when there are changes). Chat | Console stays the only view switch. Each button opens a **bottom sheet** on the phone and a side panel on the desktop, so you return to the chat without losing your place. Both are **read-only views of the bot's own workspace**; to change anything you ask the bot (e.g. "Ask @app to commit"), so every write still goes through approval (SI-1).

| Topic | Design |
|---|---|
| **Changes** (P2) | `ws.changes{bot}` → the node runs `git status --porcelain=v2` + `git diff --numstat` in the workspace and answers `ws.changesListing` (path, status, +/−, attribution); `ws.diff{bot, path}` → `ws.diffContent` (unified diff, ≤ 256 KB, redacted). A workspace that is not a git repo shows "Changes need git" |
| **Attribution** | The node records `file_touches` for every write-kind tool call: paths from the ACP tool call `locations` 🧪 Q34, and for commands without locations, the files whose git state changed while that call held the **workspace write lock** (the lock guarantees a single writer). Each change shows `@bot · chat · approved by <device> · time` (or `@bot · in terminal mode (approved in the TUI) · time` for `file_touches.source = 'terminal'`) (the approving device comes from `approvals_local.answered_by`); tool cards and approval cards get a **View diff** link · A change the node cannot attribute (edited outside Wraith, before the node started, or with no lock window) is shown as **unattributed**, never guessed |
| **In a group** | No Files or Changes buttons. To see what a member changed, tap its bot chip → bot card → **Open chat**, and use that bot's Changes button. Change attribution still names the group chat (`@app · # main`) |
| **Files** (P9.13) | `fs.read{bot, path}` → `fs.content` (text only, ≤ 256 KB, syntax-highlighted read-only view); tree from `fs.tree{bot, path}` (names, sizes, git status) |
| **Privacy and safety** (owner choice A) | Works from any device (phone included) through the relay, so: device-signed, never the watch; **not persisted** by the relay and sent only to the asking device (`meta.to="device:<id>"`); the path must be inside **that bot's workspace** after realpath; **deny-list**: `.env*`, `*.pem`, `*.key`, `*.p12`, `id_*`, `.git/`, `.wraith/` (except the bot's own assistant folder), anything ignored by `.gitignore`, and binary files (`E_FILE_DENIED`); every body passes the redactor (SI-8); size cap (`E_FILE_TOO_LARGE`) |

### 4.10 Platform layer and supported systems 💡 DECISION-59 (C76)
**Supported:** macOS (arm64, x86_64) and Linux (x86_64) for `wraith-node`, `wraith-sprite` and Wraith.app · **Windows:** WSL2 from P9.20 (the Linux build inside WSL), native from P9.21 · any OS can be a **device** already (browser/PWA, phone, watch).

- **`packages/platform`** holds every OS-specific call used by `apps/node`, `apps/sprite` and `apps/relay-node`, behind one interface with the adapters `darwin.ts`, `linux.ts` (also used inside WSL2, with `isWsl()`) and `win32.ts`:
  - local IPC: listen/connect for `sprite.sock` and `control.sock`, the 0600 file / 0700 folder checks
  - files that hold secrets: create with mode 0600, check the mode before reading (§11.1)
  - the login environment capture (`$SHELL -l`, §4.3) and finding a command on PATH
  - the service unit (`service install|uninstall|status|restart`: launchd / systemd user unit)
  - process control: signals, killing a process tree, and the sprite's "pane lost" signals (SIGHUP, SIGTERM, TTY read error → exit 75, §7.6)
  - paths (`~/.wraith`, temp files) and opening a URL in the browser
- **`win32.ts` is a stub until P9.21:** `wraith-node` and `wraith-sprite` print "Native Windows is not supported yet. Run Wraith inside WSL2: <docs link>" and exit 1 (a unit test selects the adapter with the platform mocked). No Windows build is published before P9.21
- **CI check `tools/check-platform.ts`** (run in `ci.yml` lint): fails when `process.platform`, `os.platform()`, `os.type()`, `launchctl`, `systemctl`, `chmod`/`fchmod`, `SIGHUP`, `SIGTERM` or a `/tmp/` path literal appears in `apps/` or `packages/` outside `packages/platform` (tests, `spike/`, `tools/` and `packages/fakes` excluded). In `apps/desktop/src-tauri`, `#[cfg(target_os …)]` is allowed only in `src/platform.rs`
- relay-core and the Cloudflare adapter stay platform-free as before (§6.8); the PWA already runs on any OS

## 5. Agent Integration Tiers

| Tier | Used with | Method | Chat | Console | Approval | Agent-specific code needed? | Phase |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **T0 Generic** | Any CLI | Plain herdr pane | Status card (from the program's OSC 7501 report if it sends one, else "running" with no status) + **raw snapshot** (`terminal.read{lines:0}`, the visible screen as plain text, only on status change; history is never requested, because hc reads a full-screen program's history by scrolling it ✅; a reply with `complete:false` is shown with a "partial" note) | Real TUI | "bot is waiting" card + Open console button | No | P2 |
| **T1 ACP** ⭐ | Kiro ✅, Claude Code via the Agent SDK driver (`--mode sdk`, same sprite events) 🧪 Q36, others 🧪 | sprite | Full | sprite view + side command pane 🧪 Q4 | `session/request_permission` (spec ✅, **whether Kiro actually sends it 🧪 Q3**) | No | P1 |
| **T2 Native** | Claude Code TUI | TUI + hooks + transcript | Full | Real TUI | `PreToolUse` → `permissionDecision` ✅ (set `timeout` in config 🧪) | Yes | P9 |

**Terminal mode** (DECISION-52, §8.1): a T1 local bot's main session can be handed to the agent's own TUI and back (Claude via `claude --resume`, Kiro via `kiro-cli chat --v3 --resume-id`), so features Wraith does not render stay reachable. T2 (P9.4) is needed only if Q36 fails.

**Bot layout for the demo / real use**

| Agent | Tier | Location | Capability tags | Launch (managed in `agents.json`, §11.1) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `kiro` | T1 | Any node with `kiro-cli` | `local-files`, `can-approve` (+ `gpu` if the node has one) | `kiro-cli acp --agent wraith-<botId> --agent-engine v3` (Q43: Kiro's V3 ACP docs say `acp` rejects `--agent/--model/--effort/--trust-*`; if so the sprite launches `kiro-cli acp --agent-engine v3` and selects `wraith-<botId>` after `initialize` with `session/set_mode` or the agent config option) · Terminal mode: `kiro-cli chat --v3 --agent wraith-<botId> --resume-id <sessionId>` 🧪 Q42 |
| `kiro-cloud` | T1 (Cloud) | Kiro Cloud (sprite on the node holding the cloud-host lease) | `cloud`, `autonomous`, `git-only` | §7.4 |
| `local-llm` | T1 🧪 (falls back to T0 if not) | V100 | `local-files`, `gpu`, `private`, `can-approve` | sprite `--mode sdk` (Claude Agent SDK) + `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:4000` |
| `grok` (Grok Build, DECISION-48) | **T0** until Q38 passes; then T1 | Nodes with the `grok` CLI | T0: `untrusted`, `no-handoff` · T1: `local-files`, `can-approve` | T0: `grok` in a plain pane · T1: `grok agent stdio` (ACP) 🧪 launched with `permission_mode = "ask"` forced 🧪; if Q38 shows it does not send `session/request_permission` for every tool, a Wraith-managed `PreToolUse` hook blocks each call until a device-signed approval (same hook-endpoint + one-shot grant as §7.2); if neither works it stays T0 · no Rewind (none documented) |
| `shell` | T0 | Every machine | `untrusted`, `no-handoff` | `$SHELL` |

### 5.1 Membership Matrix

| Capability | **Bot on any node** (T1/T2) | **Kiro Cloud bot** | T0 | **Watch (device cap `watch`)** |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| In a group (timeline, rail, @mention from the user) | ✅ (as a group seat) | ❌ own chat only (`E_CLOUD_NO_GROUPS`); may send/receive handoffs | ✅ (status card) | Sees short status + sends quick messages |
| Receives handoff from other bots | ✅ | ✅ sent as prompt + context packet | ❌ | – |
| Sends handoff to other bots | ✅ MCP `wraith_mention` | ✅ **`@name` in the ACP message** → host node converts it into a packet (§7.4.1) | ❌ | – |
| Returns results (return) | ✅ | ✅ automatically when the turn ends | ❌ | – |
| Reads room context | ✅ `wraith_room_context` | ✅ context packet attached | ❌ | – |
| Exchanges code or files | git + local files | **git** (PR/branch) | – | – |
| Small attachments (≤ 8KB) | ✅ | ✅ (embedded in the prompt) | – | – |
| Per-command approval | ✅ from device/watch | ❌ (Kiro Cloud's own Autonomous mode only ✅) | Warning card | ✅ `allow_once`/`reject_once` only |
| Console | sprite view + side pane | sprite view on the host node | Real TUI | ❌ |
| Crypt / Resurrect | ✅ (on the same node) | ✅ `--resume-id` via cloud-host lease | – | ❌ |
| Host machine asleep | bot `offline`, work waits in queue | Work waits in queue · Move host (P3) or automatic (P8) | `offline` | – |
| How far results can be trusted | Normal | **untrusted-input** (§12) | untrusted | – |

## 6. Interfaces & Protocols

> Every schema in this section lives in `packages/protocol` (zod) and `packages/crypto` · route/verify code must import from here only. Do not redeclare shapes in apps.

### 6.1 Envelope (meta + body + relay)

```json
{ "v": 1,
  "meta": { "id": "01JC…(ULID from origin)", "ts": 1793523205000,
            "space": "…", "room": "main", "bot": "01JB…", "node": "macbook-pro", "epoch": 3,
            "type": "bot.tool", "to": "devices",
            "persist": true,
            "prompt": { "promptId": "…", "replyTo": "…", "kind": "task", "to": "01JB…", "requires": ["gpu"] },
            "mentions": ["01JB…"], "leaseEpoch": 7 },
  "body": { },
  "sig": { "by": "<memberId>", "alg": "ES256", "value": "<base64url>" },
  "relay": { "seq": 1043, "recvTs": 1793523205123, "root": "01JC…", "forwardDepth": 1 } }
```

- **`meta`** = data the relay uses to route, count loop-guard, and send push · **`body`** = content (JSON, not encrypted) · **`relay`** = fields the relay adds after receipt. They are **outside the signature scope**. Receivers must not use them for security decisions.
- **Signature scope:** `sig.value` = ECDSA P-256 (SHA-256) over `JCS({v, meta, body})` (RFC 8785), excluding `sig` and `relay` · encoding (C78): raw IEEE P1363 `r‖s` (64 bytes), base64url without padding; low-S is not enforced (replay protection rests on `meta.id`, SI-5) · JCS is in-house in `packages/crypto` (§16) · `sig.by` must equal the `memberId` of the sending WS (relay checks) and be in the roster (node checks)
- **`requestHash`** = `SHA-256(JCS({v, meta, body}))` of the `approval.request` envelope (the same bytes the node signs), base64url-encoded
- **Who signs what:** a device signs device signed commands · a node signs `approval.request/resolved/rejected_late`, handoff/return, and **stored bot events** (`bot.message.done`, final-status `bot.tool`, `bot.post`, `media.shared`, `bot.registered/updated/removed`, `bot.summary`, `bot.worked_for`, `node.configured`, `node.paused`, `bot.commands`, `bot.rewound`, `bot.terminalState`, `bot.terminalReturn`, `bot.terminalTurn`) and, not stored, `media.ready`, `media.stored`, `bot.unsupported` and **every reply addressed to `device:<requester>`** (`node.rootsListing`, `fs.listing`, `ws.changesListing`, `ws.diffContent`, `fs.treeListing`, `fs.content`, `bot.rewindPointsListing`, `bot.rewindPreviewListing`, `voice.text`) (C77) · Not signed: delta, console frame, `bot.state`, ping, non-final `bot.tool` (`pending`/`in_progress`, also not persisted), `media.chunk`, `media.data`, `media.ack` (**both directions**: flow control only, C77) — the receiver trusts media bytes only when they hash to the signed `sha256` (C74)
- `seq` is issued only for `persist: true` · `id` is created at the origin and used for dedupe at every layer and for replay protection
- **No E2E** (DECISION-6). Signatures protect against **forged commands**, not against reading.

### 6.2 Message catalogue (zod-ready) 💡

**Base schemas (`packages/protocol/src/base.ts`):**
```ts
export const Ulid = z.string().regex(/^[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26}$/);
export const MemberId = z.string().min(1).max(64);        // "dev_<ulid>" | "node_<name>"
export const PaneRef = z.string().regex(/^[a-z0-9-]+\/[A-Za-z0-9:_-]+$/); // nodeId/paneId
export const To = z.union([z.literal("relay"), z.literal("devices"), z.string().startsWith("device:"), z.string().startsWith("node:"), z.string().startsWith("bot:")]);   // device:<memberId> = only that device; never persisted
export const PromptKind = z.enum(["task", "return", "summary"]);   // summary: node-issued, excluded from auto-return and loop-guard
export const PromptMeta = z.object({ promptId: Ulid, replyTo: Ulid.nullable(), kind: PromptKind, to: Ulid, requires: z.array(CapTag).max(8).default([]), handoffMode: z.enum(["assign", "transfer"]).default("assign") });
export const Meta = z.object({
  id: Ulid, ts: z.number().int().positive(), space: z.string(), room: z.string().optional(),
  bot: Ulid.optional(), node: z.string().optional(), epoch: z.number().int().nonnegative().optional(),
  type: MessageType, to: To, persist: z.boolean(),
  prompt: PromptMeta.optional(), mentions: z.array(Ulid).max(16).optional(), leaseEpoch: z.number().int().optional(),
}).strict();
export const Sig = z.object({ by: MemberId, alg: z.literal("ES256"), value: z.string() });
export const RelayFields = z.object({ seq: z.number().int().optional(), recvTs: z.number().int(), root: Ulid.optional(), forwardDepth: z.number().int().optional(), budgetLeft: z.number().int().optional(), from: MemberId.optional() });   // from: stamped by the relay on media.data (routing and DoS only, C77); like every relay.* field it is never used for a security decision (SI-4)
export const Envelope = z.object({ v: z.literal(1), meta: Meta, body: z.unknown(), sig: Sig.optional(), relay: RelayFields.optional() });
// Typed envelope per type: EnvelopeOf("approval.request") = Envelope whose body passes BODY["approval.request"] and whose meta.type matches
export const BODY: Record<MessageType, z.ZodTypeAny> = { /* table below */ };
export const SIGNER: Record<MessageType, "device"|"owner"|"node"|"none"|"challenge"> = { /* "Signed by" column */ };
```

**Message type table** (Direction: D=device/watch, R=relay, N=node, G=sprite) · `persist` ✓ = gets a `seq` and is replayed

| `meta.type` | Direction | persist | Signed by | `body` (zod) | Extra checks by verifier/relay |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `hello` | D/N→R | – | challenge (WS auth) | `{kind:"device"\|"node", lastSeq:int, lastAckedId?:Ulid, nodeId?, herdrVersion?, bots?:BotSummary[], leasesHeld?:{sessionKey,leaseEpoch}[], caps?:NodeCaps}` | `kind` must match the roster · `NodeCaps = {cloudHost?:boolean, voice?:{engine:"whisper-local"\|"cloud", model:string, ready:boolean}}` (node only; `ready` = `voice test` passed at start) |
| `ping` / `pong` | D/N↔R | – | – | `{t:int}` | auto-response in CF |
| `ack` | R↔N/G | – | – | `{id:Ulid, seq?:int}` | – |
| `snapshot` | R→D | – | – | `{seq, rooms:Room[], bots:Bot[], openApprovals:ApprovalView[], recentEvents:Envelope[], leases:LeaseView[], members:MemberView[], frozen:string[]}` | sent instead of replay when the gap > 5,000 |
| `user.message` | D→R→N | ✓ | **device** (or `pane-unverified` from a pane) | `{text:z.string().max(16_000), by:"phone"\|"desktop"\|"watch"\|"pane-unverified", attachments?:Attachment[], media?:MediaRef[] (≤ 4, P9.19, bot chats only; not for `watch`; the node accepts each `mediaId` only if its `media` row has `origin='device'`, `uploader` = the signer, `bot_id` = the chat's bot and `attached_at` empty, then sets `attached_at`; else `E_MEDIA_DENIED`)}` + `meta.mentions[]` | watch: `text` must be in `roster.quickMessages` (owner-signed; checked by relay **and** node) and ≤ 140, **or** `via:"voice"` and ≤ 280 (a transcript the user confirmed on the watch, P9.18) · optional `via?:"voice"` = mic glyph on the message; on Full devices it is display-only, for the `watch` cap it selects the ≤ 280 free-text rule (relay and node, SI-9) (DECISION-55) |
| `bot.post` | N→R→D | ✓ | node | `{text:string}` (from `wraith_post`) | `meta.node` = WS member |
| `prompt.queued` / `prompt.started` / `prompt.done` | N→R→D | ✓ | – | `{promptId:Ulid, bot:Ulid, position?:int, stopReason?:string}` | – |
| `bot.message.delta` | N→R→D | – | – | `{promptId, messageId:string, text:string}` | – |
| `bot.message.done` | N→R→D | ✓ | node | `{promptId, messageId, text:string, stopReason:string}` (full text of the turn: ACP `agent_message_chunk` / SDK text blocks only, never `agent_thought_chunk` or thinking blocks, DECISION-53; `stopReason:"transferred"` after a transfer) | – (turns imported from Terminal mode use `bot.terminalTurn`) |
| `bot.tool` | N→R→D | ✓ (final status only) | node (final status) | `{toolCallId:string, title:string, kind:ToolKind, status:"pending"\|"in_progress"\|"completed"\|"failed", diffs?:Diff[], locations?:Location[], output?:string(≤32KB, redacted)}` | – |
| `bot.state` | N→R→D | – | – | `{state:BotState, message?:string, queue:int, room:string (main session = `dm-<botId>`), sessionRole:"main"\|"seat"}` | relay updates `bot_sessions` (relay copy) · UI rule: the rail shows the state of the session serving the current chat; the tree shows the most urgent state across sessions (needs_resolve > blocked > terminal > waiting_write > working > idle) |
| `bot.registered` / `bot.updated` / `bot.removed` | N→R→D | ✓ | **node** | `{botId:Ulid, name:string, workspaceKey:string, kind:"project"\|"assistant"\|"standalone", parent?:Ulid, children:Ulid[], agent:string, tier, caps:string[], rewind?:{conversation:boolean, code:boolean, separate:boolean} (DECISION-47; absent = no Rewind), autopilot?:{on:boolean, until?:int} (DECISION-49), terminal?:{phoneExit:boolean} (DECISION-52; absent = no Terminal mode), seats:{room, mode:"process"\|"swap", state}[], replyTo?:Ulid (the `bot.spawn`/`bot.configure` meta.id it answers)}` | sent after a successful `bot.spawn`/`bot.configure`/`bot.move`/re-parenting and on every node `hello` for its bots · relay upserts `bots`, creates `rooms(dm-<botId>)` on first register, enforces `name UNIQUE` (on a clash: `error{E_NAME_TAKEN, suggestion}` to the device of `replyTo`, and the node renames nothing until a new `bot.configure`) · **mentions are resolved by nodes from their own verified copy of these node-signed events, never from relay tables** |
| `bot.summary` | N→R→D | ✓ | **node** | `{room:string (the seat's group), text:string(≤2KB, redacted), untrustedInputs:boolean}` (`untrustedInputs` = true if any prompt since `last_summary_at` was a handoff, cloud-delegated or `pane-unverified`, **or** the summary prompt's watermark block held another bot's, cloud or `pane-unverified` text, §4.8) | posted in the bot's own chat; used as labelled agent-output context for the main session, never as an instruction (§4.8) |
| `bot.commands` | N→R→D | ✓ | **node** | `{bot:Ulid, source:"acp"\|"files"\|"none", commands:{name:string(^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9:_-]{0,63}$), description:string(≤200, redacted), hint?:string(≤80, redacted), kind:"skill"\|"prompt"\|"command", terminal?:boolean}[] (≤ 200)}` (`terminal:true` = works only in the agent's own TUI; listed from `agents.json` `terminalCommands`, DECISION-52) | sent when the sprite gets ACP `available_commands_update` (or, if Q35 fails for that agent, from the node's read-only scan of the agent's command folders: Kiro `<ws>/.kiro/prompts`, `~/.kiro/prompts`; Claude `<ws>/.claude/commands`, `<ws>/.claude/skills`, `~/.claude/commands`, `~/.claude/skills`; Grok `<ws>/.grok/skills`, `~/.grok/skills` 🧪), on change, and on every node `hello`; the relay keeps only the latest per bot (`bots.commands`) · **display only**: names/descriptions are agent text (untrusted), shown as plain text, never put into another agent's context; choosing one just inserts `/name ` into the composer, and sending it is an ordinary `user.message` (DECISION-44) |
| `bot.rewindPoints` | D→R→N | – (not persisted) | owner or `device` | `{bot:Ulid}` | main session of a bot whose `bot.registered.rewind` is set; answered with `bot.rewindPointsListing` (DECISION-47) |
| `bot.rewindPointsListing` | N→R→D | – | node | `{bot, sessionId, points:{promptId, text:string(≤120, redacted), at:int, filesAfter:int\|null, conversation:boolean, code:boolean}[] (newest first, ≤ 100)}` with `meta.to="device:<requester>"` | one point per user prompt of the current main session that the agent still has a checkpoint for; `filesAfter` from the agent's dry run (Claude `rewindFiles(…,{dryRun:true})` 🧪 Q36) or null |
| `bot.rewindPreview` | D→R→N→G | – (not persisted) | owner or `device` | `{bot:Ulid, toPromptId:Ulid, restore:"both"\|"code"}` | answered with `bot.rewindPreviewListing{bot, toPromptId, files:{path, action:"restore"\|"delete"}[] (≤ 200, redacted), filesHash:string}` (`filesHash = SHA-256(JCS(files sorted by path))`) to the asking device only (`meta.to="device:<requester>"`, not persisted, node-signed, C77); built from the agent's dry run 🧪 |
| `bot.rewind` | D→R→N→G | ✓ | owner or `device` (not watch, not viewer) | `{bot:Ulid, toPromptId:Ulid, restore:"both"\|"conversation"\|"code", filesHash?:string}` (`filesHash` required when `restore` includes code) | **every session of the bot idle** (else `E_BOT_BUSY`), main session's history only (never a seat or group), `restore` must be allowed by `rewind` caps (`E_REWIND_UNSUPPORTED`), the point must still exist (`E_REWIND_POINT_GONE`); a code restore takes the workspace write lock (synthetic `toolCallId = "rewind:<meta.id>"`) for the whole restore, re-runs the dry run and refuses with `E_REWIND_CHANGED` if the file set's hash differs from the signed `filesHash`, and refuses (`E_PATH_OUTSIDE_WORKSPACE`) if any path is outside the bot's write area; the user's signed command, bound to the listed files by `filesHash`, is the human approval for restoring them |
| `bot.rewound` | N→R→D | ✓ | **node** | `{bot, toPromptId, restore, filesRestored:int, filesSkipped:int (links/unreadable, SDK `skippedLinks`), promptText:string, sessionId:string (may be new after a conversation restore; the node updates the workspace's session)}` | posted as a "Rewound to …" card; timeline events after the point stay (marked rewound, greyed), never deleted; the PWA puts `promptText` back into the composer after a conversation restore |
| `bot.terminal` | D→R→N | ✓ | owner or `device` (not watch, not viewer) | `{bot:Ulid, action:"enter"\|"exit", run?:string(^/[a-z0-9][a-z0-9:_-]{0,63}( [\x20-\x7e]{0,200})?$)}` (printable ASCII only; the sprite strips anything else) | **Terminal mode** (DECISION-52, §8.1) · main session of a T1 local bot whose `bot.registered.terminal` is set (else `E_TERMINAL_UNSUPPORTED`) · `enter` only when the main session is `idle` with no open approval, no queued prompt being started and the write lock free (`E_TERMINAL_BUSY`), `enter` while already in Terminal mode → `E_TERMINAL_ACTIVE` · `run` = a `/command` the node types into the pane after launch **without Enter** (`terminal.keys`, one key per character, ASCII only 🧪 Q39/Q42; if hc refuses a character nothing is typed and the banner says "Type `/mcp` in the terminal") · `exit` from any Full device (not only the one that entered) only when the agent reports a reliable idle signal (`terminal.phoneExit`, Claude per Q40), else `E_TERMINAL_BUSY` ("Exit in the terminal: Console → Take control → `<terminal.exit[0]>`", e.g. `/exit` for Claude, `/quit` for Kiro) · `exit` while not in Terminal mode → ack, no-op · a replayed `meta.id` → `E_REPLAY` |
| `bot.terminalState` | N→R→D | ✓ | **node** | `{bot, state:"entering"\|"idle"\|"busy"\|"exiting"\|"off", by:string, enteredAt:int, sessionId:string, reason?:string}` | drives the chat banner, Home badge and the pane's Open in terminal / Back to Wraith button; `busy` only from a reliable signal (Claude registry/hooks), otherwise the node sends `idle` and the UI says "in terminal" |
| `bot.terminalReturn` | N→R→D | ✓ | **node** | `{bot, enteredAt, exitedAt, turns:int, interrupted:boolean, changes:{files:int, added:int, removed:int}\|null, settingsChanged?:{file:string, created:boolean, added:{path:string, value:string}[], si2WouldPass:boolean}[] (new allow rules, `defaultMode`, hooks; `si2WouldPass` = the node's SI-2 dry run with the entries kept), sessionDiverged?:{newSessionId:string}, model?:{before:string, after:string}, cacheCostUsd?:number}` | posted as the "Back from terminal" card; `settingsChanged` or `sessionDiverged` leaves the bot in `needs_resolve` until a `bot.terminalResolve` |
| `bot.terminalResolve` | D→R→N | ✓ | owner or `device` | `{bot, settings?:"remove"\|"keep", session?:"follow"\|"keep", model?:"revert"\|"keep"}` | `settings:"remove"` deletes exactly the listed entries (a file the TUI created is deleted) (backup kept in `~/.wraith/backups/`), then SI-2 runs again; `keep` → the bot starts only if SI-2 passes with the entries kept, otherwise it goes to `failed` with `E_AGENT_UNSAFE` (queued prompts kept, SI-2 re-runs at the next spawn) · `session:"follow"` makes the TUI's new session the bot's main session (old one → Crypt) |
| `bot.terminalTurn` | N→R→D | ✓ | **node** | `{bot, room:string (the bot's own chat), role:"user"\|"assistant", text:string(≤ 32 KB, redacted), at:int, enteredAt:int, n:int}` | one per turn imported from Terminal mode (DECISION-52), at most the last 50 turns of a stay (card: "N older turns stay in the agent's own history"); labelled "from terminal · not verified"; **never** a prompt, approval, handoff, `room.return`, `bot.post`, `ask_human` or `bot.commands`; excluded from context-builder, `wraith_room_context` and handoff context (SI-6) |
| `bot.unsupported` | N→R→D | – | node | `{bot, room, kind:string(≤64), count:int}` | an agent update Wraith cannot render (unknown ACP `session/update` kind or SDK message type); shown as a chip "N updates Wraith can't show · View in console"; never carries content (DECISION-52) |
| `approval.request` | N→R→D | ✓ | **node** | `{approvalId:Ulid, bot:Ulid, sessionId:string, epoch:int, promptId?:Ulid, turnOrigin:"user"|"handoff"|"return"|"cloud"|"summary"|"nudge"|"pane"|"none", autopilot?:boolean (§7.2.1), requestKind:"tool"|"spawn" (default "tool"), title:string, kind:ToolKind, detail:{command?:string, cwd?:string, diff?:string, raw?:unknown}, summary:z.string().max(120), options:Option[], expiresAt:int}` | relay records meta + `request_hash` |
| `approval.respond` | D→R→N→G | ✓ | **device/watch (Must)** | `{approvalId, epoch:int, optionId:string, requestHash:string}` | `ts < expiresAt`, epoch matches, `optionId` is one of the request's `options` (else `E_OPTION_INVALID`), `requestHash` matches what the node sent, cap watch → optionKind ∈ {allow_once, reject_once} |
| `approval.resolved` | N→R→D | ✓ | node | `{approvalId, optionId, optionKind, by:"device:<id>"\|"watch:<id>"\|"autopilot"\|"timeout"\|"expired"}` | – |
| `approval.rejected_late` | N→R→D | ✓ | node | `{approvalId, reason:"already_resolved"\|"stale_epoch"\|"expired"\|"unknown"}` | – |
| `room.handoff` / `room.return` | N→R→N (+D) | ✓ | **node** | HandoffPacket (§6.6) | relay: `replyTo` must be a prompt that `from.bot` is currently working on; capability matches `requires`; loop-guard · receiving node: signature comes from the node where `from.bot` lives |
| `bot.spawn` | D→R→N | ✓ | device | `{kind:"project"\|"assistant"\|"standalone", workspace:string (absolute path under a root, or `~/.wraith/scratch/<name>`), createFolder?:boolean, name:string, agent:"kiro"\|"claude"\|"local-llm"\|"grok"\|"shell", instructions?:string(≤8000)}` → if the workspace already has a bot: `E_WORKSPACE_TAKEN{botId}` and the UI opens it; otherwise the node answers with `bot.registered` and the relay creates its chat `dm-<botId>` (§4.8) | `createFolder` (project kind only): the last path segment matches `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,63}$`, its parent exists under a root (after realpath), and the folder must not exist yet (`E_FOLDER_EXISTS`) |
| `bot.move` | D→R→N | ✓ | device | `{bot:Ulid, workspace:string}` | target folder has no bot; under a root |
| `bot.configure` | D→R→N | ✓ | owner or `device` | `{bot:Ulid, name?:string, instructions?:string(≤8000), agent?:string, autopilot?:{on:boolean, until?:int}}` (autopilot: §7.2.1, DECISION-49) | agent must be detected on the node; launch argv never contains `--trust-all-tools` (SI-2) |
| `bot.kill` / `bot.cancel` | D→R→N | ✓ | device | `{bot:Ulid, room?:string, reason?:string}` | `bot.kill` stops **all** sessions of the bot (main + seats); `bot.cancel{room}` cancels only the session serving that chat (default: the bot's own chat); if no seat serves `room`, it cancels the main session only when its current prompt's `meta.room` equals `room` |
| `console.open` / `console.close` | D→R→N | – | device or viewer (relay checks cap ≠ watch) | `{paneRef}` | read-only |
| `console.frame` | N→R→D | – | – | `{paneRef, mode:"snapshot"\|"stream", data:string(≤64KB), hash:string, cols:int, rows:int}` | – |
| `console.takecontrol` | D→R→N | ✓ | device (Must) | `{paneRef, session:Ulid, until:int}` | `until − ts ≤ 10 min` · `paneRef` must not belong to a `role='seat'` session (`E_SEAT_READ_ONLY`) |
| `console.input` | D→R→N | – | device (Must) | `{paneRef, session:Ulid, n:int, text?:string, keys?:string[]}` | `text` (any Unicode) is pasted and **submitted with Enter** once nobody has typed at the machine for ~3 s (`terminal.send`); `keys` are named keys only (enter, esc, tab, arrows, `ctrl+…`, single ASCII letters; `terminal.keys`); there is no way to type text without submitting it (C75) · session not yet expired · `n` > previous · `now − ts ≤ 60s` · `paneRef` must not belong to a `role='seat'` session (`E_SEAT_READ_ONLY`) |
| `crypt.snapshot` | N→R | – | – | `{sessions:SessionRef[]}` | – |
| `crypt.list` | D→R | – | device or viewer (relay checks) | `{node?:string}` | – |
| `crypt.listing` | R→D | – | – | `{sessions:CryptEntry[]}` with `meta.to = "device:<requester>"` | – |
| *(Crypt rules, DECISION-31)* | | | | Resurrect of a **dormant** bot → that session becomes its main session · Resurrect on a **live** bot = "switch session": the current main session goes to the Crypt first (one main session per bot, one bot per folder) · `fork:true` only for a dormant bot · seat sessions can only be resurrected by re-adding the bot to that group | |
| `crypt.resurrect` | D→R→N | ✓ | device | `{sessionRef:SessionRef, fork?:boolean}` | must have no lease and not be in grace |
| `room.freeze` | D→R(→N notified) | ✓ | device, watch or viewer | `{room:string, frozen:boolean}` | watch/viewer: `frozen` must be `true` · Freeze blocks new `room.handoff` only; `room.return` and its ReturnCard still land in a frozen room (a result is not new work, C66) |
| `room.create` / `room.rename` | D→R | ✓ | device | `{room:string, name:string (1–48 Unicode characters after trim, no newlines; unique per Space), members:BotId[] (≥ 1)}` / `{room, name}` | groups only; bot chats are created by `bot.spawn` and cannot be renamed or deleted |
| `room.members` | D→R→N | ✓ | device | `{room:string, add?:BotId[], remove?:BotId[]}` | groups only; cloud bots cannot be added (`E_CLOUD_NO_GROUPS`); adding starts a group seat on the bot's node for T1/T2 bots (T0 members get no seat: status card only, `seats:[]`; an agent with `seats:"none"` → `E_SEATS_UNSUPPORTED` from the node, DECISION-54), removing closes it (session → Crypt) and drops its queued prompts from that group with a system card |
| `room.nudge` | D→R→N | ✓ | owner or `device` (not watch, not viewer) | `{room:string, bot:Ulid, upToId:Ulid}` + `meta.mentions=[bot]` | groups only; the bot must be a member with a T1/T2 seat (else `E_TIER_UNSUPPORTED`); the target's node builds a Wraith-template prompt ("The user asked you to respond to the latest messages in # <group>") with the group's verified events after the seat's `delivered_seq` (the watermark, DECISION-53) up to `upToId` inclusive (the same block rule as §4.8: ≤ 24 KB with the summary line, no count cap; SI-6 allowlist; other bots' messages labelled untrusted, SI-10; the watermark then moves to `upToId`) and queues it on that seat with `origin = 'nudge'`; counts as a user-rooted prompt in the loop-guard; never Autopilot-eligible (DECISION-51) |
| `node.roots` | D→R→N | – (not persisted) | owner or `device` (not watch, not viewer) | `{node:string, add?:string[], remove?:string[]}` | each added path is realpath'd, must exist, be a directory, and must not be `/`, `~` itself or an ancestor of `~` (`E_PATH_NOT_DIR`) |
| `node.rootsListing` | N→R→D | – | node | `{roots:string[]}` with `meta.to = "device:<requester>"` | – |
| `node.configure` | D→R→N | ✓ | owner or `device` (not watch, not viewer) | `{node:string, approvalTimeoutMin?:int(1–240), autopilotAsk?:string[](≤ 50 globs, each ≤ 200), autopilotOff?:("git-push"\|"git-destroy"\|"publish"\|"rm-recursive"\|"pipe-to-shell"\|"network-out"\|"remote-install"\|"system")[]}` (default rules turned off; the hard floor cannot be listed) | node checks the signature + cap, writes `node.json` atomically, applies the new value to **approval requests created after it** (pending ones keep their `expiresAt`) by sending `config` to its running sprites (§6.5), audits it, and answers `node.configured` (DECISION-41) · out-of-range value → `E_SCHEMA_INVALID` (zod) |
| `node.configured` | N→R→D | ✓ | node | `{node, approvalTimeoutMin, autopilotAsk, autopilotOff}` | sent as the reply and on **every node `hello`** (like `bot.registered`), so a device that pairs later sees the current value · shown in Settings |
| `node.paused` | N→R→D | ✓ | node | `{node, paused:boolean}` | sent before the node stops for a pause (`true`) and with its next `hello` on resume (`false`); the relay keeps the latest value per node (`members.paused`), and while it is `true` that node's bots read as `paused`, not `offline` (§9, DECISION-43) |
| `fs.list` | D→R→N | – (not persisted) | device or viewer (not watch) | `{node:string, path:string}` | path must be under a root after realpath (roots are realpath'd at load; prefix check uses `root + "/"`; the listing is refused if the realpath differs from the requested path's resolution); folder names only; hidden folders excluded except managed `.wraith/assistants/*` |
| `ws.changes` / `ws.diff` | D→R→N | – (not persisted) | device or viewer (not watch) | `{bot:Ulid}` / `{bot:Ulid, path:string}` | bot's workspace must be a git repo (`E_NOT_GIT`); path inside the workspace; deny-list (§4.9) |
| `ws.changesListing` / `ws.diffContent` | N→R→D | – | node | `{files:{path, status, adds, dels, by?:{bot, room, source:"locations"\|"lock_window"\|"terminal", promptId?, approvalId?, approvedBy?:MemberId, at}}[]}` (no `by` = unattributed, §4.9) / `{path, diff:string(≤256KB, redacted), truncated}` with `meta.to="device:<requester>"` | relay forwards only, stores nothing |
| `fs.tree` / `fs.read` | D→R→N | – (not persisted) | device or viewer (not watch) | `{bot:Ulid, path:string}` | P9.13 · inside that bot's workspace after realpath; deny-list; text only (`E_FILE_DENIED`, `E_FILE_TOO_LARGE`) |
| `fs.treeListing` / `fs.content` | N→R→D | – | node | `{path, entries:{name, kind, size, git?}[]}` / `{path, text(≤256KB, redacted), truncated}` with `meta.to="device:<requester>"` | – |
| `fs.listing` | N→R→D | – | node | `{path, dirs:{name, kind:"folder"\|"assistant", hasBot:boolean, botId?}[] (≤ 200), truncated:boolean}` with `meta.to = "device:<requester>"` | relay forwards to that device only and stores nothing |
| `cloud.move` | D→R→N | ✓ | device | `{bot:Ulid, toNode:string, force?:boolean}` | `toNode` must have cap `cloud-host` |
| `roster.update` | D→R→N | ✓ | **owner** | `{version:int, entries:RosterEntry[], revoked:MemberId[], quickMessages:string[](≤ 8, each ≤ 140), signature:string}` | version > pinned |
| `pair.ticket` / `pair.offer` / `pair.complete` | D↔R↔(new member) | – | owner (complete) | §3.4: `{ticketId}` / `{members:{memberKind, signPub, caps[] (advisory for devices), hostPriority?, label}[] (1–2; two only for Wraith.app Join = one node + one device)}` / `{ownerPub, rosterEntries:RosterEntry[], hmac}` | ticket not yet expired and not yet bound (one offer per ticket; binding consumes it) · `hmac` over the entries sorted by memberId (§3.4) · each new member verifies the HMAC and **its own** entry |
| `lease.acquire` / `lease.granted` / `lease.release` / `lease.revoke` / `lease.lost` | N↔R | – | – (authenticated WS) | `{sessionKey:string, bot:Ulid, leaseEpoch?:int, paneRef?:PaneRef, reason?:string}` | no `renew` |
| `push.subscribe` / `push.unsubscribe` | D→R | – | any device cap (device, watch, viewer) | `{endpoint:string, keys:{p256dh,auth}, expirationTime?:int}` | – |
| `system.card` | R→D | ✓ | – | `{kind:"respawned"\|"host_moved"\|"queue_dropped"\|"quota"\|"revoked"\|"info", text:string, ref?:string}` | relay may create it itself because it is display-only (never reaches an agent) |
| `bot.worked_for` | N→R→D | ✓ | **node** | `{bot:Ulid, fromRoom:string, requester:Ulid, promptId:Ulid, task:string(≤200)}` | posted by the target's node into the target's own chat when it runs a handoff from another chat; display-only, never in agent context (DECISION-32) |
| `node.caps` | N→R→D | – | – (authenticated WS) | `{nodeId, caps:NodeCaps}` | sent when the node's caps change after `hello` (e.g. `voice install` finished, engine switched); the relay updates `MemberView.caps` and forwards to devices; display/routing only, never a security decision |
| `voice.transcribe` | D→R→N | – | device or watch | `{id:Ulid, room?:string, mime:"audio/webm"\|"audio/mp4"\|"audio/ogg"\|"audio/wav"\|"audio/opus", durationMs:int(≤ 30_000), audio:base64(≤ 144KB decoded, so the envelope stays < 256KB)}` with `meta.to="relay"` (C77): the relay picks the node and forwards it to one online node whose `caps.voice.ready` is true, preferring the node hosting the bot of the bot chat `room` (if any), then the highest `hostPriority`; none → `E_VOICE_UNAVAILABLE` · `persist:false` · the client records with `MediaRecorder` at `audioBitsPerSecond: 32000` (Opus where supported) so 30 s ≈ 120 KB | P9.18, DECISION-55: the node converts with ffmpeg, runs its engine, deletes the audio, and answers only the requester; nothing is stored or logged except `{id, engine, ms, chars}` |
| `voice.text` | N→R→D | – | node | `{id:Ulid, text:string(≤ 2000), engine:"whisper-local"\|"cloud", model:string, ms:int, fixedMentions:{said:string, handle:string}[]}` with `meta.to="device:<requester>"`, `persist:false` | the client puts `text` into the composer as a draft (never sends it); `fixedMentions` are the spoken names the node replaced with `@handles`: the node matches against **every bot in its verified `bot.registered` copy** (all nodes), case-, space- and hyphen-insensitive on the handle or a handle segment (`app-v100` ← "app v100"), plus the owner's **spoken aliases** per bot (`bot.configure{aliases:string[] ≤ 5, each ≤ 32}`, e.g. "แอป" for `@app`, set in the bot card); the client drops a fix whose handle is not a member of the open room |
| `media.shared` | N→R→D | ✓ | node | `{media:MediaRef, bot:Ulid, promptId?:Ulid, caption?:string(≤ 500), preview:boolean}` | P9.19 · from `wraith_share_file` after approval (§7.2.1); the card shows the thumbnail; `preview:false` = video whose preview exceeds the cap ("Preview too large · Open on this Mac"); `media.origin="bot"` |
| `media.get` | D→R→N | – | device or viewer (not watch) | `{mediaId:Ulid, variant:"full"\|"preview", fromChunk?:int}` with `meta.to="node:<mediaNode>"`, `persist:false` | P9.19 · `full` = image only (video originals stay on the machine); `preview` = 720p video; node offline → `E_BOT_OFFLINE` at once (never queued, §4.2); device re-sends `media.get{fromChunk}` after 15 s of silence, 3 strikes → viewer error |
| `media.ready` | N→R→D | – | node | `{mediaId, variant, bytes:int, sha256, chunks:int, mime}` with `meta.to="device:<requester>"`, `persist:false` | sent before the first chunk; `full`: `sha256` equals `MediaRef.sha256`; `preview`: the preview's hash; the device trusts the joined bytes only if they match and drops them otherwise |
| `media.chunk` | N→R→D | – | – (WS-authenticated) | `{mediaId, variant, seq:int, data:base64(≤ 32 KB decoded)}` with `meta.to="device:<requester>"`, `persist:false` | at most 8 chunks unacknowledged; the node resends from `upTo+1` after 5 s without `media.ack`; the relay forwards and never stores them |
| `media.ack` | D↔R↔N | – | – (WS-authenticated, never signed in either direction, C77) | `{mediaId\|uploadId, upTo:int}` with `meta.to` = the other end of the transfer, `persist:false` | download: device → node · upload: node → device |
| `media.begin` | D→R→N | – | device | `{uploadId:Ulid, bot:Ulid, name:Basename, mime, bytes:int, sha256}` with `meta.to="node:<bot's node>"`, `persist:false` | P9.19 upload, **bot chats only in v1** (C74); `Basename` = zod `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._ -]{0,199}$`, the node re-derives the extension from magic bytes; answers `E_MEDIA_DENIED`/`E_MEDIA_TOO_LARGE` or starts `media.ack` |
| `media.data` | D→R→N | – | – (WS-authenticated) | `{uploadId, seq:int, data:base64(≤ 32 KB decoded)}` with `meta.to="node:<bot's node>"`, `persist:false` | the relay stamps `relay.from=<memberId>` (routing and DoS only); the node drops frames whose `relay.from` is not the `media.begin` signer, checks the magic bytes on chunk 0, aborts when bytes exceed `media.begin.bytes`, and keeps the file only if it hashes to the signed `sha256` |
| `media.stored` | N→R→D | – | node | `{uploadId, media:MediaRef}` with `meta.to="device:<uploader>"`, `persist:false` | the device then sends `user.message{media:[…]}`; unattached uploads are deleted after `uploadKeepDays` |
| `error` | R/N→D | – | – | `{code:ErrorCode, message:string, ref?:Ulid, retryAfter?:int, data?:object}` | §6.9 · `data` only for the codes that carry extra data, typed per code: `E_WORKSPACE_TAKEN{botId}`, `E_NAME_TAKEN{suggestion}`, `E_CAPABILITY_MISSING{suggested:Ulid[]}` (C77) |

**Supporting enums/shapes:** `MemberView = {memberId, kind:"device"|"node", label, caps?:NodeCaps, online:boolean, lastSeen:int, paused?:boolean}` (§6.2 `hello`, `node.caps`) · `ToolKind = "read"|"edit"|"delete"|"move"|"search"|"execute"|"think"|"fetch"|"other"` (per ACP ✅) · `Option = {optionId:string, name:string, kind:"allow_once"|"allow_always"|"reject_once"|"reject_always"}` ✅ · `BotState` §9 · `CapTag = "gpu"|"local-files"|"cloud"|"autonomous"|"git-only"|"can-approve"|"private"|"untrusted"|"no-handoff"` · `Attachment = {name, mime, text(≤8KB), truncated:boolean}` · `MediaRef = {mediaId:Ulid, node:string, name:string(≤ 200), mime, bytes:int, sha256 (image: SHA-256 of the stripped file a device receives as `full` · video: SHA-256 of the original, identity only; the preview's hash comes in `media.ready`), w?:int, h?:int, durationMs?:int, thumb:base64(≤ 32 KB WebP)\|null, origin:"bot"\|"device"}` (P9.19; never a path) · `SessionRef = {agentKind:"kiro"|"claude"|"other", sessionId, cwd, nodeId, location:"local"|"cloud", title?, lastActive?}` · `CryptEntry = {botId, workspaceKey, botName, role:"main"|"seat", room?, session:SessionRef}` · `Cap = "owner"|"device"|"watch"|"viewer"|"node"|"cloud-host"` · `RosterEntry = {memberId, kind:"device"|"node", signPub:JWK, caps:Cap[], hostPriority?:int, label:string, addedAt:int}`

### 6.3 Node ↔ Relay
- **Authentication:** challenge-signature with the node key in the roster (private key at `~/.wraith/node.key` 0600) · no other secret token · relay sends `{nonce}` (128-bit, single use, 60 s); the member replies `sig = ES256(JCS({v:1, purpose:"ws-auth", space, member, nonce}))`. Devices and the watch API use the same payload
- **hello:** relay sends pending queued items, reports expired leases (`lease.lost`), and sends the latest roster if its version is newer
- **At-least-once in both directions:** node → relay from an on-disk outbox (deleted on `ack{id}`) · relay → node from `outbox_relay` (deleted on node `ack`) · dedupe by `meta.id` at every layer
- **Signed commands received by a node** must always pass the `verifier` first. On failure → `error{E_BAD_SIGNATURE|E_REPLAY|E_EXPIRED|E_CAP_DENIED}` + audit
- **The relay accepts a bot's envelopes only from the node where that bot lives** (`bots.node` = memberId of the WS) · cloud bot = the node holding the current cloud-host lease, and `meta.leaseEpoch` must match

### 6.4 Wraith MCP (bot → local mcp-endpoint → relay)

| Tool | Input (zod) | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `wraith_post` | `{text:string(≤8KB)}` | Post a summary to the room (`bot.post`) |
| `wraith_mention` | `{bot:string(name\|botId), task:string, mode?:"assign"\|"transfer" (default "assign"), refs?:{repo?,branch?,pr?,commit?}, attachments?:Attachment[]}` | Hand off work as a Handoff Packet through loop-guard. The system creates `replyTo` automatically · `assign`: the sender keeps the task and gets the result back as a `return` · `transfer`: the sender hands the whole task over; once the relay accepts the handoff the node sends `prompt.transferred` and the sprite ends the turn at once (§6.5), and the target's result answers the sender's own requester (DECISION-53, §7.3) · a relay error (`E_LOOP_*`, `E_ROOM_FROZEN`, `E_CAPABILITY_MISSING`, `E_REPLY_TO_UNKNOWN`, …) is returned as the tool result and nothing is transferred · one transfer per turn: a second one, or any `wraith_mention`/`wraith_reply` after it in that turn, and a transfer from a `return` or `summary` turn → `E_TRANSFER_NOT_ALLOWED` |
| `wraith_reply` | `{summary:string}` | Send the result back to the requester (if the bot does not call it, the sprite sends the last message of the turn instead, **except for `return` and `summary` prompts and prompts marked transferred** (`prompt.transferred`)) |
| `wraith_ask_human` | `{question:string, choices?:string[]}` | Question card to the user (+ push "Bot asks"). The answer comes back as a `user.message` with `replyTo` |
| `wraith_room_context` | `{n:int(≤50), beforeSeq?:int}` | Latest N messages (or the N before `beforeSeq`, for paging back past the watermark block), **only events with a valid signature**, no thinking or tool commentary |
| `wraith_list_bots` | `{}` | Members + capability tags + machine + state + queue length + `workspace`, `parent`, `children` (§4.8) |
| `wraith_spawn_request` | `{workspace:string, agent:string, name?:string, instructions?:string(≤8000), reason:string(≤500)}` | **Asks** for a new bot (DECISION-20). The node checks: the agent is enabled for bot requests (`agents.json` `spawnableByBots:true`, set in Settings); the workspace is inside the requester's own subtree (DECISION-25); at most 3 bot-requested bots alive in the requester's subtree (counts toward the root budget); `name` defaults to the folder name (`-2` on clash). It emits a node-signed `approval.request{requestKind:"spawn", detail.spawn:{workspace, agent, name, instructions}}`; the sheet shows **the full instructions verbatim** and `summary` is marked truncated, so the watch shows "Open on phone". Options `allow_once`/`reject_once` only; timeout rejects (SI-1). It is a request, **not** an approval tool (SI-7). Requests made while serving a cloud-delegated prompt are labelled `from cloud (untrusted)` (SI-10) |
| `wraith_share_file` | `{path:string, caption?:string(≤ 500)}` | (P9.19, DECISION-57) Show an image or video from the bot's own workspace in the room it is answering. **Asks like `wraith_post`** (§7.2.1: Autopilot may allow it; the approval sheet shows the thumbnail, name, type and size). The node checks it (§4.3 `media`) and emits `media.shared`; returns `{mediaId}` or `E_MEDIA_DENIED` / `E_MEDIA_TOO_LARGE` (images only; a video with an oversized preview still gets a card with `preview:false`). Nothing is shared unless the bot calls this tool. Uploaded media reach the bot as lines in the prompt: `[Attached image: .wraith/media/in/<mediaId>-<name> (image/jpeg, 1280×960, 412 KB)]` |

- Passed via `session/new { mcpServers:[{type:"http", name:"wraith", url:"http://127.0.0.1:<port>/mcp", headers:[{name:"X-Wraith-Bot-Token", value}]}] }` ✅ (ACP requires `mcpCapabilities.http` for http ✅ · **whether Kiro advertises this capability 🧪 Q11**). If not, use the stdio shim `wraith-mcp-stdio` (type stdio is mandatory for every agent ✅) or put it in `.kiro/agents/*.json`
- **MCP has no approval tool** (intentional) · Cloud bots cannot call MCP on localhost 🧪 → they communicate through messages (§7.4.1) · `wraith_mention` to a T0 returns `E_TIER_UNSUPPORTED`

### 6.5 Sprite ↔ wraith-node (sprite-hub)
NDJSON over `~/.wraith/sprite.sock` (0600) · schema in `packages/protocol/src/sprite.ts` · **compatible across one release** (C76): fields are only added, new fields are optional, unknown fields and unknown message types are ignored (zod strip, never `.strict()` on these messages) · the envelope `Meta` inside `event` stays `.strict()` (§6.1), so a new Meta field needs two releases (readers first, then writers) · each release records sprite fixtures to `fixtures/sprite-protocol/<version>/`, and a CI test runs the previous release's fixtures against the current node and the reverse (skipped until a first release exists) · **every per-session row below (`event`, `prompt.*`, `approval.*`, `lock.*`, `bot.prompt`, `bot.cancel`, `prompt.transferred`, `rewind*`, `terminal.*`) carries `sessionId?`, required in swap mode** (DECISION-54)

| Direction | Message | body |
|---|---|---|
| G→N | `hello` | `{botId, role:"main"\|"seat", room?, sessionId?, paneId, pid, epoch, lastAckedId, mode:"acp"\|"exec"\|"sdk", leaseEpoch?, spawnToken, build?}` (`spawnToken` = the pane's `WRAITH_SPAWN`, §7.6; `build` = the sprite's build hash; a value different from the node's is logged and shown in `wraith-node status`, never refused, §4.6.1) · sprite-hub keys connections by `(botId, sessionId)` (a fresh seat gets its sessionId after `session/new`) · **swap mode** (DECISION-54): one connection per bot; `hello` carries `sessions:[{sessionId, role, room, leaseEpoch}]` instead of `role`/`room`/`sessionId`/`leaseEpoch`, sprite-hub routes by `sessionId`, and `bot.prompt`, `bot.cancel`, `lock.*`, `approval.*`, `prompt.*` and `event` carry `sessionId` |
| G→N / N→G | `lock.acquire` / `lock.granted` / `lock.release` | `{toolCallId, approvalId?, sessionId?}` (workspace write lock, §4.8; `sessionId` required in swap mode) |
| N→G / G→N | `session.open` / `session.opened` | `{id, room, seed?:string, fork?:boolean, resume?:string}` / `{id, sessionId, room}` · swap mode only (DECISION-54): the node asks the bot's one sprite to create (`session/new` with `seed` sent as the first context block as in process mode, or `fork:true` = a fork of the bot's **main** session per Q33d) or reopen (`session/load`/`resume`) a seat session; in process mode a seat sprite does this itself at start |
| N→G | `session.lease` | `{id, sessionId, leaseEpoch}` · swap mode only: sent after the relay's `lease.granted` for a session opened at runtime; the sprite stamps it on that session's `meta.leaseEpoch` and reports it in `hello.sessions[]` (process-mode sprites get it from `--lease-epoch`) |
| G→N | `event` | envelope (no sig; node adds meta.node and signs if that type must be signed) |
| G→N | `prompt.started` | `{id}` **before** calling `session/prompt` |
| G→N | `closing` | `{id, reason:"exit"\|"detach"}` · the user's `/exit` or `/detach` in the pane; the node records intent `close` (§7.6) and answers `ack`; the sprite exits 0 only after that ack (C75) |
| G→N | `approval.ack` | `{approvalId, optionId, by}` after the decision is committed at the sprite (for a write-kind `allow_*`: before `lock.acquire`, §7.2; otherwise after the ACP response) |
| G→N | `clock.get` → N→G `clock` | `{}` → `{nowMono, freshAt}` (self-fencing §7.4.2) |
| N→G | `bot.prompt` | `{id, promptId, room, sessionRole:"main"\|"seat", sessionId? (required in swap mode), text, resources?:[{json}], replyTo?, kind}` · the node stamps `meta.room` on every sprite event of a prompt from that prompt's `room` (by `promptId`); MCP tools (`wraith_post`, `wraith_room_context`, `wraith_mention`) resolve "the room" the same way |
| N→G | `prompt.transferred` | `{id, promptId, target:Ulid}` · sent only after the relay acked a `room.handoff` with `mode:"transfer"` made during that prompt (DECISION-53); the sprite marks the prompt transferred, ends the turn at once (ACP `session/cancel`, SDK `interrupt()`; tool calls still running in that turn are cancelled), posts the text so far as `bot.message.done{stopReason:"transferred"}` and sends **no** auto-return; the node refuses later `wraith_mention`/`wraith_reply` for that prompt (`E_TRANSFER_NOT_ALLOWED`) |
| N→G | `approval.answer` | `{id, approvalId, epoch, optionId, by}` |
| G→N | `rewindPoint` | `{promptId, agentRef}` · sent when the sprite learns the agent's id for a prompt (Claude user-message `uuid`, Kiro checkpoint tag); the node stores it in `rewind_points` |
| N→G / G→N | `rewindPoints` / `rewindPointsListing` | `{id}` / `{id, points:{promptId, filesAfter:int\|null, conversation, code}[]}` (dry runs per point) |
| N→G / G→N | `rewindPreview` / `rewindPreviewListing` | `{id, toPromptId, restore}` / `{id, files:{path, action}[]}` (the node computes `filesHash`) |
| N→G | `rewind` | `{id, toPromptId, restore, filesHash?}` · for Claude the SDK session must be open (resume with an empty prompt first) 🧪 · the sprite maps `toPromptId` to the agent's own point (Claude: user-message uuid → `rewindFiles` and/or resume with `resumeSessionAt`; Kiro: checkpoint tag → `/checkpoint restore <tag> --hard`) and answers `event{bot.rewound}` (DECISION-47) |
| N→G | `config` | `{approvalTimeoutMin:int}` · sent right after the sprite's `hello` and again on every accepted `node.configure`; the sprite uses the latest value for approval requests it creates afterwards (DECISION-41) |
