| N→G | `terminal.enter` / `terminal.exit` | `{id, argv:string[], env:object, guardPath?:string, run?:string, exitKeys:string[]}` / `{id}` · §8.1; the sprite cannot type into a TTY it handed to the TUI, so it asks the node, which types `run` with `terminal.keys` (no Enter) and sends `exitKeys` (from `agents.json` `terminal.exit`, which ends with `\r`) as `terminal.send` of the text before the `\r` (pasted and submitted; the only input Wraith submits to a TUI on its own), only while the TUI reports `idle` · the node does this itself: it types `run` after the sprite's first `terminal.state{idle|busy}`, and sends `exitKeys` on Back to Wraith; `run`/`exitKeys` in the N→G message are informational for the sprite · `exit`, `neverSend` and `terminalCommands` come from a protocol-side agent catalogue (`packages/protocol/src/agents.ts`), never written by `doctor` |
| G→N | `terminal.state` | `{state:"entering"\|"idle"\|"busy"\|"exiting"\|"off", sessionId, pid?}` |
| G→N | `terminal.return` | `{id, sessionId, leafRef, interrupted:boolean, turns:{role, text, at}[] (≤ 50, redacted), newSessionId?}` · best-effort import; the node owns the return path from `terminal_stays` (git diff, settings diff, model and session checks, `bot.terminalTurn`, `bot.terminalReturn`) and runs it even without this message (after a sprite crash or a lost pane: `turns:0`, card note "turns could not be imported") |
| N→G | `terminal.resume` | `{id, epoch}` · sent only after the node's return path finished, the SI-2 re-check passed and any `needs_resolve` was answered; the sprite never restarts its driver without it |
| N→G | `bot.cancel` / `bot.shutdown` | `{id, reason, sessionId?}` (swap mode: `bot.cancel` names the session; `bot.shutdown` ends them all) |
| N↔G | `ack` | `{id}` in both directions |

- The sprite sends `hello` first, then `G→N ping` every 15 s (lock holders are expired after 60 s without a ping or on socket close) · the node sends `ack` periodically. The sprite removes acked events from its ring buffer
- If the sprite crashes and the node finds a prompt in `started` → **do not resend**. Show the `E_PROMPT_PARTIAL` card "prompt may have partially run — retry?"
- On sprite `hello`, the node **resends** un-acked prompts (with their stored payload, never a rebuilt watermark block, §4.8) and approval answers still in `pending_commit` (the sprite dedupes by `id`)
- **The sprite does not verify signatures itself.** The node has verified them before sending

### 6.6 Handoff Packet (one format for every bot)

```json
{
  "promptId": "01JD…", "root": "01JC…", "replyTo": "01JC…", "kind": "task",
  "from": { "bot": "architect", "botId": "01JB…", "node": "macbook-pro" },
  "to":   { "bot": "tester", "botId": "01JE…" }, "mode": "assign",
  "task": "Run load test on PR #12 and report p95 latency",
  "refs": { "repo": "github.com/me/app", "branch": "wraith/01JB/01JD", "pr": 12, "commit": "a1b2c3d" },
  "attachments": [ { "name": "bench.log", "mime": "text/plain", "text": "… ≤ 8KB …", "truncated": false } ],
  "context": "Room summary ≤ 30 lines (context-builder of the sending node)",
  "constraints": { "forwardDepth": 1, "budgetLeft": 9, "requires": ["gpu"] }
}
```
- `promptId/replyTo/kind/to/requires` are copied to `meta.prompt` for the relay to route and check capability · the relay computes `root`/`forwardDepth`/`budgetLeft` itself and puts them in `relay`, and **rejects a `replyTo` that is unknown or is not a prompt the sending bot is currently working on** (`E_REPLY_TO_UNKNOWN`)
- **`mode`** (DECISION-53): `assign` (default) or `transfer`; copied into `meta.prompt.handoffMode` so the relay routes the return (§7.3)
- **Code or large files always go via `refs` (git)** · `attachments` total ≤ 8KB and pass through redaction
- **Delivery by receiver:** T1 = ACP `session/prompt` with the task text + a JSON resource block of the packet · **Cloud** = plain text from a template (§7.4.1) with marker `[wraith:<promptId>]`
- **`requires`:** receiver lacks the capability → `E_CAPABILITY_MISSING` with a suggested suitable bot

### 6.7 HTTP API for watch and non-WS clients (`/api/v1`, all adapters) 💡

**Authentication (every request except `/pair`):** header
```
Authorization: WraithSig v1;member=<memberId>;ts=<unix-ms>;nonce=<ulid>;sig=<base64url>
sig = ES256( JCS({ v:1, member, ts, nonce, method, path, query, bodySha256 }) )   // bodySha256 of the raw body ("" if none)
```
Field encodings (C78): `ts` = JSON integer (unix ms) · `nonce` = the ULID string · `method` = upper-case as sent · `path` = raw request path without the query · `query` = raw query string without `?` (`""` if none), not decoded or re-sorted · `bodySha256` = base64url of SHA-256 of the raw body bytes, or `""` when the request has no body (not the hash of an empty body). The payload builders live in `packages/crypto` (S-crypto); the header text format and the window/nonce checks stay with the relay.
Relay checks: member is in the roster and not revoked · `|now − ts| ≤ 5 min` · nonce not reused within 10 min (`api_nonces`) · on failure → 401 `E_BAD_SIGNATURE`/`E_REPLAY` · per-member rate limit: 120 req/min → 429 `E_RATE_LIMITED`

| Method & path | Query / body | Response | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET /api/v1/inbox` | `since=<seq>` `wait=<0..25>` (seconds) | `200 {seq, now, approvals:ApprovalView[], bots:BotSummary[], rooms:{id,name,frozen}[], cards:SystemCard[], voice:boolean}` (`voice` = some node has `caps.voice.ready`; the watch hides **🎙 Speak** when false) · `304` if nothing new after `wait` | `ApprovalView = {approvalId, bot, botName, node, kind, title, summary(≤120), expiresAt, epoch, options, requestHash, envelope}` (`envelope` = the full `approval.request` with sig so the client can verify it) · long-poll uses `Timers`; in CF, alarms cannot be used per request, so `setTimeout` is used in the DO while the request is pending (keeps the DO awake for the whole `wait` → limited to 25s and 1 pending poll per member) |
| `POST /api/v1/cmd` | body = signed envelope (`user.message`, `approval.respond`, `room.freeze`) | `202 {id, seq?}` or `4xx {error}` | relay checks the same as WS, then routes; node re-checks as usual · cap watch is checked here first (`E_CAP_DENIED`) |
| `GET /api/v1/roster` | – | `200 {version, ownerPub, entries, revoked, signature}` | owner-signed as a whole. The client pins `ownerPub` at pairing and verifies the signature every time |
| `GET /api/v1/approvals/:id` | – | `200 ApprovalView` (with full `detail`) | For "Open on phone", not for the watch |
| `GET /api/v1/bots` | `group?` | `200 {bots:BotSummary[]}` | `BotSummary = {botId, name, workspaceKey, kind, parent?, node, tier, state, queue, location}` |
| `POST /api/v1/pair` | `{ticketId, memberKind:"device", caps:["watch"], signPub, label}` | `200 {pairSession, ownerPub?}`, then the client polls `GET /api/v1/pair/:pairSession` until it gets `{rosterEntries, hmac}` | No auth, but requires a valid ticket · per-IP rate limit · `caps` is advisory; the owner's signed entry decides (a watch code is always `[watch]`) |
| `GET /api/v1/quick-messages` | – | `200 {messages:string[]}` | List set by the owner in Settings (defaults §14.4) |
| `POST /api/v1/voice` | body = signed `voice.transcribe` envelope | `200 {id, text, fixedMentions}` (relay holds the request ≤ 25 s for the node's `voice.text`) · `504 E_VOICE_TIMEOUT` | watch dictation (P9.18); the watch shows the text and sends `user.message{via:"voice"}` only after **Send** |
| `GET /healthz` | – | `200 {ok, version, mode, spaceCreated}` | No auth; `spaceCreated` drives the Settings → Relay wizard (§3.4 item 7); never reveals `spaceId` |

**Error body:** `{error:{code, message, retryAfter?, data?}}` (`data` as in the §6.2 `error` row) using `E_*` from §6.9 · HTTP status per the column in that table

### 6.8 `relay-core` adapter interfaces (TypeScript) 💡

```ts
// packages/relay-core/src/ports.ts
export interface Storage {
  /** Run one SQL statement (parameterized), return rows; must be synchronous-safe in DO (sql.exec) and Node (better-sqlite3) */
  exec<T = Record<string, unknown>>(sql: string, ...params: SqlValue[]): T[];
  /** Run several statements atomically; in DO = ctx.storage.transactionSync, in Node = db.transaction */
  transaction<T>(fn: () => T): T;
  /** Apply relay-core migrations (§10.3); return the current version */
  migrate(migrations: Migration[]): number;
  /** Small, frequently read key-value (boot_ts, roster version) — DO: storage.get/put, Node: kv table */
  getKV<T>(key: string): T | undefined;
  putKV<T>(key: string, value: T): void;
}

export interface SocketRef { readonly memberId: MemberId; readonly kind: "device" | "node"; readonly tags: string[]; }
export interface Sockets {
  /** Send a message (string) to every socket with a matching tag; the adapter batches; return the number sent */
  send(tag: string, data: string): number;
  broadcast(kind: "device" | "node" | "all", data: string): number;
  list(tag?: string): SocketRef[];                       // DO: ctx.getWebSockets(tag)
  close(tag: string, code: number, reason: string): void;
  /** Time of the runtime's last auto-response on that socket (ms epoch), or undefined */
  autoResponseTimestamp(ref: SocketRef): number | undefined;
  /** Set the auto-response once at boot: request "ping" → reply "pong" */
  setAutoResponse(request: string, response: string): void;
  getAttachment<T>(ref: SocketRef): T | undefined;       // ≤ 16KB in DO
  setAttachment<T>(ref: SocketRef, value: T): void;
}

export interface Timers {
  now(): number;                                         // wall clock ms
  /** Named durable timer: relay-core stores it in `timers`, then the adapter sets alarm/setTimeout at min(fire_at) */
  schedule(name: string, fireAt: number): void;
  cancel(name: string): void;
  /** Wait within one request (long-poll); DO: setTimeout inside the handler, Node: setTimeout */
  wait(ms: number, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
}

export interface Http {
  fetch(input: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<Response>;   // Web Push to push service, GitHub API (node only)
  randomBytes(n: number): Uint8Array;
  subtle: SubtleCrypto;                                           // same WebCrypto on Worker/Node 22
}

export interface RelayEnv { setupToken: string; vapid?: { publicKey: string; privateKey: string; subject: string }; allowedOrigins: string[]; publicUrl?: string; mode: "local" | "selfhosted" | "cloudflare"; }

export class RelayCore {
  constructor(ports: { storage: Storage; sockets: Sockets; timers: Timers; http: Http }, env: RelayEnv, log: Logger);
  boot(): void;                                                   // migrate, boot_ts, setAutoResponse, reschedule alarm
  // ---- WebSocket lifecycle (called by the adapter) ----
  onOpen(ref: SocketRef): void;
  onMessage(ref: SocketRef, data: string): void;
  onClose(ref: SocketRef, code: number, reason: string, wasClean: boolean): void;
  onError(ref: SocketRef, err: unknown): void;
  onAlarm(now: number): void;                                     // multiplexed: read `timers` and run handlers that are due
  // ---- HTTP (adapter converts Request → call) ----
  http: {
    createSpace(setupToken: string, ownerPub: JWK): Result<{spaceId, recoveryCode}>;
    challenge(memberId: MemberId): { nonce: string; expiresAt: number };
    verifyChallenge(memberId: MemberId, nonce: string, sig: string): boolean;
    api(req: ApiRequest): Promise<ApiResponse>;                   // §6.7
  };
}
```
- **relay-core rules:** imports nothing from `node:*` or `cloudflare:*` · no global mutable state whose loss would break logic (everything can be re-read from `Storage`) · all tests run against an `InMemoryAdapter` that can simulate hibernation (`adapter.hibernate()` clears all in-memory state except Storage and still-open sockets)
- **Adapter conformance suite** (`packages/relay-core/test/conformance/*.test.ts`) runs against 3 adapters: in-memory, Node (temporary in tmpdir), Cloudflare (`@cloudflare/vitest-plugin`, formerly `vitest-pool-workers`)

### 6.9 Error code catalogue 💡

| Code | Raised by | HTTP | Meaning | What the client must do |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `E_BAD_SIGNATURE` | relay, node, pwa | 401 | Signature fails, or `sig.by` does not match the WS | Show a warning card (may signal relay tampering) + audit · never auto-retry |
| `E_SCHEMA_INVALID` | relay, node, sprite | 400 | Envelope or body fails the zod schema (other than version/size) | Do not retry; log the field path (never the body) |
| `E_REPLAY` | relay, node | 409 | Duplicate `meta.id` or nonce | Do nothing (idempotent) |
| `E_EXPIRED` | node | 410 | `ts` older than 7 days, or approval past `expiresAt` | Show that it is past time |
| `E_UNKNOWN_MEMBER` | relay | 401 | Member is not in the roster | Must pair again |
| `E_REVOKED` | relay, node | 403 | Key has been revoked | Device: clear key + show pairing screen · Node (machine role revoked): stop sprites, delete `node.key` and the relay binding, exit (§3.4) |
| `E_CAP_DENIED` | relay, node | 403 | Signer's cap is insufficient (e.g. watch requests `allow_always`, viewer sends `user.message`) | Hide that control via the `cap()` gate (§14.1) |
| `E_ROSTER_STALE` | node | 409 | Roster version lower than pinned | Relay sends the latest roster |
| `E_BAD_SETUP_TOKEN` | relay | 403 | Wrong `POST /space` token | Show how to find the token |
| `E_SPACE_EXISTS` | relay | 409 | Space created twice | Go to the pair page instead |
| `E_TICKET_INVALID` / `E_TICKET_EXPIRED` | relay | 404 / 410 | pairing ticket | Owner creates a new one |
| `E_PAIR_HMAC_MISMATCH` | node, watch, device (incl. the Wraith.app window) | – | HMAC mismatch at pairing (wrong secret or MITM) | Abort pairing + clear warning |
| `E_APPROVAL_UNKNOWN` | sprite | 404 | approvalId not in the map | refresh |
| `E_ALREADY_RESOLVED` | sprite/relay | 409 | Already answered (including timeout) | Show the actual result |
| `E_STALE_EPOCH` | sprite/node | 409 | Approval epoch does not match (agent restart) | Close the sheet, show expired |
| `E_REQUEST_HASH_MISMATCH` | node | 400 | The user saw a different version of the request | Show the new request for re-confirmation |
| `E_WORKSPACE_TAKEN` | node | 409 | The workspace already has a bot (`{botId}` included) | Open that bot instead |
| `E_PATH_OUTSIDE_WORKSPACE` | node | 403 | A bot asked to create a bot outside its own workspace | Ask the lead bot, or create it from the New-bot screen |
| `E_SEAT_LIMIT` | node | 409 | The bot already has `maxSeatsPerBot` live seats (`state ≠ 'closed'`) (checked by the node before starting a seat; the relay only forwards) | Remove it from another group first |
| `E_OPTION_INVALID` | node | 400 | `approval.respond.optionId` is not among the request's options (e.g. `allow_always` while seats exist) | Refresh the sheet |
| `E_SEAT_READ_ONLY` | node | 403 | Take control / typing into a group seat's pane | Write in the group chat instead |
| `E_NOT_MEMBER` | relay | 403 | A user `@mention` in a group names a bot that is not a member | Offer "Add to group" |
| `E_CLOUD_NO_GROUPS` | relay | 409 | Kiro Cloud bots cannot join groups (DECISION-31) | Talk to it in its own chat |
| `E_SEATS_UNSUPPORTED` | node | 409 | The bot's agent has `seats:"none"` (Q33a and Q33c failed, DECISION-54) | Talk to it in its own chat |
| `E_TRANSFER_NOT_ALLOWED` | node | 409 | A second transfer, or `wraith_mention`/`wraith_reply` after a transfer, in the same turn; or a transfer from a `return`/`summary` turn (DECISION-53) | Tool result only; the turn is ending |
| `E_NAME_TAKEN` | relay | 409 | Another bot in the Space already uses that name (`{suggestion}` included) | Offer the suggestion |
| `E_PATH_NOT_DIR` | node | 400 | Path does not exist, is not a folder, or is `/` or the home folder itself | Check the path |
| `E_NOT_GIT` | node | 409 | Changes asked for a workspace that is not a git repo | Show "Changes need git" |
| `E_REWIND_UNSUPPORTED` | node | 409 | The bot's agent cannot rewind (or not that kind of restore), or Kiro checkpointing is off | Hide Rewind / show how to enable it |
| `E_AGENT_UNSAFE` | node | 409 | Any SI-2 violation: launch argv or config would run tools without asking (Kiro `--trust-all-tools`, Claude setting sources, a non-asking permission mode or prompts value, allowed tools or managed-settings allow-rules, Grok `always-approve`) | Show which setting to change; the bot does not start |
| `E_REWIND_POINT_GONE` | node, sprite | 410 | The agent no longer has that checkpoint (older than its limit, cleaned up, other session) | Refresh the list |
| `E_BOT_BUSY` | node | 409 | Rewind asked while the bot is working | Stop it first |
| `E_REWIND_CHANGED` | node | 409 | Files to restore changed since the preview (hash mismatch) | Show the new list for re-confirmation |
| `E_TERMINAL_UNSUPPORTED` | node | 400 | Terminal mode for a T0, cloud, seat or `exec` session, or an agent without by-id TUI resume | Hide the Open in terminal button |
| `E_TERMINAL_BUSY` | node | 409 | Enter while working / approval open / lock held (swap mode: any session of the bot running a turn), or a device exit without a reliable idle signal | "Stop @bot first" / "Exit in the terminal" |
| `E_TERMINAL_ACTIVE` | node | 409 | Commands aimed at the main session while it is in Terminal mode: `bot.cancel{room:dm-<botId>}`, Autopilot on, Rewind, `bot.configure{instructions\|agent}`, `bot.move`, `crypt.resurrect` of that bot, a second `enter` (group `bot.cancel` is unaffected; handoffs, nudges and seat prompts queue, §8.1) | Show the banner; open console |
| `E_TERMINAL_CLOSE_TIMEOUT` | sprite | 504 | The driver process still exists 5 s after SIGKILL (10 s → SIGTERM → 5 s → SIGKILL → 5 s) | Stay in Wraith mode; retry |
| `E_TERMINAL_LAUNCH_FAILED` | sprite | 502 | The TUI did not start within 15 s or exited with an error before its first screen | The node runs the return path and sends `terminal.resume` (the sprite waits for it as after any exit); show the pane output |
| `E_TERMINAL_SESSION_DIVERGED` | node | 409 | After exit the TUI's session id differs (`/clear`, `/branch`, `/resume`, Kiro `/rewind`) | Follow the new session / keep the old one |
| `E_TERMINAL_SETTINGS_CHANGED` | node | 409 | The TUI wrote allow rules, a permission mode, `hooks.*` entries or new files among the settings files checked by SI-2 | Remove them and continue / Keep (the note "the bot can't start" only when `si2WouldPass` is false) |
| `E_TERMINAL_RESUME_FAILED` | sprite | 500 | The normal driver could not resume the session after exit | Bot → `failed`; Resurrect from the Crypt |
| `E_FILE_DENIED` | node | 403 | Path outside the bot's workspace, on the deny-list, git-ignored or binary | Explain why; no retry |
| `E_FILE_TOO_LARGE` | node | 413 | File or diff over 256 KB | Show the first part and "too large" |
| `E_MEDIA_DENIED` | node | 403 | `wraith_share_file` or upload outside the bot's workspace, on the deny-list, or not an allowed image/video type by magic bytes (SVG, HEIC from bots, executables renamed) | Explain why; no retry |
| `E_MEDIA_TOO_LARGE` | node | 413 | Image > `imageMaxMB`, upload > `uploadMaxMB`, or upload data beyond `media.begin.bytes` (an oversized video preview is not an error: the card says "Preview too large · Open on this Mac") | Show the limit |
| `E_MEDIA_GONE` | node | 410 | The cached copy was evicted and the original was moved, deleted or no longer hashes to `MediaRef.sha256` (image: after re-stripping; video: original) | Card shows "No longer on <machine>" |
| `E_FOLDER_EXISTS` | node | 409 | `createFolder` for a folder that already exists | Pick the existing folder instead |
| `E_PATH_OUTSIDE_ROOTS` | node | 403 | Path is not under `workspaceRoots` after realpath | Show the configured roots |
| `E_LEASE_HELD` | relay | 409 | The session already has a lease holder | UI goes to the pane/bot that holds it |
| `E_LEASE_HELD_EXTERNAL` | sprite (Kiro lock) | – | "active in another process" ✅ | Show + retry every 60s (Q18) |
| `E_LEASE_LOST` | relay | – | The previous holder lost the lease (old `leaseEpoch`) | Sprite exits |
| `E_NOT_LEASE_HOLDER` | relay | 403 | Node sent events for a bot it does not hold | Drop the event |
| `E_BOT_OFFLINE` / `E_BOT_NOT_FOUND` | relay | 503 / 404 | bot/node is not online or does not exist | Show "waiting for X (node offline)" / fix the mention |
| `E_TIER_UNSUPPORTED` | relay, node | 400 | Handoff to a T0, or Autopilot / Nudge for a bot that cannot use it | Suggest another bot / hide the control |
| `E_CAPABILITY_MISSING` | relay | 400 | Receiver lacks `requires` | Message includes `suggested:[botId]` |
| `E_LOOP_DEPTH` / `E_LOOP_BUDGET` / `E_LOOP_RATE` | relay | 429 | loop-guard | Card in the room + Freeze button |
| `E_ROOM_FROZEN` | relay | 423 | Room is frozen | Unfreeze button (device only) |
| `E_REPLY_TO_UNKNOWN` | relay | 400 | `replyTo` is not a prompt in progress | sprite log; do not forward |
| `E_QUOTA_EXCEEDED` | relay (CF) | 503 | Cloudflare quota exhausted | node local-only + status bar |
| `E_QUEUE_DROPPED` | relay | – | Queue of a node offline > 7 days was dropped | Notification card with the count |
| `E_PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE` | relay, node | 413 | envelope > 256KB / frame > 64KB / attachment > 8KB | Truncate or send via refs |
| `E_RATE_LIMITED` | relay | 429 | Rate limit exceeded (API, pair, space) | Wait `retryAfter` |
| `E_ORIGIN_DENIED` | relay | 403 | `Origin` not in the allowlist | Configure `allowedOrigins` |
| `E_UNSUPPORTED_VERSION` | all sides | 400 | `v` ≠ 1 | Update the client |
| `E_TAKECONTROL_REQUIRED` / `E_TAKECONTROL_EXPIRED` / `E_INPUT_COUNTER_STALE` | node | 403 | console input without a session / timed out / `n` not increasing | Request take control again |
| `E_PROMPT_PARTIAL` | node | – | Sprite crashed during a prompt | "retry?" card |
| `E_AGENT_FAILED` | sprite | – | Agent crashed 3 times | bot `failed` + reason |
| `E_REDACTION_BLOCKED` | node | – | body contains a secret the redactor cannot remove | Do not send + card showing the location |
| `E_AGENT_UNAVAILABLE` | node | 400 | Agent not installed on that node | Install the CLI, or pick another agent |
| `E_HERDR_UNAVAILABLE` | node | 503 | Cannot connect to the herdr-core socket, or its protocol generation is not 1 | Show `hc server start` (or `hc update`) |
| `E_SPRITE_DUPLICATE` | node | 409 | A second sprite connected for a session that already has one (C75, §7.6) | The refused sprite exits 75; the node kills the pane whose `WRAITH_SPAWN` differs from the slot's `spawn_token` |
| `E_VOICE_UNAVAILABLE` | node, relay | 503 | no node with a working voice engine (`caps.voice`) | Mic shows "Voice needs a Wraith node with Whisper" + `wraith-node voice install` |
| `E_VOICE_TOO_LONG` | relay, node | 413 | `durationMs` > 30 000 or audio > 144KB | Client stops recording at 30s |
| `E_VOICE_FAILED` / `E_VOICE_TIMEOUT` | node / relay | 500 / 504 | engine crashed or returned nothing / no `voice.text` within 25 s (WS clients give up at 30 s) | "Couldn't hear that, try again"; nothing is sent |
| `E_RELAY_UNREACHABLE` | app (Settings → Relay) | – | `GET <url>/healthz` failed (DNS, TLS, timeout 10 s) or the answer is not a Wraith relay (a Wraith relay with another contract version → `E_UNSUPPORTED_VERSION`) | Check the URL; for Cloudflare finish the deploy first |
| `E_RELAY_SWITCH_LOCAL_ONLY` | app | – | Relay cards used where no local node is driven by this app (device-only Wraith.app, browser, phone) | "Change the relay on a machine: Wraith.app or `wraith-node`" |
| `E_INTERNAL` | all sides | 500 | Other | log + retry with backoff |

---
## 7. Key Flows

### 7.1 Message to a bot (T1)
```
PWA: parse @mention → user.message{meta.mentions} + sig(device) → relay: seq, persist
 → router(meta) → loop-guard → queue/forward to the bot's node
 → node: verify sig + dedupe by meta.id → sprite-hub → sprite.queue.push → prompt.queued
 → sprite: session/prompt ✅ → agent_message_chunk* → ring buffer → node → relay(persist:false) → PWA (stream)
 ← stopReason → bot.message.done (persist, node-signed) → prompt.done → state idle (+ OSC 7501 `done`)
```
FIFO prompt queue at the sprite · "Interrupt" = `bot.cancel` → `session/cancel` ✅ (ACP agents) / `interrupt()` (Claude, `--mode sdk`) — whatever key the agent uses in its own terminal; UI: the ■ Stop send button, desktop shortcut Esc Esc, DECISION-45/46 · **A message with no mention is just posted to the room and goes to no agent** (no implicit broadcast) to prevent accidental commands 💡

### 7.2 Approval (core of differentiator 2)
```
agent ─session/request_permission (🧪 Q3)─► sprite: approvalId = ULID, epoch = E
   sprite keeps map approvalId → (JSON-RPC id, epoch, 30 min timer)
   └─► approval.request → node: sign with node key → relay (meta + request_hash) → PWA/desktop/watch + Web Push
client: verify node signature against roster → compute requestHash → show sheet
client approval.respond{approvalId, epoch, optionId, requestHash} + sig(device key)
   → relay: check cap (watch → allow_once/reject_once only) → status=pending_commit → node
   → node.verifier: signature valid, device in owner-signed roster, meta.id not duplicate,
       requestHash matches the request the node actually sent, ts < expiresAt
       on failure → error{E_BAD_SIGNATURE|E_REQUEST_HASH_MISMATCH|…} + audit
   → sprite (the single commit point):
       approvalId not in map / epoch ≠ current / already answered → nack{E_APPROVAL_UNKNOWN|E_STALE_EPOCH|E_ALREADY_RESOLVED}
       otherwise, reject_* → ACP response → approval.ack
       otherwise, allow_* on a write-kind tool → stop the timeout timer → approval.ack{optionId}
                    (node signs approval.resolved now) → lock.acquire → state waiting_write
                    → lock.granted → ACP response {outcome:{outcome:"selected", optionId}} → tool runs → lock.release
       otherwise, allow_* on a read-only tool → ACP response → approval.ack
   → node signs approval.resolved (or approval.rejected_late) → relay → every client verifies the signature
```
- The relay cannot forge or alter an approval (it has no device key; the request is signed by the node; the node checks `requestHash`). The relay can only **not deliver** (the approval times out and is rejected)
- **Cancel/crash while `waiting_write`:** the sprite answers the pending `request_permission` with `cancelled`, the node posts `approval.resolved{outcome:"cancelled_after_allow"}` for clarity, and nothing runs; after a crash the agent re-asks under a new epoch
- **Transfer while approvals are open** (DECISION-53): `prompt.transferred` cancels the turn; its open approvals → `approval.resolved{outcome:"cancelled"}` (`cancelled_after_allow` while `waiting_write`), no grant is kept, nothing runs, and the holder's write lock is released
- **Lock ordering (DECISION-31/32):** a verified `allow_*` **stops the timeout timer** and the approval is resolved at once (the UI shows "Allowed"); only then does the sprite wait for the workspace write lock (`waiting_write`). A long lock wait can never turn an Allow into a timeout reject; any later answer → `E_ALREADY_RESOLVED`
- **Timeout** (default 30 min per machine, `approvalTimeoutMin` 1–240, changed from Settings via signed `node.configure` and pushed to running sprites with `config` (§6.5), DECISION-41; timer lives at the sprite only, runs only until an answer is verified): the sprite answers `reject_once` (or `cancelled` if there is no reject option). **A timeout never approves** (SI-1). Then ack `by:"timeout"`

#### 7.2.1 Autopilot (DECISION-49, DECISION-50)

For long unattended runs a bot can be put on **Autopilot**: the agent still asks for every tool call (ACP `request_permission` / SDK hook / Kiro hook), but the **node** answers `allow_once` itself when every rule below holds, instead of waiting for a device. Agent-native bypass flags stay forbidden (SI-2), so Wraith keeps every card, the write lock, Changes attribution and the audit. **Honest scope:** inside an eligible turn the node approves on the user's behalf, so text the agent reads during that turn (a web page, a repo file, `CLAUDE.md`) can make it act; the bounds are the bot's write area, the classifier below, the time limit and visibility — not a sandbox (trust boundary 7).

| Rule | Detail |
|---|---|
| Turned on by a human | `bot.configure{bot, autopilot:{on:true, until?:int}}` signed by the owner or a Full device (not watch, not viewer). `until` = unix ms, `ts < until ≤ ts + 24 h` (else `E_SCHEMA_INVALID`); absent = until turned off (UI offers 1 h / 2 h / 8 h / until I turn it off). T1/T2 local bots only: T0, `shell` and cloud bots → `E_TIER_UNSUPPORTED`. Stored in `autopilot` (§10.2), announced in `bot.registered/updated.autopilot` |
| Turns off | `on:false`; expiry; `bot.terminal{enter}` (DECISION-52); **any** `bot.cancel` or `bot.kill` for that bot (bot-wide, not only one chat; the end of a turn by `prompt.transferred` is not a `bot.cancel` and leaves Autopilot unchanged); `room.freeze{true}` of the bot's own chat **or of any group it is in** (so the watch's group Freeze stops it); `node.paused{true}`; a relay switch (§3.4 item 7, C72); revoke of the device in `set_by`; node restart with an expired `until`. On every off transition the node sends `bot.updated{autopilot:{on:false}}`. A request that is pending at that moment stays a normal request |
| Eligible turns only | **only the bot's main session in its own chat** (`dm-<botId>`), and only when the prompt that started the current turn (the `started` row of `(bot, sessionId, epoch)` in `prompts_local`) has `origin = 'user'`, i.e. a device-signed `user.message` typed in that chat (a confirmed watch voice transcript, `by:"watch"`, `via:"voice"`, counts like any other; the owner accepted this risk: a stolen device is revoked from the roster, C67). **Never** in groups (no seat turn is eligible, DECISION-50), never for `handoff`, `return`, `cloud`, `summary`, `nudge` or `pane` origins, never when the prompt carries an attached summary block or another bot's resources, and never when no `started` prompt exists (Resurrect, restart, `session/load`, Rewind re-send) |
| Classifier (fail-closed) | evaluated by the node when it receives the request, from the ACP tool call (`kind`, `rawInput`, `locations`, diff paths), never from `title`/`summary`. Auto-allow is possible only for: **(a)** `kind ∈ {edit, delete, move}` where every path is present, realpath'd and inside the write area (DECISION-25) and not in the Files deny-list (§4.9); **(b)** `kind = execute` with `rawInput.command` present, `cwd` inside the write area, and every segment passing the deny matcher. **Everything else asks**: `fetch`, `other`, MCP tools except the read-only Wraith tools and the auto-allowable Wraith tools listed below, `requestKind:"spawn"` (DECISION-20), missing paths/command, unknown shapes, a request whose `options[]` has no `allow_once` |
| Deny matcher (execute) | split the command on newlines, `;`, `&&`, `\|\|`, `\|`, `$(`, backticks; for each segment take the first non-assignment token (basename) and its arguments. **Hard floor (always asks, cannot be turned off):** `sudo`/`su`/`doas`; `hc`, `herdr`, `herdr-app` (they reach other machines and start or type into agents outside Wraith, C75); `bash\|sh\|zsh\|fish -c`, `eval`, `source`/`.` or running a script file; any argument that is an absolute path or contains `..` and resolves outside the write area; any path in the Files deny-list. **Default rules (owner can turn each off, `node.configure.autopilotOff`):** `git-push` (`git push`), `git-destroy` (`git reset --hard`, `git clean`, `git checkout -- .`, `git branch -D`, `git stash drop`), `publish` (`npm\|pnpm\|yarn\|cargo\|gem publish`, `docker push`, `wrangler deploy`, `gh release\|repo delete`), `rm-recursive` (`rm` with `-r/-R/-f`), `pipe-to-shell` (`curl\|wget … \|` into `sh\|bash\|zsh\|python\|node`), `network-out` (`curl\|wget` with `-d/-T/--data/--upload-file/-X POST\|PUT`, `ssh`, `scp`, `rsync`, `nc`, `ncat`), `remote-install` (`npx\|uvx\|pipx\|pip install` with a URL or `git+`), `system` (`kill`, `pkill`, `launchctl`, `crontab`, `chmod`/`chown` outside the write area). **Owner patterns** (`node.configure.autopilotAsk`, ≤ 50): case-insensitive globs matched against each normalised segment. The matcher is a convenience filter, not a sandbox: a determined agent can evade it through files it writes and runs later in an eligible turn |
| Wraith MCP tools | `wraith_room_context`, `wraith_list_bots`: read-only, never asked · `wraith_post`, `wraith_reply`, `wraith_mention`, `wraith_ask_human`, `wraith_share_file` (C74; the sheet shows the thumbnail, name, type and size): may be auto-allowed (a handoff's target turn has origin `handoff`, so it always asks) · `wraith_spawn_request`: **always asks** |
| Option | only `allow_once`; never `allow_always` / `reject_*` |
| Result | the node marks the request `autopilot:true` (node-signed body field) **before** sending it, so the relay sends **no push and no watch-inbox entry** for it and clients render it collapsed; it then sends `approval.answer` to the sprite and node-signed `approval.resolved{by:"autopilot"}`; the write lock and `file_touches` work as usual; `approvals_local.answered_by = 'autopilot'` |
| Visible | a purple **Autopilot** badge with the time left in the bot chat header, the bot card and the Home row; turning it on shows what it allows and what still asks |
| Not for | groups, T0/`shell`/cloud bots, Approve only / View only devices (cannot turn it on), the watch |
| Suggested, never automatic | Autopilot is never turned on by itself. When the **same device** answers `allow_once` for the **same bot** **3 times within 10 minutes** in that bot's own chat with `turnOrigin = 'user'`, the next approval sheet shows a strip "Approving a lot? Autopilot for @bot · 1 h" under the buttons; tapping it opens the Autopilot sheet (rules + **Turn on**), the request on screen still needs its own Allow/Reject · "Not for this bot" stops suggestions for that bot on that device (localStorage) · at most once per bot per app session · not shown for requests with another `turnOrigin`, hard-floor or default-rule matches, groups, T0 bots, Approve only / View only devices or the watch (UI-only rule) |
| Code location | all auto-approval code lives in `apps/node/src/autopilot/`; the `no-auto-approve` hook enforces this (§12.1) |

- The UI shows approval state **always matching what the agent actually received** (PBT §21.1)
- **Sprite or agent restart:** all old approvals from the previous epoch are closed as `expired`
- **Relay drops while waiting:** the sprite still holds the request and timer. When the node reconnects, open approvals are resent (dedupe by `approvalId`)
- **If Q3 fails → fallback to Kiro `preToolUse` hook** (requires Q3b: the hook works under `kiro-cli acp`):
  1. hook → `POST /hook {tool, args}` to hook-endpoint
  2. A **one-shot grant** matching `(botId, sessionId, epoch, hash(tool,args,cwd))` exists and has not expired → consume the grant and **allow** (exit 0)
  3. None → create an approval and long-poll until just before the hook timeout. If the user approves in time (signed command) → allow
  4. Still no answer → **block** (exit 2) "awaiting human approval". When the user approves later → create a grant + the sprite sends the prompt "approved, please retry `<tool>`"
  5. **Write lock in hook mode (DECISION-31/32):** for a tool outside the SI-2 read-only set, after step 2 or 3 says allow, the hook-endpoint acquires the workspace lock (same `write_locks` row, holder = that session) before returning exit 0, blocking until granted or just before the hook timeout (then exit 2, the grant is kept for the retry). It is released by a `postToolUse` hook (`POST /hook/done {toolCallId}`), or on pane exit, epoch bump, cancel/kill or `expires_at`
- **T2 (Claude):** `PreToolUse` → `POST /hook` → approval (signed the same way) → allow/deny; write tools take the workspace lock the same way as step 5, released by `PostToolUse`. On timeout, return `ask` ✅
- **T0:** card from the program's own OSC 7501 `blocked` report (no card if it never reports status) ✅ · **Kiro Cloud:** no per-change approval ✅

### 7.3 Handoff and Return
```
app (working on P1, root=R): wraith_mention("app-v100", "run GPU tests on branch X")
 → node(Mac): build packet + context, sign with node key → relay
 → relay computes from prompts: root=R, forwardDepth=depth(P1)+1 → loop-guard(≤ 4, budget(R) ≤ 12, rate)
 → prompt{P2, replyTo:P1, kind:"task"} → node(V100) → verify sig(node Mac) + dedupe → tester queue
tester turn ends → wraith_reply(...) or last message → room.return{P2→P1}
 → prompt{P3, root:R, replyTo:P1.replyTo (or null), forwardDepth:1, kind:"return"} → architect queue
    (return does not count as a forward hop but counts toward budget(R), and when P3's turn ends there is no automatic reply)
```
- **Transfer** (DECISION-53): `wraith_mention(..., mode:"transfer")` → the packet goes out the same way (same loop-guard hop and budget); the relay stores `prompts.handoff_mode = 'transfer'` for P2 and acks; **only then** the node sends `N→G prompt.transferred` and the sender's sprite ends the turn at once (`session/cancel` / SDK `interrupt()`; the text so far is posted with `stopReason: transferred`, card "@app handed this to @tester"; no auto-return). If the relay refuses (`E_LOOP_*`, `E_ROOM_FROZEN`, `E_CAPABILITY_MISSING`, …) the tool result is that error and the turn goes on, untransferred. One transfer per turn; a later `wraith_mention`/`wraith_reply` in it, or a transfer from a `return`/`summary` turn → `E_TRANSFER_NOT_ALLOWED`. When the target's turn ends, the relay resolves `room.return{P2}` from `prompts.handoff_mode`: `assign` → P1; `transfer` → the relay **climbs**: `Q = P2.replyTo` (= P1); while `prompts.handoff_mode(Q) = 'transfer'`, `Q = Q.replyTo`; it delivers to `Q.replyTo` (the climb always ends: `reply_to` points to an older prompt, user prompts default to `assign`, and the chain is bounded by `forwardDepth` ≤ 4; a cloud bot never transfers, so the climb stops at it, and a transfer **to** a cloud bot resolves at its `DONE:`/last message; an offline final target waits in `outbox_relay`; a frozen room still takes the return) — a bot → a `return` prompt; `null`, i.e. the user asked → the return is still posted as the node-signed ReturnCard in the root chat and is the answer, and the relay creates no prompt (so in user → @app → transfer @tester → transfer @reviewer, @reviewer's answer reaches the user and neither @app nor @tester gets a return); the sender gets no `return` · **assign and transfer in one turn are allowed:** handoffs the sender `assign`ed before transferring still return to it later as ordinary `return` prompts (no auto-reply); only the transferred task yields no return · a transferred task can be transferred again only within the same loop-guard limits · cloud `@` lines are always `assign` (§7.4.1)
- **Fan-out:** each return enters the queue separately (no automatic result merging; fan-in = P9)
- **Cross-machine is the normal case.** If uncommitted code is needed, the sender must push the branch first (the node warns if `refs` is empty but the task mentions files)
- **Receiver offline:** the relay keeps the packet in `outbox_relay`. The room shows "waiting for tester (V100 offline)"
- **The receiving node checks** that the signature comes from the node where `from.bot` actually lives per roster/bots

### 7.4 Kiro Cloud bot (ACP only, no TUI parsing)
1. Create a cloud session with `kiro-cli --cloud` or pick one from the session dashboard ✅ (a human does it the first time, or the node spawns `kiro-cli --cloud` in a pane)
2. The sprite (engine v3) on **the node holding the cloud-host lease** calls `session/list`, then `session/load` on the remote session 🧪 Q5 · **Do not use `noReplay`** (remote load does not attach the live update stream if used ✅). Test that updates after load actually arrive (Q5b) · If Kiro advertises `sessionCapabilities.resume`, also try `session/resume`
3. **If Q5 fails → "Cloud Status Bot"** ✅ DECISION-5: `wraith-sprite --mode exec` in a herdr pane. Each prompt spawns `kiro-cli chat --no-interactive --resume-id <id> "<task>"` 🧪 (CLI 2.32 ✅). The sprite owns the stdout pipe and echoes it to the pane · Wait until the turn ends or return immediately? 🧪 Q5c · Session lock "active in another process" ✅ → `E_LEASE_HELD_EXTERNAL` · **PR detection via GitHub API** (fine-grained read-only PAT on a node with cap `cloud-host`), matched by branch `wraith/<botId>/<promptId>`
4. Work exchange between cloud and devices **goes through git**: the cloud bot delivers results as a PR ✅. The PR card has a button "@app-v100 checkout & test" (Q16)
5. Limits: one session open per process at a time ✅, cloud ≤ 10 sessions ✅, plan Pro or higher ✅ (the user already has it)

#### 7.4.1 Cloud bot in Group Chat (DECISION-9 headline feature)
**Receiving work:** the host node renders the Handoff Packet as prompt text (fixed template `packages/protocol/templates/cloud-prompt.txt`):
```
[wraith:<promptId>]
<task>
Refs: <repo> <branch> <pr>
Context:
<context ≤ 30 lines>
---
To delegate, end your reply with lines of the form:
@<bot> <task>            (bots available: app-v100[gpu,local-files], app[local-files])
Name branches wraith/<botId>/<promptId>. Reply "DONE: <summary>" when finished.
```
**Delegating onward (`@name` from ACP messages):**
- The host node reads only **the last message of the turn** from ACP `agent_message_chunk` and accepts only **lines starting with `@<a bot that actually exists>`** (regex `^@([a-z0-9_-]+)\s+(.+)$` per line, at most 4 lines)
- Build a Handoff Packet `from: kiro-cloud` with `refs` = the detected PR/branch · **deterministic `promptId`** = `ulidFromHash(sha256(sourcePromptId ‖ lineIndex))` (26 Crockford characters), so two hosts that see the same message get the same id and the relay dedupes to one
- Passes loop-guard as usual (always `handoffMode:"assign"`; cloud lines have no transfer syntax) · card in the room "delegated by cloud (untrusted)" · the target bot still needs approval for every command
- No `@` lines → treated as a return (`DONE:` or the last message) to the requester
- **If this is not feasible in practice** (e.g. Q5 and Q5c both fail so the last message cannot be read) → fall back to the PR card button and **the human must be informed** (DECISION-9)

**Receiving results from other bots:** a return to the cloud = a new prompt (kind `return`), no automatic reply · **one task at a time** (one session open per process ✅)

#### 7.4.2 Cloud-host lease (who runs the cloud bot's sprite) 💡
**Why it is needed:** Kiro has no public API to send messages into a cloud session. It must go through `kiro-cli` on a logged-in machine, and a Worker/DO cannot run programs · one session open per process ✅, so there must be exactly one holder

**Eligible:** nodes with cap `cloud-host` (enabled by the user; requires `kiro-cli` login, a PAT, and a working native clock helper), ordered by `hostPriority` (1 = highest; default 1 for every node; ties pick the lowest `nodeId` · H3)

**Steps (one protocol for both voluntary move and failover):**
1. The relay picks the online node with the lowest `hostPriority` → `lease.granted{sessionKey, leaseEpoch}` and updates `bots.node`
2. The node starts a pane running `wraith-sprite --bot <cloudBotId> --lease-epoch <n>` (agent `kiro-cloud`)
3. **Voluntary move** (Move host button, or moving back to a higher-priority machine): the relay sends `lease.revoke` → the old host stops sending work, closes the sprite, and replies `lease.release` → the relay grants the lease to the new host (`lease_epoch + 1`) · If the old host is online but does not `release` within 60s → notify the user + **Force move** button (signed command `cloud.move{force:true}`), warning that there may briefly be two hosts (relies on Kiro's lock)
4. **Failover on timeout** (P8): the lease may be reassigned **only when `now ≥ lastSeen + 45s`** · lease alarm every 60s, only while a cloud-host lease exists and another eligible node is online
5. **Self-fencing (old host):** the sprite may send work to Kiro only when `now − freshAt < 35s` (`freshAt` = send time of the latest ping that got a pong; the relay sets `lastSeen` no earlier than `freshAt` and reassigns the lease no earlier than `freshAt + 45s`; margin 10s) · uses a monotonic clock that counts sleep time via a native helper 🧪. If unavailable, **disable cap `cloud-host`** · the sprite asks the node `clock.get` on every check · re-check immediately before writing to `kiro-cli` stdin, and if the event loop stalls > 2s, treat it as not fresh and wait for a new pong
6. The new node resumes the same session id and **fetches back history** (ACP replay or PR) to show the missing part
7. The old host wakes → `hello` → `lease.lost{leaseEpoch}` → kills the cloud bot's sprite · the relay **drops every cloud bot event with an old `leaseEpoch`** · message dedupe by ACP message id 🧪 Q23 (fallback `hash(turnIndex, text)`), handoffs per §7.4.1 · Cloud Status Bot mode has no replay → card "output from previous host may be missing — see PR"

**Trade-off:** fencing shrinks the window in which two hosts send work at once to nearly zero, but **the real duplicate guard is Kiro's lock** ✅
**While no one holds it:** the cloud agent continues the current turn on AWS ✅. New prompts wait in the queue. The room shows "Kiro·Cloud: no host online (queued: 2)"
**Duplicate-send guard:** the new sprite checks whether **user-side messages** in the session history already contain the marker `[wraith:<promptId>]` (agent messages do not count). If so, do not resend · Cloud Status Bot mode shows the card "may have been delivered twice"
**Assumptions that must pass a spike:** Q18 (Kiro lock stuck when the node sleeps) · Q22 (auto-response + hibernation)

### 7.5 The Crypt (P8)
| Source | How to read | How to wake |
|---|---|---|
| Kiro local | ACP `session/list` (v3) ✅ or `kiro-cli chat --list-sessions` (format 🧪 Q7) on the node where the session lives | sprite + `session/load` on the same node |
| Kiro cloud | `session/list` (v3) from any logged-in node | via cloud-host lease (§7.4.2) |
| Claude Code | scan `~/.claude/projects/*/*.jsonl` (metadata only; may break when the format changes) | SDK `resume` / `forkSession` via the sprite (`--mode sdk`) 🧪 Q36 |

- Resurrect = **reuse the same `botId`** (looked up from `crypt.bot_id`) + the same `cwd` + must obtain the lease + a signed command from a device
- Node holding the session is offline → Resurrect button disabled, showing which machine

### 7.6 Restart & Restore
**The node owns restore** (DECISION-58): herdr-core keeps programs running through core restarts and updates ✅, and never re-runs a lost program (`keep` only gives a shell) ✅, so there is no resume argv. The node decides from **its own record of intent**, not from hc's reasons alone.
- **Pane slots:** the node reconciles **panes, not sessions**: one slot per main session and per `process`-mode seat. `swap`-mode seats have no pane; they come back through `session.open{resume}` after the bot's one pane is back (§4.8, DECISION-54)
- **Pane identity = `core_id` + address.** An address (`t7`) names one terminal "forever on this core" ✅ and `core.id` changes only when hc's state is wiped ✅, so `bot_sessions` stores `pane_id` with `core_id` (from `hello` → `core.id`) and a **`spawn_token`** (ULID). The node acts on a stored address only while the connected core's id equals `core_id`; otherwise the slot is lost and the old address is never read, typed into or killed. The `wraith.*` labels are for discovery only: any hc client can change them ✅, so a label mismatch on a known address is audited and changes nothing. The check runs once per connection, per Console drawer open and per take-control session (not on every 250 ms read), and on every `labels`/`finished` event. A `terminal.get`/`terminal.list` record is used only when its `address` equals the stored `pane_id` (the `terminal` param also accepts names, Q2). Addresses of the form `machine/tN` are ignored · **A slot missing from a label-filtered snapshot is not "gone":** a relabelled pane leaves the filtered view ✅, so the node first runs `terminal.get(pane_id)`; if it is still live on the same core, the slot keeps it (audited) and the node watches it with `terminal.get` every 30 s, since its events no longer reach the filtered subscription
- **Starting a pane** (first start and every respawn): write a new `spawn_token` to the slot **before** `terminal.run`, pass it as label `wraith.spawn` and env `WRAITH_SPAWN`, then store the returned address and `core_id`, and clear `close_intent`. If the node dies in between, the next scan **adopts** a live pane whose `wraith.spawn` equals the stored token on the same core instead of starting another
- **Intent** (`bot_sessions.close_intent`), set by the node **before** every stop it causes:
  - `close` → no respawn; release the lease; session to the Crypt: `bot.kill`, `bot.shutdown`, seat close, and the user's `/exit` (alias `/detach`) in the pane (the sprite sends `closing` over `sprite.sock` and waits for the ack; if the node is unreachable it shows "Closing · waiting for wraith-node" and retries after reconnect, and never exits before the ack)
  - `pause` → no respawn, not the Crypt; the bot shows `paused`; the node respawns it when the pause ends (`node.paused`, Pause all bots, Run bots on this Mac off)
  - `moved` → no respawn, not the Crypt: `lease.lost` or a cloud-host move (§7.4.2)
  - `failed` → set when the sprite reports `failed` (agent crashed 3 times in 10 minutes, or `E_TERMINAL_RESUME_FAILED`); the sprite stays in its pane showing the failure until the node stops it; no respawn; the lease is released, so the session is in the Crypt; Resurrect first `terminal.kill`s and `terminal.rm`s the old address (same core), then runs the start step
  - `revoked` → the node's own key was revoked: no respawn; sessions go to the Crypt (§3.4), then the node exits
  - The sprite **exits 0 only after its `closing` was acked, or when the node told it to stop** (`bot.shutdown`); on SIGTERM, SIGHUP or a TTY read error (EIO) it exits **75** without reporting (S-sprite-acp R8)
- **When a slot ends** (`finished` event, the snapshot inside a `missed` notification or a reconnect, or the node-start scan with `terminal.list{all:true, labels:{"wraith.node":<nodeId>}}` plus `terminal.get(pane_id)` for each recorded slot):
  - an intent is set → act on that intent
  - reason `killed` with no intent → **closed by a person**: same as `close`. `hc kill` records `killed` even when the sprite exits 75 on the hangup ✅ (checked 2026-11-05 on 0.1.16); Herdr.app's close button is expected to do the same 🧪 Q6
  - reason `spawn_pending` → **wait**: re-check every 30 s, at most 3 times (hc never starts another program on a guess ✅); then `terminal.kill` it and treat the slot as lost
  - reason `server_stop` (someone ran `hc server stop`) → the slot is lost, but the running node **does not restart the core**; bots show `offline` with "herdr-core was stopped · `hc server start`", and respawn happens when the core is back
  - anything else with no intent → **lost** → respawn: `holder_lost`, `host_shutdown`, `signaled`, `stopped_by_restart`, `exited` with any code, or the address is gone and no pane carries the stored `spawn_token`. A SIGTERM from outside hc (e.g. `kill <pid>` or an OS shutdown) records `exited` with code 75 ✅, which is why 75 counts as lost: after a reboot the bot must come back; what a real reboot records is checked in Q14 🧪
  - The node first persists the outcome (writes `close_intent='close'` for `killed`, or the respawn decision), then `terminal.rm`s the finished pane (every case), so `terminal.list{all:true}` does not grow and a crash in between cannot turn a close into a respawn
- **Respawn** (compare-and-set): only if `bot_sessions.pane_id` still equals the ended address (or is null with no adoptable pane); start the new pane as above with the **respawn argv** `[<absolute path of wraith-sprite>, "--bot", <botId>, ("--seat", <roomId>), "--resume", <sessionId>, ("--lease-epoch", <n>)]` (ids only, never a command string; launch spec from the node, C2, DECISION-26) and the same labels, cwd and env as the first start. The bot is `restoring` (grace 120 s) meanwhile. A sprite crash still needs the lease, as before
- **Respawn cap:** back off 1 s, 5 s, 30 s; `bot_sessions.respawns` holds the unix-ms times of recent respawns, pruned to 10 minutes; a 4th within 10 minutes, or `spawn_failed` from `terminal.run`, sets intent `failed` (above)
- **Duplicates:** sprite-hub accepts one live connection per `(botId, sessionId)` (swap: per bot). When a second `hello` arrives, it keeps the one whose `spawnToken` equals the slot's `spawn_token` and answers the other `E_SPRITE_DUPLICATE` (if neither carries it, both are refused and the slot is treated as lost); that sprite exits 75 by itself, and the node also kills its pane if the address is on the current core (no slot intent is written: the address is not the slot's `pane_id`, so the compare-and-set ignores its end). The sprite exits 75 when its TTY hangs up, so an orphan from a lost holder does not stay connected
- **Cloud bots** are respawned only after the cloud-host lease is re-acquired (§7.4.2)
- **Terminal mode:** a lost pane takes the TUI with it. If the slot has an open `terminal_stays` row, the node **runs the return path first** (git diff, settings check, session check; import `turns:0`). If it reaches resume, the node starts the sprite in Wraith mode (never `claude`/`kiro-cli chat`); a sprite started this way runs SI-2 at launch like any start and needs no `terminal.resume`; if it ends in `needs_resolve`, **no sprite is started** until `bot.terminalResolve`, so no driver exists while the session is `needs_resolve` (PBT #24) (§8.1 step 5)
- **herdr-core missing:** at its own start the node runs `hc server start` once (with `HC_SESSION` set when `node.json` `herdr.session` is not `default`); this applies even if the core was stopped with `server_stop` before the node started. While running, the node never starts the core

| Event | Result |
|---|---|
| PWA disconnects and reconnects | `hello{lastSeq}` → replay or snapshot |
| DO hibernate | No effect |
| DO redeploy / evict | All sockets drop → reconnect → replay · lease grace 45s only for holders that have not come back (counted from `boot_ts`) |
| node disconnects from relay | That machine's bots go `offline`; other machines keep working · outbox keeps events · cloud bot moves via Move host or automatically (P8) |
| sprite **crash** | node sees `finished` with reason `exited` and a non-zero code ✅ → still holds the lease → respawn with the same `botId` |
| user **closes** bot / pane | No respawn → release lease → Crypt |
| herdr-core restart / update / Herdr.app quit | programs keep running ✅ → node reconnects and reconciles from the snapshot; no `restoring` |
| pane lost (holder died) | node respawns the sprite → bot enters `restoring` (grace 120s), does not appear in the Crypt |
| reboot | launchd/systemd starts wraith-node, which starts herdr-core if needed (Q14) → every lost pane is respawned as above |

### 7.7 Lease (prevents opening a session twice; no primary)
- The relay is **the sole lease issuer**: `leases(session_key → holder_node, bot, pane_ref, state, lease_epoch, granted_at)` **entirely in SQLite**
- `state`: `active` | `restoring` | `offline-held`
- **Fencing:** holder change → `lease_epoch + 1` · bot envelopes carry `meta.leaseEpoch`; the relay drops mismatches
- **Local bot:** the lease prevents duplicate opens on the machine and overlapping resurrects · node disconnects → `offline-held`, **does not expire, does not move**
- **Cloud bot:** can move (§7.4.2)
- **Swap mode** (DECISION-54): one lease per session as usual; all of a bot's swap sessions share its one `pane_ref`, and the sprite's `hello.sessions[]` carries each `leaseEpoch`
- **No renew:** liveness comes from the socket + auto-response timestamp at decision time
- Respawn after a lost pane: `restoring` does not appear in the Crypt · Resurrect is allowed only for sessions with no lease and not in grace
- Claude (T2): has no lock of its own ✅. Our lease is the only guard · herdr-core never starts or resumes an agent by itself ✅, so nothing outside the node can reopen a session

---

## 8. Console Mode

**Principle:** Console = a herdr pane, with no parsing of any kind

| Order | Strategy | Status |
|---|---|---|
| S1 | `terminal.attach{observe:true}` raw stream (first paint, then live bytes with colour; watch-only, never resizes) ✅ | P9.5: raw bytes cannot be redacted reliably (escape sequences split secrets), so it needs a redaction design first |
| ~~S2~~ | attach as an active viewer | **Dropped** (an active viewer resizes the pane and disturbs the user's screen at the machine) |
| **S3** ⭐ | poll `terminal.read{lines:0}` (screen as plain text, no colour ✅) every 250ms **only while the drawer is open**, redact, then send `console.frame{mode:"snapshot", persist:false}` only when the hash changes | Main plan 🧪 Q1 (cost), Q20 (size) |

- **Relay load:** drawer open 1 h at 4 fps ≈ 14,400 messages ≈ 720 requests (CF) · if Q1 shows this is too much, drop to 1s
- **Take control:** `console.takecontrol` (signed, session ≤ 10 min), then each `console.input` must be signed + `n` increasing + `ts` within 60s → `text` = `terminal.send{text, force:true}` (pastes and presses Enter after ~3 s without typing at the machine ✅; `force` because hc refuses a `blocked` pane otherwise ✅ — the signed take-control session is the human's intent, and hc's refusal is not an access boundary ✅) · `keys` = `terminal.keys` (named keys only: enter, esc, tab, arrows, ctrl+…, single ASCII letters ✅) + audit · while someone types at the machine, hc holds remote text until ~3 s of quiet, so the drawer shows "waiting for the machine to be quiet" · text goes in as a bracketed paste when the program asked for it ✅ (the sprite strips the markers, S-sprite-acp R9) · on timeout → the PWA returns to read-only automatically
- **Group seats are read-only:** `console.takecontrol`/`console.input` for a seat pane are rejected by the node with `E_SEAT_READ_ONLY`; the console drawer shows no Take control button for seat panes (DECISION-34). Swap-mode seats (DECISION-54) have no pane of their own; the bot's one pane follows the main-session rules
- **Security:** typing into a sprite pane's console under take control = typing into the sprite, equivalent to a `pane-unverified` prompt (not a signature), so the sprite labels it the same way · **in Terminal mode** it is typing into the agent's own TUI, which can answer that TUI's permission prompts: allowed only for the owner or a Full device, after a sheet "You are typing into the real terminal — anything you approve here runs without Wraith"
- **Never** render a TUI permission prompt as a Wraith approval card, and never offer any button tied to a TUI permission prompt that types an answer (`y`, Enter, an option number); the only input in Terminal mode is the raw console under Take control after that sheet (its generic Send keys, including Enter, are part of that raw console) (DECISION-2, DECISION-52)

### 8.1 Terminal mode 💡 DECISION-52

**Purpose:** reach the agent's own TUI for anything Wraith does not render (model and login screens, `/mcp`, `/config`, commands that appear in new agent versions) without a second session. **Rule: one driver per session at a time.** Two processes on one Claude session interleave the transcript and break its `parentUuid` chain ✅; Kiro refuses a second process ("Session is active in another process") ✅. So Wraith never runs the TUI next to the driver; it hands the session over and back.

| Who / where | Rule |
|---|---|
| Bots | T1 local bots whose agent has a by-id TUI resume and passed its spike (Claude: Q39–Q41; Kiro: Q42), **main session only** (never a group seat, cloud bot, T0 or `exec`), announced as `bot.registered.terminal` |
| Who can switch | owner or Full device (`bot.terminal`); the person at the machine can also exit from the TUI itself · watch / viewer / Approve only: banner only |
| Clients | Only from Console (DECISION-52, C61): the **head bar of the bot's main-session pane** in Console has a small **>_ Open in terminal** button, which becomes **Back to Wraith** during a stay (shown only when allowed); seat panes never show it; no standing switch outside Console — only the TUI-only command strip and chip below also enter it, through the same confirmation sheet · Phone: the same button in the pane head bar (the row under the header), with a note "The terminal needs arrow keys — easier on a computer" and extra Send keys ↑ ↓ ← → |

**State machine** (node-arbitrated; `bot_sessions.state` gets `terminal`, `BotState` shows `terminal`):
```
idle ─bot.terminal{enter}→ entering ─→ terminal.idle ⇄ terminal.busy ─exit→ exiting ─→ idle
  ▲      │ E_TERMINAL_BUSY / ACTIVE          │ E_TERMINAL_LAUNCH_FAILED (15 s) → return path → terminal.resume → idle
  │      │ E_TERMINAL_CLOSE_TIMEOUT → idle   │
  │                                         exiting ─ settings changed / session diverged → needs_resolve (bot.terminalResolve) → idle
  └──────────────────────────────────────── exiting ─ E_TERMINAL_RESUME_FAILED → failed (Resurrect)
```
1. **Enter** (`entering`): Autopilot is turned off (not restored later) · the node takes the workspace write lock with holder `terminal:<sessionId>`, `tool_call_id = "terminal:<enteredAt>"` and `expires_at = NULL` for the whole stay (no heartbeat expiry; released when the return path ends — at the driver's resume or on entering `needs_resolve` — or when the bot closes (the return path incl. `E_TERMINAL_LAUNCH_FAILED` and `E_TERMINAL_CLOSE_TIMEOUT`, `bot.kill`, `E_TERMINAL_RESUME_FAILED`); `holder_epoch` = the post-bump epoch; the heartbeat, socket-close and epoch-bump release rules of §4.8 do not apply to `terminal:*` holders; a seat turn of the same bot already running finishes, and its writes wait in `waiting_write` with "waiting for @bot's terminal mode to end"; new seat prompts queue, step 3) · `swap` mode (DECISION-54): `enter` → `E_TERMINAL_BUSY` while any session of the bot has a turn running, since the one process is closed · it snapshots `git rev-parse HEAD`, a hash of `git status --porcelain`, hashes of every settings file SI-2 checks (and which of them do not exist yet, since "don't ask again" usually **creates** `.claude/settings.local.json`), the model setting and the last session leaf · the sprite closes its driver and waits for the process to exit (10 s → SIGTERM → 5 s → SIGKILL; a process still present 5 s later → `E_TERMINAL_CLOSE_TIMEOUT` and back to Wraith mode) · Claude: the node also checks that no `~/.claude/sessions/*.json` entry holds this sessionId 🧪 · `epoch` +1.
2. **Launch** (guarded argv, from `agents.json` `terminal`, checked against SI-2 like any launch):
   - Claude: `claude --resume <sessionId> --permission-mode manual --permission-prompts host --setting-sources <none> --settings <guardPath> --append-system-prompt-file <contextPath>` 🧪 Q39 (`<none>` = no `user`, `project` or `local` source, the exact form is read in Q39 and task 13.8; `<contextPath>` is a 0600 temp file too), `cwd` = workspace realpath (Claude keys sessions by project path); `<guardPath>` is a 0600 temp file built from `packages/protocol/templates/claude-terminal-guard.json` that disables auto mode and bypass mode, pins `defaultMode` to an asking mode (the template follows `CLAUDE_ASKING_MODES`; which name Claude Code 2.1.323 reads is confirmed in Q40 and task 13.8) and adds Wraith's `UserPromptSubmit`/`Stop`/`SessionEnd` hooks (`POST /hook/terminal`, busy/idle/exit only) 🧪 Q40; the guard file, its token and the context file are never logged or rendered, and are deleted on return (SI-12) · before launch SI-2 also refuses any `hooks.PreToolUse` or `hooks.PermissionRequest` entry in any checked settings file, **including the guard** (in SDK mode the sprite hook runs first; in the TUI nothing would) · the guard template contains only `permissions.defaultMode:"default"`, the keys that disable auto and bypass mode, and `UserPromptSubmit`/`Stop`/`SessionEnd` hooks that call `/hook/terminal`; a `packages/protocol` unit test asserts it has no other `hooks.*`, no `permissions.allow` and no `allowedTools` (P0-D6, P9.16-D3) · never `--bg`, `--remote-control`, `--allow-dangerously-skip-permissions` or any bypass flag. The Claude TUI's own default start mode is `auto` (since 2.1.283) ✅, so a launch without the guard is refused. `terminal.args` inherit the agent's `env` (so `local-llm` keeps its `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`).
   - Kiro: `kiro-cli chat --v3 --agent wraith-<botId> --resume-id <sessionId>` in the workspace (sessions are keyed by directory ✅; `--v3` always, the v2 store is different) 🧪 Q42 · never `--trust-all-tools`.
   - If `run` was given, the node types it into the pane without Enter (`terminal.keys`) after the sprite's first `terminal.state{idle|busy}` report.
   - herdr-core never re-runs a lost program ✅, so nothing can relaunch `claude`/`kiro-cli chat` by itself; after a lost pane the node respawns the sprite in Wraith mode (§7.6) 🧪 Q39/Q42.
3. **While in terminal** (`terminal.idle`/`busy`): the TUI answers its own permission prompts (SI-1 exception, boundary 8) · every client shows the banner "@bot is in terminal mode on <machine> · approvals happen in the terminal" with **Open console** and, when allowed, **Back to Wraith** · user messages to the bot's own chat **and handoffs/returns addressed to its main session queue** ("delivered when the terminal closes"; the requesting bot's chat shows "waiting for @bot (in terminal mode)", like a node that is offline) · commands aimed at the main session → `E_TERMINAL_ACTIVE` (§6.9) · **the bot's group seats queue too** (C64, owner 2026-11-04: settings the TUI writes in the shared workspace could reach a seat's agent before the SI-2 re-check): a seat turn running at entry finishes (its approvals still come to Wraith, its writes wait for the lock); new seat prompts, handoffs and nudges are held at the node (`prompts_local` status `queued`, not sent to the seat sprite, so no watermark block is built until delivery) with "waiting for @bot (in terminal mode)"; group `bot.cancel` still works; `room.members add` starts the seat, which takes prompts only after the return (its seed `summary` request to the main session waits ≤ 60 s as in §4.8, then the dialog opens with an empty seed) · no push for anything inside the TUI · Home shows "Terminal · 2 h 10 m" · no reminder notification (it could not close anything); the node never closes the TUI by itself (`bot.kill` is the remote way out).
   - **Other events during the stay:** `bot.kill` → the sprite sends SIGTERM to the TUI (5 s → SIGKILL), the node runs the return path, then the bot closes as usual (card "Stopped while in terminal mode") — the remote way out when `phoneExit` is false · `node.paused` / Pause all bots → the TUI is stopped the same way and the return path runs on resume ("Terminal mode ended by a pause") · Freeze of the bot chat or a group → no effect on the TUI (Freeze stops handoffs, which already queue) · revoking the device that entered → audited only (the person at the machine may be mid-turn) · relay down / Local mode → unchanged; the desktop direct local channel (DECISION-22) can send `bot.terminal` and `bot.terminalResolve`.
4. **Exit** (the user quits the TUI, Ctrl-D/`/exit`, the pane's child dies, or **Back to Wraith** from a device when the agent reports `idle`): the sprite waits ≤ 2 s for `SessionEnd`, then (`exiting`):
   - **Interrupted turn:** a prompt submitted without a later `Stop` (Claude hooks), or a last `tool_use` without a result → `interrupted:true`, card "A turn was cut off in the terminal" (Claude does not rerun that tool on resume ✅).
   - **Import:** turns after `enteredAt` reachable from the session's final leaf (rewinds in the file are branches, so never read it top to bottom; Claude: SDK `getSessionMessages`, raw file only for labels 🧪) → `bot.terminalTurn` (≤ 50, redacted), labelled "from terminal · not verified" · shown in the chat only: never prompts, never Autopilot-eligible, never in another bot's context (SI-6).
   - **Changes:** `git diff` against the snapshot → "Changes made in terminal mode" card + `file_touches.source = 'terminal'` (non-git workspace: modified-time list, `E_NOT_GIT` note).
   - **Settings check:** settings files whose hash changed are diffed; new or newly created files, new `permissions.allow` rules, a `defaultMode` change or new `hooks.*` entries → `E_TERMINAL_SETTINGS_CHANGED` card "Terminal mode added 2 allow rules → **Remove and continue** · **Keep**" (the note "the bot can't start" appears only when `si2WouldPass` is false) · a changed default model → note "Default model changed to X" with **Revert** (`bot.terminalResolve{model:"revert"}`).
   - **Seats after the return:** they resume when the return path ends; while a `settingsChanged` entry waits in `needs_resolve` they stay queued until `bot.terminalResolve` (those settings apply to them too); a session-only `needs_resolve` does not hold them.
   - **Session check:** the TUI's last session id (Claude registry / `SessionEnd`; Kiro `--list-sessions --format json` newest for this cwd) differs from the bot's → `E_TERMINAL_SESSION_DIVERGED` card **Follow the new session** / **Keep the old one**.
   - **Resume:** SI-2 runs again; only then does the node send `terminal.resume` and the sprite resumes the session with its normal driver (Claude SDK `resume` with checkpointing on), `epoch` +1, the lock is released, queued prompts of the main session **and its seats** are delivered in order, `bot.terminalReturn` is posted with `cacheCostUsd` when the agent reports it.
5. **Restarts:** node restart → the sprite re-announces `terminal.state` and the node rebuilds the lock from `terminal_stays` · herdr-core restart or update → nothing happens (the TUI keeps running ✅) · lost pane or sprite crash (the TUI dies with the pane) → the node runs the return path first, then respawns the sprite in Wraith mode (§7.6) 🧪; **the node runs the return path from `terminal_stays`** (import best-effort) and says "Terminal mode ended by a restart".
6. **herdr / UI:** before handing the TTY over the sprite writes OSC 7501 `state=idle` with title "@bot · terminal mode" and writes nothing while the TUI runs; whatever the TUI reports itself is shown by herdr only and never read by Wraith; when the TUI exits the sprite writes its own status again, replacing the TUI's last record; the state dot is a `--warn` outline with a terminal glyph (§14.6).

**TUI-only and unknown features in the chat** (DECISION-52):
- **TUI-only `/` commands:** `agents.json` lists per agent the commands that work only in its TUI (`terminalCommands`, e.g. Claude `/mcp`, `/login`, `/config`, `/permissions`, `/hooks`, `/agents`, `/plugin`, `/memory`, `/doctor`, `/model` until a model picker exists); they appear at the end of the `/` picker with a **Terminal** badge. Sending one, or any `/name` that is in neither `bot.commands` nor the Wraith section, is **not sent to the agent**: a strip above the composer says "`/mcp` works only in @bot's terminal · **Open in terminal and run it** · Send as text anyway" (unknown names: "Wraith doesn't know `/foo` for @bot"); the first button sends `bot.terminal{enter, run:"/mcp"}` · commands that would change or end the session (`agents.json` `neverSend`, e.g. `/clear`, `/resume`, `/exit`, `/quit`, `/branch`) show the same strip **without** "Send as text anyway"; `neverSend` wins over `bot.commands` (a listed name is never sent, even if the agent advertises it).
- **Unknown agent updates:** an approval or question tool Wraith does not know still goes through the normal approval path and shows a generic card (tool name + redacted raw input, Allow/Reject); display-only updates of an unknown kind become `bot.unsupported` → chip "1 update Wraith can't show · View in console". The bot never waits on something Wraith cannot show.
- **The bot asks for a TUI command in text:** if a bot message contains `/name` from that agent's `terminalCommands`, a chip under it offers **Run /name in terminal** (same as above). Only names from the list, so bot text can never prefill anything else; the user still presses Enter in the TUI.

---

## 9. Bot State Model

```
 dormant ─resurrect→ starting → idle ⇄ working → blocked ─Allow(write)→ waiting_write → working
    ▲                    │        ▲                 │
    │                    ▼        └───── answered ──┘
    └── closed ← failed/offline ← exit / sprite lost
 restoring (after a lost pane, 120s) → idle | failed
 migrating (cloud bot: moving host) → idle | working
```
`BotState = "dormant"|"starting"|"idle"|"working"|"blocked"|"waiting_write"|"restoring"|"migrating"|"offline"|"paused"|"terminal"|"needs_resolve"|"failed"|"closed"` (`terminal`: the main session is handed to the agent's own TUI, §8.1 · `needs_resolve`: back from Terminal mode but waiting for `bot.terminalResolve` (settings or session changed); main-session prompts keep queuing; no driver or TUI runs for that session and the write lock is released; the bot's seats resume unless a `settingsChanged` entry is pending (§8.1); commands are refused as in Terminal mode (`E_TERMINAL_ACTIVE`) except `bot.kill` and `bot.terminalResolve`, and `bot.terminal{enter}` → `E_TERMINAL_BUSY`; one "Needs you" push (§14.5) · `waiting_write`: a write-kind tool call waits for the workspace write lock held by another session of the same bot · `paused`: the node announced `node.paused{paused:true}` (Run bots on this Mac off, or Pause all bots in Local mode); the relay keeps queuing prompts for it and shows "paused on <node>", DECISION-38/40/43)
- Source priority: **ACP (T1) > hooks (T2) > the program's own OSC 7501 report (T0)** · Wraith never reads hc status for T1/T2 panes
- `offline` = `now − lastSeen ≥ 45s`, computed at read time, **except** that a node whose last `node.paused` is `true` shows its bots as `paused` until its next `hello` (cloud bot → `migrating` if failover is enabled and another node can take it)
- On every state change the sprite writes an OSC 7501 root report to its stdout (`\e]7501;state=<s>:app=wraith:title=<base64 "@name">\e\\`) so hc and Herdr.app see the same as the PWA ✅: `idle`/`working`/`blocked` (`kind=permission` for approvals, `kind=question` for questions); a finished turn → `done`, kept until the next prompt starts; `starting`/`restoring` → `working`; `paused` → `idle` with title "@name · paused"; `failed` → `error`; `waiting_write` is reported as `working`, `terminal` and `needs_resolve` as `idle`

---

## 10. Data Model

### 10.1 Relay (SQLite: Space DO or `better-sqlite3` · same schema)
```sql
schema_version(version INTEGER PK, applied_at)
events(seq INTEGER PK, id TEXT UNIQUE, ts, recv_ts, room, bot, node, epoch, type, meta JSON, body BLOB, sig JSON)
roster(member_id PK, kind, sign_pub JSON, caps JSON, host_priority, label, signed_entry JSON, revoked BOOL, added_at)
roster_meta(version INTEGER)
space(id PK, owner_pub JSON, boot_ts, created_at, build_hash)
rooms(id PK, kind TEXT CHECK(kind IN ('bot','group')), name, bot_id, frozen BOOL, created_at)
bot_sessions(bot_id, room, role, pane_ref, state, queue, mode, PRIMARY KEY(bot_id, room))   -- relay copy, fed by bot.state / bot.updated{seats}   -- bot chat: kind='bot', id='dm-<botId>'
room_members(room, bot_id, added_at, PRIMARY KEY(room, bot_id))
bots(id PK, workspace_key TEXT UNIQUE, parent_id, name TEXT UNIQUE, tier, node, pane_id, agent, location, caps JSON, commands JSON, autopilot JSON, session_key TEXT, epoch INT, state, queue INT, created_at, updated_at)
prompts(id PK, bot, room, root, reply_to, forward_depth, kind, source, status, handoff_mode TEXT CHECK(handoff_mode IN ('assign','transfer')) DEFAULT 'assign', created_at)   -- status: queued|sent|started|done|dropped · handoff_mode from meta.prompt.handoffMode of the room.handoff; room.return{P2} → P1 (assign) or the §7.3 climb (transfer), DECISION-53
approvals(id PK, bot, session_id, epoch, room, status, option_id, option_kind, answered_by, request_hash, summary, created_at, expires_at, resolved_at)
leases(session_key PK, holder_node, bot, pane_ref, state, lease_epoch, granted_at, revoke_sent_at)
members(member_id PK, connect_ts, last_seen, paused BOOL DEFAULT 0)   -- paused: latest node.paused
timers(name PK, fire_at)
pairing_tickets(id PK, created_by, expires_at, bound_at, pair_session, offer JSON, completion JSON)   -- offer = 1–2 members; consumed when an offer binds (bound_at), not on page load
recovery(hash TEXT)
outbox_relay(id PK, to_member, envelope BLOB, created_at)
crypt(session_key PK, bot_id, workspace_key, role, room, agent_kind, session_id, node, cwd, location, title, last_active)   -- many per bot
push_subscriptions(id PK, device, endpoint, keys JSON, created_at, failures INT)
api_nonces(nonce PK, member, ts)
quick_messages(idx PK, text)                              -- cache of roster.quickMessages for the watch API; not authoritative
audit(id PK, ts, actor, action, target, detail JSON)
```
- Events are kept for 30 days · deltas, console frames and pings are **not stored** · `events.body` has already passed through the node's redactor

### 10.2 Node (`~/.wraith/node.db`, SQLite)
```sql
workspaces(workspace_key PK, realpath, bot_id UNIQUE, kind, agent_kind, session_id, instructions TEXT, sprite_variant INT, created_at, last_open)   -- §4.8
schema_version(version INTEGER PK, applied_at)
outbox(id PK, space_id, envelope BLOB, created_at)      -- space_id: only rows of the active Space are sent (§3.4 item 7)
history(space_id, seq, room, envelope BLOB, verified BOOL, PRIMARY KEY(space_id, seq))      -- copy of room history; verified = signature passed
seen_cmds(id PK, ts)                                      -- 8 days
bots_local(bot_id PK, agent, epoch_counter INT, updated_at)   -- epoch is per bot, monotonic across ALL its sessions; any session restart bumps it
prompts_local(id PK, space_id, bot_id, room, session_id, status, origin TEXT CHECK(origin IN ('user','handoff','return','cloud','summary','nudge','pane')), signer, has_attachments BOOL, payload BLOB, created_at)  -- queued|sent_to_sprite|started|done  (waiting_write is a tool-call state, not a prompt state)
bot_sessions(space_id, bot_id, session_id, role TEXT CHECK(role IN ('main','seat')), room, pane_id, core_id, spawn_token, close_intent TEXT CHECK(close_intent IN ('close','pause','moved','failed','revoked')), respawns JSON, mode TEXT CHECK(mode IN ('process','swap')), delivered_seq INT DEFAULT 0, epoch INT, state, lease_epoch, last_active, last_summary_at, started_at, PRIMARY KEY(bot_id, session_id))   -- DECISION-31/32/54; rows are kept with state='closed' when a seat closes, so a Crypt resume of the same session keeps delivered_seq (DECISION-53)
write_locks(workspace_key PK, holder_session, holder_epoch, tool_call_id, since, expires_at)
file_touches(workspace_key, path, bot_id, session_id, room, prompt_id, approval_id, tool_call_id, source TEXT CHECK(source IN ('locations','lock_window','terminal')), at)   -- DECISION-35
approvals_local(approval_id PK, bot_id, session_id, epoch, request_hash, request_envelope BLOB, status, answered_by, created_at, expires_at)   -- answered_by: device memberId | 'autopilot' | 'timeout'
grants(bot_id, session_id, epoch, hash, expires_at, PRIMARY KEY(bot_id, session_id, epoch, hash))
autopilot(bot_id PK, on BOOL, until INT, set_by, set_at)   -- DECISION-49/50; until = unix ms; cleared on any §7.2.1 off transition
```
