```sql
rewind_points(bot_id, session_id, prompt_id, agent_ref TEXT, at, PRIMARY KEY(bot_id, session_id, prompt_id))   -- agent_ref = Claude user-message uuid or Kiro checkpoint tag (DECISION-47)
terminal_stays(bot_id, session_id, entered_at, entered_by, state TEXT CHECK(state IN ('entering','open','exiting','needs_resolve','done','failed')), head, status_hash, settings_hashes JSON, model, leaf_ref, exited_at, result JSON, PRIMARY KEY(bot_id, entered_at))   -- DECISION-52; snapshot for the return path
takecontrol(session PK, pane_ref, until, last_n)
media(media_id PK, space_id, bot_id, room, origin TEXT CHECK(origin IN ('bot','device')), uploader, attached_at, preview_ok BOOL, rel_path, mime, bytes, sha256, stripped_sha256, w, h, duration_ms, thumb BLOB, preview_path, created_at, deleted_at)   -- P9.19; rel_path is inside the bot's workspace, never sent
audit(id PK, ts, actor, action, target, detail JSON)
```
- `~/.wraith/spaces/<spaceId>/trust.json`, `~/.wraith/node.key`, `~/.wraith/agents.json`, `~/.wraith/node.json`: see §11.1 · Space-scoped rows (`space_id`): `history`, `outbox`, `prompts_local`, `media`, and **seat** rows of `bot_sessions` (`role='main'` rows have `space_id NULL`: main sessions belong to the machine) · the columns exist from migration 0001 (Local mode uses the Local Space id) · deliberately **global**: `seen_cmds`, `approvals_local`, `grants` (cleared on switch), `autopilot` (turned off on switch), `terminal_stays`, `file_touches`, `audit`, `rewind_points`, `workspaces` — their ids are ULIDs and a row from another Space fails verification anyway

### 10.3 SQLite migrations: ownership and rules 💡

| DB | Migrations owner | Location | Applied by |
|---|---|---|---|
| Relay | `packages/relay-core` | `packages/relay-core/migrations/NNNN_<name>.sql` (exported as an array in `migrations/index.ts`) | Every adapter via `Storage.migrate()` during `boot()` · DO: in the constructor under `blockConcurrencyWhile` · Node: when the db is opened |
| Node | `apps/node` | `apps/node/migrations/NNNN_<name>.sql` | `wraith-node` at startup |

Rules (checked by the test `migrations.test.ts`): **forward-only**, no down migrations · sequence numbers are unique; merged files are never edited · every statement must run in both DO SQLite and better-sqlite3 (do not use `ALTER TABLE … DROP COLUMN` if DO's SQLite does not support it 🧪; use create-copy-rename instead) · test "migrate from every previous version, then the schema equals a fresh one" · sample data per version in `migrations/fixtures/` · `schema_version` is read before anything else, and if the version in the DB is **higher** than the code knows → refuse to start (`E_INTERNAL` "downgrade not supported")

## 11. Config & CLI 💡

### 11.1 Node-side config files (`~/.wraith/`)

| File | mode | Contents |
|---|---|---|
| `node.json` | 0644 | `{ "v":1, "nodeId":"macbook-pro", "label":"macbook-pro", "relay": { "mode":"local"\|"remote", "url":"wss://…/ws", "spaceId":"…" }, "local": { "port":7777, "bind":"127.0.0.1", "allowedOrigins":["http://localhost:7777"] }, "caps": { "cloudHost": false, "hostPriority": 1 }, "herdr": { "session":"default", "socket":null, "bin":"hc" }, "mcpPort":7781, "hookPort":7782, "console": { "pollMs":250 }, "approvalTimeoutMin":30, "autopilotAsk":[], "autopilotOff":[], "redaction": { "extraPatterns":[] }, "workspaceRoots":["~/code"], "maxSeatsPerBot":4, "media": { "imageMaxMB":20, "videoPreviewMaxMB":50, "uploadMaxMB": { "image":20, "video":100 }, "previewHeight":720, "cacheGB":2, "uploadKeepDays":7 }, "voice": { "engine":"whisper-local"\|"cloud"\|"off", "model":"~/.wraith/models/ggml-large-v3-q5_0.bin", "fallbackModel":"~/.wraith/models/ggml-thonburian-distill-th-large-v3.bin", "language":"th", "cloud": null } }` · `voice` (DECISION-55, P9.18): default `off` until `wraith-node voice install`; `doctor` picks `model` on machines with a GPU (Metal/CUDA) and ≥ 12 GB RAM, else `fallbackModel`; **no prompt/vocabulary hint is passed to Whisper** (it made Whisper return nothing in the owner test); **one transcription at a time per node**, queue ≤ 3 (else `E_VOICE_UNAVAILABLE` with `retryAfter`), `ffmpeg` → 16 kHz mono s16 WAV, `whisper-cli` and `ffmpeg` are killed after 25 s → `E_VOICE_FAILED`; model URLs and SHA-256 live in `packages/protocol/src/voice.ts` and every download is verified; `doctor` prints the per-OS install hint for `whisper-cli`/`ffmpeg` (brew / apt / winget, `.exe` names on Windows) like it does for herdr; `cloud` = `{provider, keyRef}` set only by the owner in Settings after the notice "Audio leaves this machine and the provider may charge per minute" |
| `node.key` | 0600 | the node's private key (JWK, ECDSA P-256) |
| `spaces/<spaceId>/trust.json` | 0600 (folder 0700) | one per Space the machine has joined; `node.json relay.spaceId` selects the active one (§3.4 item 7) · `{ "ownerPub": JWK, "rosterVersion": 12, "roster": [RosterEntry…], "revoked": [memberId…], "pinnedAt": ts }` · written only by `verifier` after the HMAC/owner signature check passes |
| `agents.json` | 0644 | Managed by Wraith (not hand-edited): `{ "v":1, "agents": { "kiro": { "tier":"t1", "mode":"acp", "bin":"kiro-cli", "args":["acp","--agent-engine","v3"], "detected":true, "spawnableByBots":false, "seats":"process", "terminal": { "args":["chat","--v3","--agent","wraith-{botId}","--resume-id","{sessionId}"], "exit":["/quit","\r"], "phoneExit":false }, "terminalCommands":["/model","/agent","/tools","/mcp"], "neverSend":["/clear","/quit"] }, "claude": {… "terminal": { "args":["--resume","{sessionId}","--permission-mode","manual","--permission-prompts","host","--setting-sources","","--settings","{guardPath}","--append-system-prompt-file","{contextPath}"], "exit":["/exit","\r"], "phoneExit":true }, "terminalCommands":["/mcp","/login","/config","/permissions","/hooks","/agents","/plugin","/memory","/doctor","/model"], "neverSend":["/clear","/resume","/exit","/branch"] }, "local-llm": { "env": {"ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL":"http://127.0.0.1:4000"} }, "shell": { "tier":"t0" } } }` · `doctor` fills `detected` and `seats` (`process` if Q33a passed for that agent, else `swap` if Q33c passed, else `none`, DECISION-54; Claude and Kiro default to `process` until Q33a has run) · the only user-editable fields are `env` for `local-llm` and `spawnableByBots` (Settings) · launch args (including `terminal.args`) are validated against SI-2 on every start · `terminal` is set by `doctor` only when that agent's Terminal-mode spike passed (DECISION-52); `terminal.exit`, `terminalCommands` and `neverSend` are copied from the protocol agent catalogue (`packages/protocol/src/agents.ts`) and validated against it on every start, never edited |
| `secrets.json` | 0600 | `{ "githubPat": "…" }` (only on nodes with `cloudHost: true`) · alternative: OS keychain via a `keytar`-equivalent (P8) |
| `node.db` | 0600 | §10.2 |
| `sprite.sock` | 0600 | sprite-hub's unix socket |
| `control.sock` | 0600 | **local control socket** (C72, DECISION-56): NDJSON requests from `wraith-node pair` / `unpair` / `status` run by the same OS user (peer uid checked, `SO_PEERCRED`/`getpeereid`); no TCP, no HTTP; the running node answers `busy{reasons[]}` (Switch refused) or applies the switch (writes `spaces/<id>/trust.json` + `node.json relay`, drops the WS, reconnects with the new Space) and streams progress; with no running node the CLI writes the files itself · Wraith.app reaches it the same way through its Rust parent (the `--json` pairing output still travels only on the child's stdout pipe, SI-12) |
| `logs/` | 0700 | daily rotated logs, kept 7 days, passed through the redactor |

### 11.2 Relay env / secrets

| Variable | Local (embedded in node) | Self-hosted (`wraith-relay`) | Cloudflare (`wrangler secret` / vars) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `SETUP_TOKEN` | generated automatically, stored in `~/.wraith/relay-local.json` | generated by `wraith-relay init`, stored in `<dataDir>/relay.json` (0600) | `wrangler secret put SETUP_TOKEN` |
| `VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY` / `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY` / `VAPID_SUBJECT` | not used (no push on localhost except desktop notifications) | generated by `init` | secrets · `VAPID_SUBJECT` = `mailto:<email>` |
| `WRAITH_PUBLIC_URL` | `http://localhost:7777` | e.g. `https://relay.example.com` | `https://<name>.workers.dev` |
| `WRAITH_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` | `http://localhost:7777,tauri://localhost` | default = `WRAITH_PUBLIC_URL` | same (+ `tauri://localhost` if Q24a uses that origin) |
| `WRAITH_DATA_DIR` | `~/.wraith/relay-local/` | `/var/lib/wraith-relay` (Docker: volume `/data`) | – (DO storage) |
| `WRAITH_PORT` / `WRAITH_BIND` | 7777 / 127.0.0.1 | 8787 / 0.0.0.0 | – |
| `WRAITH_EVENT_RETENTION_DAYS` | 30 | 30 | 30 (var) |
| `WRAITH_LOG_LEVEL` | info | info | info |
| wrangler bindings | – | – | `[[durable_objects.bindings]] name="SPACE" class_name="SpaceDO"` · `[[migrations]] tag="v1" new_sqlite_classes=["SpaceDO"]` · `[assets] directory="../web/dist"` · `compatibility_date` ≥ 2026-04-07 |
| PWA build | `VITE_MODE=live` | `live` | `live` · the demo site uses `VITE_MODE=demo` |

### 11.3 CLI

**`wraith-node`**

| Command | Purpose | Phase |
|---|---|---|
| `wraith-node init [--name <n>]` | create `~/.wraith` (node key, `node.json`). `nodeId`/`label` default to the slugified short hostname (`-2` suffix on collision) (H3) | P1 |
| `wraith-node open [path]` | (default `.`) realpath the folder; if it is outside every root, add it as a root (local config only); if it has a bot, print/open that bot's chat link; otherwise print/open `http://localhost:7777/new?node=<id>&path=<realpath>`. **It never creates a bot itself**: creation is always a device-signed `bot.spawn` from the browser/desktop (C19) | P1 |
| `wraith-node roots list\|add <path>\|rm <path>` | manage `workspaceRoots` | P1 |
| `wraith-node start [--foreground] [--bin-dir <dir>]` | start the node (and the embedded relay if mode=local); print the setup link on first run · `--bin-dir` = the stable folder that holds `wraith-sprite` (§4.6.1) | P1 |
| `wraith-node setup-link` | print `http://localhost:7777/setup#<token>` again (Local) | P1 |
| `wraith-node status [--json]` | relay link, herdr, bots, leases, roster version, build hash | P1 |
| `wraith-node doctor` | check herdr-core socket, version ≥ 0.1.18 and protocol generation 1, kiro-cli version + engine, `wraith-sprite` resolvable (the absolute path the node will use: `<bin-dir>` rule and owner/mode check, §4.6.1), whether a pane's PATH and agent variables match the login shell's, ports, clock helper, PAT (if cloudHost) | P1 |
| `wraith-node pair <relay-url> <ticketId> [--code-only] [--json [--device-pub <jwk>]]` | pair with an external relay (show QR + `WRP1-` code + fingerprint), then switch to `relay.mode=remote` (reuses `node.key`; writes `spaces/<spaceId>/trust.json`; refused while a bot works or an approval is open) · `--json` is for Wraith.app only: the payload (including the secret) goes to stdout for the parent process, and `--device-pub` adds the app window as the second member (§3.4 Join) | P4 / P5 (`--json`) |
| `wraith-node pair --dev --print-code` | **dev pairing** for tests: the same §3.4 flow, but the QR payload is printed as text to paste into `/pair/scan` (the normal flow now also prints the code next to the QR, DECISION-36; `--code-only` hides the QR). The secret still never goes through the relay (SI-11 unchanged) | P2 |
| `wraith-node unpair` | switch back to the Local Space (`node.json relay.mode=local`); the external Space's `spaces/<id>/` folder is kept (§3.4 item 7) · refused while a bot works or an approval is open | P4 |
| `wraith-node trust show` / `trust --rotate` | view the owner pin / start a rotation (new QR) | P4 / P8 |
| `wraith-node agents list\|validate` | show detected agents; `validate` checks SI-2 on every launch config | P1 |
| `wraith-node bot list` | read-only list of bots on this node. There is **no CLI spawn/kill**: the node has no device key, so spawning is always a signed device command (PWA/desktop/watch-disallowed) or an approved `wraith_spawn_request` (§6.4) | P1 |
| `wraith-node relay migrate --to <url> --ticket <id>` | move roster + history to a new relay | P8 |
| `wraith-node voice install\|status\|test <file>` | download whisper.cpp models into `~/.wraith/models/` (asks first, shows sizes ≈ 1 GB / 0.8 GB), check `whisper-cli` and `ffmpeg`, then set `voice.engine=whisper-local`; `test` prints the transcript and timing (DECISION-55) | P9.18 |
| `wraith-node service install\|uninstall\|status\|restart` | launchd/systemd unit for wraith-node (it starts herdr-core if needed) 🧪 Q14 · the one service mechanism for every install channel, Homebrew included (no `brew services`, §4.6.1) · the unit runs the node with a stable path and `--bin-dir` (Homebrew: the `opt` link, §4.6.1) | P1 (script) |
| `wraith-node fixture record --room <id> --out <file>` | record events + console frames through redaction (§15) | P6 |

**`wraith-relay`** (Self-hosted)

| Command | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `wraith-relay init [--data-dir]` | create SETUP_TOKEN, VAPID keys, `relay.json`; print the setup link |
| `wraith-relay start` | run Fastify + ws + relay-core |
| `wraith-relay setup-link` | print the link again (works until a Space exists) |
| `wraith-relay status [--json]` | members online, leases, queue size, db size |
| `wraith-relay export --out <file>` / `import` | back up/move SQLite (used by `relay migrate`) |
| `wraith-relay rotate-setup-token` | issue a new token (only before a Space exists) |

**`wraith-sprite`**

| flag / command in pane | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `--bot <ulid>` · `--seat <roomId>` | `--bot` required; `--seat` only for a group seat (main session has none) · the sprite requests its launch spec (agent + instructions) from sprite-hub at hello; it never reads config files itself |
| `--resume <sessionId>` | `session/load` instead of `session/new` |
| `--lease-epoch <n>` | cloud bot: put in `meta.leaseEpoch` on every event |
| `--mode acp\|exec\|sdk` | default acp; `sdk` = Claude Agent SDK driver (DECISION-47) |
| `-- <argv…>` | dev only: override argv (never in a respawn argv; the node rejects it if it contains `--trust-all-tools`) |
| `/cancel` `/queue` `/status` `/detach` `/exit` | command mode in the pane (`/detach` is an alias of `/exit`: both close the bot and send its session to the Crypt, §7.6) |

---

## 12. Security Model

**Trust boundaries:**
1. **On the machine:** any process under the same user account **can access the herdr-core socket** (no API password or per-client check ✅), read panes, send keystrokes, start programs, and reach every machine added with `hc machine add` ✅ · Wraith cannot protect at the OS-user level. What it can do is reduce the paths by which a bot could use herdr-core without going through approval (a bot's own `hc …` command is a shell command that needs approval under SI-1, and `hc`/`herdr`/`herdr-app` are in the Autopilot hard floor, §7.2.1)
2. **Relay:** **partially trusted.** It can delay, drop and reorder messages, and sees all content (no E2E). It can forge **what is shown on screen**, but it **cannot inject prompts, handoffs, approvals, tamper with requests, take control, spawn, resurrect, or edit the roster on the user's behalf** · in Local mode this boundary merges with item 1
3. **PWA code:** loaded from the relay origin. If the Cloudflare account is compromised, an attacker can modify the JS to sign any command with the device key · mitigations: self-deploy in an account with 2FA enabled, show the build hash in the UI (`X-Wraith-Build` compared against the GitHub Release), (P9) host the PWA on a separate origin
4. **Zepp app on iPhone:** the watch key lives in Side Service storage; it is only as secure as that phone → cap `watch` limits its rights
5. **One agent process, several chats (swap mode only):** when an agent runs group seats in `swap` mode (Q33a failed, Q33c passed), one process serves several chats, each in its own session; model context is never shared (the sprite switches sessions before every prompt), but an `allow_always` grant or other process-level state may cross sessions, so DECISION-33 applies to every write-kind tool while seats exist (DECISION-54). No mode lets one session serve two chats
6. **Bot-to-bot memory:** a bot remembers work it did for other bots (handoffs run in its main session, DECISION-32), and text from a shared repo, the web or a cloud bot can carry hidden instructions that persist in that memory. The control is per-command approval: such text can try to persuade the user, never act on its own. Handoff cards in the target's own chat keep this visible.
7. **Autopilot (DECISION-49/50):** inside an eligible turn (bot chat, user-started) the node approves on the user's behalf; injected text the agent reads in that turn can make it act. Bounded by the write area, the fail-closed classifier, the time limit, visibility and the Stop/Freeze off-switches — not by a sandbox. Reads are unapproved (SI-2), so anything the agent can read could leave through a command the matcher misses.
8. **Terminal mode (DECISION-52):** while a bot is handed to its agent's own TUI, approvals happen in that TUI, outside Wraith's signatures, push and audit; anyone typing into it (at the machine, or a Full device under Take control) acts with the agent's own rules. Bounded by: entry only from a signed command by the owner or a Full device, main session only, guarded launch (no auto/bypass mode), write lock held, visible banner, and a return card with the git diff, settings diff and SI-2 re-check
9. **Voice input (DECISION-55):** audio goes from the device through the relay to the user's own node (never stored) unless the owner turns on a cloud engine on that node; **Browser speech** sends audio to the browser vendor (Apple, Google or Microsoft), and the Settings label says so. A transcript is only a draft (the user presses Send); voice never answers an approval. The watch may send a confirmed transcript ≤ 280 chars (SI-9), Autopilot-eligible like a typed message (owner decision C67; a lost device is revoked).
10. **Relay switching (DECISION-56):** a node's relay and Space (`node.json relay`, `spaces/<spaceId>/trust.json`) change only on the machine itself (Wraith.app driving its own node over the parent-child pipe, or the `wraith-node` CLI over the same-user `control.sock`); no message, endpoint, `/local/v1/ws` call or network path can re-point a node, and `control.sock` is never exposed beyond the OS user.
11. **Images and video (DECISION-57, P9.19):** the redactor (SI-8) cannot read pictures, so a screenshot can show a secret. Controls: media leave a machine only when a bot calls `wraith_share_file` (asks like `wraith_post`, §7.2.1) or a device uploads (bot chats only); the file must be inside the bot's own workspace and pass the deny-list and type allowlist; metadata is stripped; the relay forwards chunks without storing them but sees them (no E2E, boundary 2); devices check every file against a node-signed SHA-256; clients show media only as `<img>`/`<video>` from blob URLs (never HTML or SVG); originals of video never leave the machine. Generating media can cost money (a GPU pod): that is an ordinary shell command and goes through approval (SI-1).

### 12.1 Security invariants the agent loop must never relax under any circumstances (SI)

| ID | Invariant | Checked by |
|---|---|---|
| **SI-1** | There is no path where an approval ends with an `allow_*`-type option without **either** an `approval.respond` carrying a valid device/watch signature **or** an **Autopilot** decision by the node (§7.2.1) that satisfies every rule there: Autopilot turned on for that bot by a device-signed `bot.configure` and not expired or turned off, the request made in the bot's main session in its own chat during a turn whose prompt has `origin = 'user'` (never a group seat, handoff, return, cloud line, summary, nudge or `pane-unverified` prompt, never with no started prompt), the call accepted by the fail-closed classifier (never a spawn request, never a hard-floor or enabled default-rule match, every path inside the bot's write area), and the option `allow_once` only · **Terminal mode exception** (DECISION-52): while a bot's main session is handed to the agent's own TUI after a device-signed `bot.terminal{enter}` from the owner or a Full device, that TUI's permission prompts are answered in the TUI by the person at it; Wraith never answers them, never records them as approvals, holds the workspace write lock for the whole stay, launches the TUI only with the §8.1 guard (manual permission mode, auto and bypass disabled), and on return shows the git diff and re-runs SI-2 before its own driver resumes · timeout = always reject · no other "auto-approve", "trust mode" or "dev bypass", even with env `NODE_ENV=test` (DECISION-49) | PBT §21.1 #5 and #23, grep hook `no-auto-approve`: outside `packages/protocol`, product code must not contain `by: "autopilot"` (additionally allowed in `apps/node/src/autopilot/`, and only that token), an `approval.answer`/`approval.respond` with an `allow_*` option built without a verified device member, or `bypassPermissions\|acceptEdits\|dontAsk\|permissionMode:\s*["'](?!(?:default|manual))\|trust-all-tools\|trust-tools=\*\|WRAITH_AUTO_APPROVE\|NODE_ENV\s*===?\s*["']test["'].*allow`; P0-D6 sample files: one per pattern class · **Allowed locations:** the forbidden tokens and the Claude Terminal guard template live only in `packages/protocol/src/unsafe.ts` and `packages/protocol/templates/claude-terminal-guard.json`; validators import them and never spell the literals; there is no other exempt folder · "product code" = everything except `**/test/**`, `*.test.ts`, `packages/fakes`, `spike/`, `fixtures/` (the hook skips those) · PBT #24 (Terminal mode) |
| **SI-2** | sprite/node reject argv containing `--trust-all-tools`, `--trust-tools=*`, or the equivalent flag of other agents (Grok Build: `permission_mode = "always-approve"` or `"auto"`, `yolo = true`, an `approval_mode` that skips asking, `--allow` rules beyond read-only tools; the node checks argv **and** `~/.grok/config.toml` / `.grok/config.toml` before a **T1** launch and refuses with `E_AGENT_UNSAFE`, DECISION-48; Claude (`--mode sdk` and the Terminal-mode TUI, DECISION-60): a Claude bot never loads the **user** setting source, and for now no `project` or `local` source either (SDK `settingSources` empty; TUI `--setting-sources` listing none 🧪 Q39), so the owner's own `~/.claude/settings.json` is neither read nor changed and Claude login (OAuth/keychain, not settings) is unaffected; SI-2 checks the **effective launch** instead: the setting sources (none); the permission mode (allow-list: `manual`, or `default` where the CLI or the SDK types still use that name; any other or unknown value, including `auto`, is refused); `--permission-prompts` (must be `host`, so Wraith answers prompts; `none` or any unknown value is refused); `allowedTools` beyond read-only tools; the managed settings files (`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`, `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`), which Claude Code always loads whatever the sources are, so an allow rule beyond read-only tools or a non-asking `defaultMode` there refuses the launch; `.claude/settings.json` and `.claude/settings.local.json` from the bot's cwd up to the workspace root only if a later decision loads those sources; and any `--settings`/env-supplied file other than the guard → `E_AGENT_UNSAFE`, checked before every launch and every epoch bump (including the Terminal-mode TUI launch, whose own `--settings` guard is the only `--settings` file allowed and for which any `hooks.PreToolUse` or `hooks.PermissionRequest` entry in any checked settings file, including the guard, is refused, and again on return: entries the TUI wrote → `E_TERMINAL_SETTINGS_CHANGED`); the sprite records `claude --version` (argv array, no shell) before a launch; below or outside the checked range (`CLAUDE_CODE_CHECKED_MIN`, 2.1.323) the launch still passes only if the allow-list above holds and the node shows a "Claude Code version not checked" notice; Kiro: if the per-bot agent is selected after `initialize` (Q43), the sprite confirms from the ACP response that `wraith-<botId>` is active and otherwise refuses the launch with `E_AGENT_UNSAFE`; a T0 plain pane cannot be checked, which is why T0 bots get no approvals and are tagged `untrusted`) · in every bot launch config (`~/.wraith/agents.json`, the generated `~/.kiro/agents/wraith-<botId>.json`) the tools that may run **without asking** (`allowedTools`/trusted) are read-only (read, grep, list); other tools (write, execute, …) may be available but every call goes through `request_permission` and a device-signed approval (SI-1) · user or bot-written instructions can never change that set · does not apply to the repo's `.kiro/agents` build agents (§17) | `agents validate`, Kiro hook `no-trust-all`, unit test, PBT on the mode allow-list (`unsafe-claude-modes.test.ts`) |
| **SI-3** | Every signed command is verified against the owner-signed roster and the owner key **pinned on the node** · the relay is not the source of truth for the roster | PBT verifier |
| **SI-4** | `relay.*` is never used for security decisions on node/PWA/watch | code review + PBT "modifying relay.* has no effect" |
| **SI-5** | Replay protection: `meta.id` stored persistently for 8 days + `ts` ≤ 7 days + API nonce · revoke is permanent · `rosterVersion` never goes backwards | PBT verifier |
| **SI-6** | Context that enters the agent (context-builder, `wraith_room_context`, handoff) contains only allowlisted event types (§4.3 context-builder) with valid signatures · prompts from a pane are always labelled `pane-unverified` · turns imported from Terminal mode (`bot.terminalTurn`) never enter it | PBT context-builder |
| **SI-7** | MCP has no approval tool · hook-endpoint/mcp-endpoint bind `127.0.0.1` + token in header · sprite.sock/control.sock 0600 (control.sock also checks the peer uid) · the herdr-core socket lives in a 0700 directory created by hc ✅ (doctor warns if that directory is reachable by other users) | integration test |
| **SI-8** | The redactor runs on every body before it leaves the node and on every fixture before publish · same pattern set | hook `fixture-redact-check`, unit test |
| **SI-9** | cap `watch` may only do `allow_once`/`reject_once`, quick message (≤ 140) or confirmed voice transcript (`via:"voice"`, ≤ 280), `voice.transcribe`, freeze=true; enforced on both relay and node | PBT cap matrix |
| **SI-10** | Text one bot sends to another (handoff task, return, cloud `@` lines, seat summaries) is **untrusted input** for the receiver, whatever its source: only `@<real bot>` targets are accepted, every handoff passes the loop-guard, and the target still needs approval for every command (SI-1) — **Autopilot never applies to a turn started by such text**, so those turns always ask a human (DECISION-49). Cloud handoffs are additionally labelled `from cloud (untrusted)` because no human approved anything in that chain | integration test |
| **SI-11** | `POST /space` requires `SETUP_TOKEN`, pairing requires a secret that does not pass through the relay (HMAC), tickets are single-use | integration test |
| **SI-12** | No secrets (PAT, VAPID private, SETUP_TOKEN, node.key) in git, logs, fixtures, envelopes or error messages | hook `secret-scan`, redactor |
| **SI-13** | lease: a session has at most one `active` holder, and the previous host does not get past self-fence after the relay grants a new lease · if the clock helper is unavailable, disable `cloud-host` | PBT lease |
| **SI-14** | The demo build (`VITE_MODE=demo`) contains no real transport code and no URL of the user's relay | build-time assertion + E2E |

### 12.2 Cap matrix (who can sign what)

| Command | `owner` | `device` | `watch` | `viewer` | `node` |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `roster.update`, `pair.ticket`, `pair.complete`, revoke | ✅ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ |
| `approval.respond` any option | ✅ | ✅ | only `allow_once`/`reject_once` | ❌ | ❌ |
| `user.message` | ✅ | ✅ | quick list ≤ 140, or a confirmed voice transcript ≤ 280 (`via:"voice"`, P9.18) | ❌ | ❌ (handoff instead) |
| `voice.transcribe` | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ | ❌ |
| `media.get` (P9.19) | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ | ✅ | ❌ |
| `media.begin` + `user.message{media}` (P9.19 upload) | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ |
| `bot.spawn/kill/cancel`, `bot.move`, `bot.configure`, `room.nudge`, `bot.rewind`, `bot.rewindPoints`, `bot.rewindPreview`, `bot.terminal`, `bot.terminalResolve`, `node.configure`, `node.roots`, `crypt.resurrect`, `cloud.move`, `console.takecontrol/input`, `room.create/rename`, `room.members`, `room.freeze{false}` | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ |
| `room.freeze{true}` | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ |
| `push.subscribe`, `push.unsubscribe` (so an Approve-only phone still gets approval notifications) | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ |
| `console.open/close`, `crypt.list`, `fs.list`, `ws.changes`, `ws.diff`, `fs.tree`, `fs.read` | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ | ✅ (console read-only) | ❌ |
| Signed by node: `bot.terminalState`, `bot.terminalReturn`, `bot.terminalTurn`, `approval.request/resolved/rejected_late`, `room.handoff/return`, `bot.message.done`, `bot.tool` (final), `bot.post`, `media.shared`, `media.ready`, `media.stored`, `bot.registered/updated/removed`, `bot.summary`, `bot.worked_for`, `node.configured`, `node.paused`, `bot.commands`, `bot.rewound`, `bot.unsupported`, and the replies to `device:<requester>` listed in §6.1 (C77) | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ✅ (only for its own bots / itself) |
| Unsigned, WS-authenticated only: `lease.*`, `media.chunk`, `media.data`, `media.ack` (both directions; the media bytes are checked against a signed hash), `crypt.snapshot`, `prompt.*`, `bot.state`, deltas | – | – | – | – | ✅ (relay accepts only from the member's own authenticated socket; never used for security decisions on clients) |

### 12.3 Threats → Controls

| Threat | Control |
|---|---|
| Outsider connects to WS or eavesdrops | WSS + challenge-signature per roster member + `Origin` check · 128-bit `spaceId` |
| Outsider claims the Space before the owner | `POST /space` requires `SETUP_TOKEN` (SI-11) |
| Relay swaps keys during pairing (MITM) | QR/code carries the public key + a 128-bit secret that does not pass through the relay · HMAC (SI-11) |
| Relay replays an old roster | `rosterVersion` always increases, revoke is permanent (SI-5) |
| Relay/Cloudflare account compromised (forged commands) | signed commands + owner key pinned on node + replay guard · approval bound to `requestHash` (SI-3, SI-4, SI-5) |
| Relay forges prompt/handoff/context | device/node signatures + SI-6 |
| Relay reads work content | **Accepted** (DECISION-6): the relay runs on the user's own machine/account · enable 2FA |
| Node compromised | can only send events/handoffs for bots on its own machine · approval only from a device · owner can revoke immediately |
| Device lost | non-owner: revoke · owner: recovery code + rotate on every node (P8) · PWA unlocks with passkey/biometric before signing (P8) |
| Watch/iPhone lost | revoke the watch member · cap `watch` limits the damage (SI-9) |
| Bot approves itself | **The real protection: every shell command a bot runs goes through human approval** (SI-1, SI-2) — except calls a user-enabled Autopilot accepts under §7.2.1 (boundary 7) · bots have no device key · additional measures: disable pane approve, remove `HC_*`/`HERDR_*`, MCP has no approval tool · T0 = untrusted · denying the `hc` command in a profile **does not count as a control** |
| Prompt injection during an Autopilot turn | eligible turns only in the bot's own chat and user-started; classifier is fail-closed; hard floor (sudo, `sh -c`, scripts, outside write area, secret files, spawn, `hc`/`herdr`) always asks; time limit; badge; Stop/Freeze turn it off |
| Exfiltration via network commands under Autopilot | `network-out` and `pipe-to-shell` rules on by default; turning them off is the owner's explicit choice in Settings |
| Autopilot left on by a lost Full device | revoking the device in `set_by` turns Autopilot off on every node; `until` ≤ 24 h |
| Unapproved edits during Terminal mode | entry needs a device signature; write lock held; return card shows `git diff`; Autopilot off; the stay is visible on every client (boundary 8) |
| Terminal mode leaves auto-approve behind (TUI "don't ask again" rules, new settings files, `hooks.*`, mode or model defaults) | guarded launch; settings hashes snapshotted on entry; return blocks the driver with `E_TERMINAL_SETTINGS_CHANGED` until the owner removes or keeps them; SI-2 re-check |
| `hc send` into a sprite pane impersonating the user | `pane-unverified` label + separate loop-guard budget · no audit of the sender: herdr-core reports no input source ✅ (Q12) |
| Cloud bot sends dangerous work | SI-10 |
| A stale node wakes up and sends duplicate cloud bot data | `leaseEpoch` fencing + `freshAt` self-fencing + Kiro's lock (SI-13) |
| GitHub PAT | fine-grained read-only, file 0600/keychain only on the `cloud-host` node, never passes through the relay (SI-12) |
| Attacks on local hook/MCP | bind `127.0.0.1`, token in header, socket 0600 (SI-7) |
| Prompt injection to keep delegating endlessly | loop-guard (meta) + Freeze (including from the watch) |
| Exhausting the Cloudflare quota (DoS) | WS must pass the challenge before reaching the DO · `POST /space` once + rate limit · API rate limit per member/IP |
| Data leak in demo | static build does not connect to a relay (SI-14), fixtures pass redaction + manual visual check before publish (human) |
| `--trust-all-tools` | SI-2 |

---

## 13. Failure Modes

| Failure | Required outcome | Card/Error |
|---|---|---|
| Autopilot turns off or expires while a call is pending | the pending request stays a normal request and waits for a device (no auto-answer after the off transition) | `bot.updated{autopilot:{on:false}}` |
| Terminal-mode TUI crashes or the pane dies | the node runs the return path from `terminal_stays` (best-effort import, `interrupted` if a turn was open), resumes the driver | `bot.terminalReturn{interrupted:true}` |
| the driver will not close when entering Terminal mode | stay in Wraith mode, lock released, nothing launched | `E_TERMINAL_CLOSE_TIMEOUT` |
| the driver cannot resume the session after Terminal mode | bot → `failed`, lock released, queued prompts kept; Resurrect from the Crypt | `E_TERMINAL_RESUME_FAILED` |
| agent crash | epoch+1, `session/load`, old approvals `expired`, post "respawned"; after 3 times/10 minutes → `failed` | `system.card{respawned}`, `E_AGENT_FAILED` |
| a seat pane is closed by the user | seat → Crypt; member chip shows "seat closed · tap to reopen" (re-adds the seat, resuming that Crypt entry) | `system.card{info}` |
| a seat crashes | same as agent crash, scoped to that session (epoch bump for the bot; only that session's approvals expire; its write lock released) | `system.card{respawned}` |
| sprite-hub disconnects | sprite buffers in a ring buffer then replays; on overflow → `gap` + snapshot | – |
| herdr-core `missed` | rebuild the view from `missed.params.terminals`, drop events with `seq` ≤ `missed.params.seq` ✅ | – |
| herdr-core not running | at node start only: `hc server start` once (also if a `server_stop` happened before the node started; while running, never, §7.6); bots are `offline`; retry the socket every 5s | `E_HERDR_UNAVAILABLE` |
| node disconnects from relay | that machine's bots go `offline`, other machines keep working · outbox keeps messages · cloud bot moves (Move host / auto P8) | rail badge |
| **relay unavailable** | node/sprite local-only · approvals wait until the relay returns or timeout (reject) · PWA status bar | `E_QUOTA_EXCEEDED` or "relay unreachable" |
| DO evict / new deploy | WS drops ✅ → reconnect → replay · lease grace 45s | – |
| queue for an offline node > 7 days | dropped + card | `E_QUEUE_DROPPED` |
| Kiro quota exhausted | "out of credits" card + freeze automatic delegation for that room | `system.card{quota}` |
| local LLM endpoint dead | Claude error (no fallback ✅) → `failed` | `E_AGENT_FAILED` |
| lease conflict | sprite exits, UI points to the pane that holds it | `E_LEASE_HELD` |
| signature fails | reject + audit + warning card | `E_BAD_SIGNATURE` |
| node disk full | outbox stops accepting → sprite buffer → alert | `system.card{info}` |
| Web Push fails 3 times in a row | delete the subscription + card in Settings to resubscribe | – |
| watch poll does not get through (iOS kills Side Service) | the user sees the notification mirrored from the iPhone → opens the Mini Program → Home fetches the inbox | – |
| App Service 600ms is not enough to send the decision | Device App opens the approval page to tap again (no loss of correctness, because the command has not been signed yet) | – |
| Tauri sidecar dies mid-run | tray shows red + Restart node button · auto-restart 3 times | – |

---
## 14. UX Spec (at a level an agent can build) 💡

> UI language = **English** (Kiroween rule + user is fine with it) · theme is in §14.6 · every screen must work at 360px wide (iPhone) and 1280px (desktop) · every signed action must have an optimistic state + rollback on `error`

### 14.1 PWA: routes and global states

| Route | Page | Must have |
|---|---|---|
| `/setup#<token>` | Create Space | create owner key → `POST /space` → show recovery code (copy + checkbox "I saved it") → phone: `/` Home (`/new` when the Space has no bots); desktop: `/new` or the last opened chat · On iOS Safari: the Add to Home Screen guard + **Copy setup link** instead of creating the key (§3.4 item 2) |
| `/pair` | Pair this device (the only page an unpaired browser can open) | ticket from the link `#<ticketId>` or a paste field; no ticket → "Ask your owner device for a pairing link" · **iOS Safari:** no key; "Add Wraith to your Home Screen first" + **Copy ticket** · otherwise create key → show QR + `WRP1-` pairing code + **Copy code** + key fingerprint + ticket countdown + "Show this to your owner device. The code never goes through Wraith." → wait → "Paired as <label> · <access level>" · errors: `E_TICKET_INVALID`/`E_TICKET_EXPIRED` ("ask for a new link"), `E_PAIR_HMAC_MISMATCH` ("pairing was tampered with, start again") · **No password login** (DECISION-37) |
| `/pair/scan` | Owner: confirm a new member (step 2 of Add machine / Add device) | camera scans the QR, or **Paste code instead** (the only option on a desktop owner) → keys and kind are **taken from the QR/code only** (`pair.offer` only finds the pairing; different keys abort) → confirm screen: **machine:** name (editable), OS, herdr version, requested caps, key fingerprint to compare with the terminal · **device:** kind/browser, label (editable), fingerprint, and the owner **chooses access: Full / Approve only / View only** (watch code: Approve only, fixed) · **Join (machine + app):** one card "<name> · machine + desktop app" with both fingerprints → **Pair** signs one `roster.update` + `pair.complete` (one HMAC) |
| `/` (phone) | Home · chats | Telegram-style list of every bot chat and group, **sorted by latest event**; chats waiting for you (approval, `ask_human`) come first. Row: sprite (bot) or `#` (group), name, state (● working / ▲ approval / ❄ frozen), last message with sender, time, unread count. Pinned banner "N approvals waiting" → approval sheet. Filter chips All / Bots / Groups (Bots also lists bots with no messages yet). Header icons: Search, ✎ New (sheet: New bot / New group), ⚙ Settings (dot when a machine is offline or a bot failed). **No bottom tab bar** (DECISION-39) |
| `/settings` | Settings (phone + desktop) | phone: Back → Home · **Machines & devices** card on top (counts, warning chip) · **Relay** (C70, §3.4 item 7): three cards **Local** · **Your Cloudflare** · **Self-hosted**, the current one outlined; on a machine in Wraith.app a tap opens the wizard (external) or the "Go back to Local" sheet; elsewhere the cards are read-only with the hint · **Current relay** row (URL, online, owner key fingerprint) · Appearance: Theme **System** (default) / Crypt / Daylight · Notifications: permission state + **Send test**, **Needs you** (approvals + `ask_human` + back from terminal), Bot finished, Bot stopped or failed, **Security alerts always on** (forged command, revoked key, relay quota; not switchable), Sound · **Approvals & watch:** approval timeout (per machine, `approvalTimeoutMin` 1–240, default 30; Edit sends signed `node.configure`, applies to new requests only), Quick messages (≤ 8, ≤ 140 chars each), **Autopilot always asks** (per machine: switch each default rule on/off, add your own globs; the hard floor is listed but locked, §7.2.1) · Security: Face/Touch ID before approving shown as **P8** (passkey unlock) until then; recovery code; this device · About: build hash + **Release notes** link, third-party licenses. **Desktop** (sidebar → Settings, two columns): Relay (Local / your Cloudflare / self-hosted cards; current relay + status; direct local channel always on; picking another relay opens the wizard of §3.4 item 7) · **This Mac** (Run bots on this Mac = node on/off: off **pauses** this Mac's bots, they show as `paused`, not offline, and the machine stays in the roster; Launch at login; global shortcut ⌥⌘W; Run doctor) · Appearance · Approvals & watch (approval timeout per machine with Edit; quick messages with Edit) · Notifications (native; same groups as phone) · Security (Touch ID = P8; this device; recovery code) · About (Release notes link; no in-app updater until P9.9) · **No switch to hide the menu bar icon** (it is the main approval signal on desktop) (DECISION-40) |
| `/new` | New bot (also the first-run screen when the Space has no bots) | 0. pick kind (Project · Project assistant · Standalone assistant) · 1. pick machine (online nodes) → 2. folder browser: first the list of roots (+ "Add folder by path"), then `fs.list` inside a root (breadcrumb, sub-folder list, folders that already have a bot show its sprite + "Open"; **+ New folder** row (project kind) creates a sub-folder of the current location when the bot starts, via `bot.spawn{createFolder:true}`) → 3. name (prefilled, unique) + agent (detected ones only) + optional instructions → if the chosen folder has bots in sub-folders, show "Will lead: …" → **Start bot** (no room choice: the bot gets its own chat, which opens next) (DECISION-28). Picking a folder that has a bot opens it instead (§4.8) · Empty Space: dimmed sprite + "Your agents live in folders. Pick one to start." + **New bot** button · **No machine online:** step 1 shows "No machine is online" with the known machines (dimmed, last seen) and the `wraith-node service install` hint; nothing can be created. **No pre-created or queued bots** for offline machines (owner's choice, 2026-11-02): the user turns the machine on first |
| `/new` on desktop | New bot (single form, not a stepper) | machine chips (This Mac first) → **folder**: on this Mac a path field + **Choose folder…** (native picker, any folder; a folder outside the roots is added as its own root after a notice) + recent folders + drop a folder from Finder onto the window; on another machine the `fs.list` browser as on the phone → kind (Project · Project assistant · Standalone) → name + agent → optional instructions → **Start bot** · a live **"Where it lands"** panel shows the workspace key and the tree position (lead, will lead, moves under it) |
| **Respond (Nudge, DECISION-51)** | Groups only | **Long-press a member chip** in the bot rail (phone) or hover it (desktop: a small **↩ Respond** button) → **"↩ Respond to the chat"**; the bot card in a group has the same button with "Will read N new messages since it last read the group (you ×2, @app ×1)" → signed `room.nudge` → the bot answers in the group like a mentioned reply · disabled with a reason for T0 bots and bots without a seat · hidden for Approve only / View only |
| **Terminal mode (DECISION-52)** | Bot chats of bots with `terminal` | **>_ Open in terminal** in the head bar of the bot's main-session pane in Console (Full devices; not in the chat header) → sheet "Open @bot in its own terminal? · Approvals will happen in the terminal, not in Wraith · Messages you send here wait until you come back · Autopilot turns off" → **Open terminal** · while active: banner under the header (amber, terminal icon) "@bot is in terminal mode on <machine> · approvals happen in the terminal" + **Open console** + **Back to Wraith** (only when allowed) · composer stays usable, sent messages show "queued · after terminal" (in groups too, for messages to that bot's seats, whose member chip shows "in terminal") · Home row badge "Terminal" · return: card "Back from terminal · N turns · M files changed" (**View changes**), imported turns labelled "from terminal · not verified", plus the settings / session cards from §8.1 when needed · `/` picker: TUI-only commands at the end with a **Terminal** badge; sending one shows the strip "`/mcp` works only in @bot's terminal · Open in terminal and run it · Send as text anyway" (Open goes through the same sheet) · watch: no Terminal mode; its bot list shows "terminal" |
| Bot rail (group and bot chat) | Same layout everywhere | **All** first (opens the bot tree), then: in a group, its members (status of their seat in this group, "swap" badge); in a bot chat, that bot's lead and team. **Tapping any bot chip opens the same bot card**: status, workspace, tags (seat mode, kind, agent), a one-line explanation of the state, **Open chat**, **Console**, and in a group **Remove from # <group>**. The full member list opens from the group title ("# main · 3 members ›") |
| `/groups/new` | New group | name → pick existing bots from the bot tree (checkboxes, grouped by machine; cloud bots shown disabled: "Kiro Cloud bots can't join groups") → per added bot, a seed-summary preview (edit/clear; "fork full history" opt-in) → **Create group** · editable later (add/remove bots, rename) |
| Bot tree (desktop sidebar; phone: "All" chip, first in the bot rail → bottom sheet) | All bots by folder | grouped by machine (+ Kiro Cloud, Scratch) with the workspace root · indented by folder depth with guide lines · collapse/expand parents · each row: sprite, folder name, role/kind · state, room chip (current room highlighted), state dot · **tap a row → open that bot's own chat** (its groups are listed in the chat header) (§4.8) |
| **Voice input (DECISION-55, P9.18)** | composer of every chat (Full devices) and the watch | 🎙 button left of Send (hidden when no engine: no Web Speech API and no node with `caps.voice.ready`; hidden for Approve only / View only) · tap to start, tap again or 30s to stop; while recording the composer shows a level bar + "Listening… 0:07" + ✕ · result goes **into the composer as editable text**, inserted at the cursor, never sent automatically · spoken names that matched members are listed in the strip above the composer ("heard 'แอป' → @app"; the textarea itself is plain text, C68) · Settings → Voice (per device, kept in localStorage): **Automatic** (default: Wraith node when any node reports `caps.voice.ready`, else Browser speech when the browser has the Web Speech API, else the mic is hidden) / **Wraith node (Whisper)** / **Browser speech** (Web Speech, `th-TH`; label "may use Apple/Google/Microsoft servers") · below it, **On your machines**: one row per node with its voice status from `MemberView.caps` ("Whisper large-v3 · ready" / "not installed · run `wraith-node voice install`") and, owner only, **Cloud service** per node (cost notice) · bot card: **Spoken names** (aliases) · · sent messages that started as voice show a small mic glyph (`via:"voice"`) · approval sheets, Autopilot and Terminal sheets have **no mic** and never accept voice · optional **Read replies aloud** per device (off by default; `speechSynthesis`, the open chat only, bot text only, never tool output) · watch: **Say** page gets **🎙 Speak**: record (≤ 30s) → text on screen → **Send** / **Retry** / **Cancel** |
| **Images and video (DECISION-57, P9.19)** | bot chats and groups (Full and View only open the viewer; Approve only (`watch` cap) shows the card only, the viewer and Save are hidden by the `cap()` gate; the watch shows nothing) | **Media card** in the bot's bubble: thumbnail (or a grey box with the file type when there is none), name, size, `1280×960` or `0:42`, caption; video shows ▶ on the poster; `preview:false` shows "Preview too large · Open on this Mac" · tap → **viewer** (full screen, pinch zoom, swipe between media of the same message, ✕/Esc; **Save** = browser download; video plays the 720p preview) with a progress ring while chunks arrive; the machine offline → "<machine> is offline. The picture opens when it is back"; gone → "No longer on <machine>" · in Wraith.app on the machine that has the file: **Open on this Mac** (through the Rust parent, never a protocol message) · **Upload:** 📎 left of 🎙 in the composer (Full devices, **bot chats only**; in groups it is greyed with "Attach in @bot's own chat") opens the photo/file picker (images and video; the PWA re-encodes images ≤ 4096 px longest side: PNG with alpha stays PNG, the rest JPEG; this also drops EXIF) → up to 4 chips above the textarea with thumbnail + progress + ✕ → Send is enabled when every chip is stored; the sent bubble shows the thumbnails · no push and no watch item for shared media (the existing Done push covers the turn, C74) · the approval sheet for `wraith_share_file` shows the thumbnail, name, type and size |
| `/rooms/:roomId` | Room | phone: Back → Home · timeline + composer + bot rail (desktop: left; mobile: horizontally scrolling top bar) + Freeze button + approvals badge |
| `/rooms/:roomId/approvals/:id` | Approval sheet (bottom sheet on mobile, modal on desktop) | §14.2 |
| Bot chat header | Path · Files · Changes · Groups · Autopilot · Freeze | Second header row of a **bot chat only**: short workspace path (long-press = full `node:path`), then small icon buttons: **Files** (P9.13, folder icon), **Changes** (P2, branch-arrow icon + changed-file count), **Groups** (people icon + count; sheet listing the groups this bot is in, with waiting approvals, and "+ New group with @bot"; replaces the old `# main` pill), **Autopilot** (⚡; purple pill with the time left while on, DECISION-49). **Phone:** **Freeze** (snowflake ❄ icon in `--accent`, never a stop square, DECISION-46) sits in the **top row**, as a 40px button (same size as Back) just before the Chat | Console switch, so the second row has room; the second-row buttons are 36px circles and their numbers (Changes count, Groups count, Autopilot time left) are **mini pill badges** on the button's top-right corner (white pill, `--grad-a` text, weight 600; Autopilot: purple circle with the time left, e.g. `1:59`, as a white pill centred on the bottom edge) (2026-11-02). Files/Changes open a bottom sheet (phone) or side panel (desktop). **Desktop:** **Files** · **Changes (n)** · **❄ Freeze** as small (32px) icon + label pills placed just before the Chat | Console switch; the panel replaces the "About this bot" column while open (clicking the active pill again closes it); groups are listed in "About this bot", so desktop has no Groups button. **Desktop side panel (both group and bot chats):** a 32px **⊐ Panel** button after the Chat | Console switch shows/hides the right panel (group: Members + Pending approvals; bot: About this bot, or Files/Changes while one is active). Opening Files/Changes shows the panel if hidden; hiding it closes Files/Changes. While hidden in a group with pending approvals the button shows an orange count. The panel is **420px wide** for every content type (Members/approvals, About, Files, Changes) and can be **resized by dragging its left edge** (320–640px; keyboard: arrow keys on the focused handle). Shown/hidden and width are remembered per device (localStorage). Console and the New bot / New group forms have no toggle. Changes: +/− counts, attribution chip `@bot · # chat`, approval/lock-window note, tap a row → diff expands inline; tool/approval cards link **View diff**. Files: expandable tree (changed files marked M/A, sub-folder bots tagged) → tap a file → read-only viewer with line numbers, uncommitted lines highlighted, and **View diff** (jumps to Changes) when the file has changes; deny-listed, too-large and binary entries are greyed and open an explanation instead of content (`E_FILE_DENIED` / `E_FILE_TOO_LARGE`). Both read-only; footer "Ask @bot to …" opens the composer. Groups never show Files/Changes |
| `/console/:nodeId/:paneId` | Console drawer (full-screen on mobile) | read-only xterm, **>_ Open in terminal** in the head bar of a main-session pane for bots with Terminal mode (§8.1; during a stay the same spot shows **Back to Wraith** only when allowed), Take control button (10:00 countdown), Send keys buttons (Ctrl-C, Enter, Esc, Tab; plus ↑ ↓ ← → in Terminal mode) — **hidden for group seat panes** (DECISION-34), whose footer reads "Group seat · view-only · write in the group" |
| `/crypt` | The Crypt (P8) | grouped by node → project; each row: agentKind icon, title, lastActive, Resurrect / Fork buttons; disabled + tooltip when the node is offline or in grace |
| `/machines` | Machines & Devices (phone: from the top card in Settings; desktop: sidebar) | **Grouped by computer** (DECISION-38): one row per computer with role chips (`machine`, `app`, `browser`, `phone`, `watch`, access Full / Approve only / View only, `owner`), online / `paused` / offline, last seen; machine rows add herdr version, caps, hostPriority and **Move cloud host here**; each role can be paused (machine) or revoked on its own; a device-only Wraith.app row hints "Run bots on this Mac from that computer" · **Add machine:** ticket screen with the copyable `wraith-node pair <relay> <ticket>` command + 10-minute countdown → step 2 = `/pair/scan`; in Local mode it opens "Needs an external relay" (§3.4) · **Add device:** pairing link `https://<relay>/pair#<ticket>` + its QR + Copy link → step 2 = `/pair/scan` |
| `/demo` | Demo mode (only with `VITE_MODE=demo`) | §15 |

**Access levels in the UI (cap gate, DECISION-37):** `cap(action)` in `packages/ui-kit` sits next to `can(feature)` (§4.5.1) and reads this device's caps from the verified roster; every control below is hidden (not just disabled) when `cap()` says no, and the relay/node still enforce §12.2.

| Access | Can see | Can do | UI differences |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Full** (`device`) | everything | everything in §12.2 except roster/pairing (owner only) | – |
| **Approve only** (`watch` cap on a phone or watch) | Home, chats, approvals, bot states | `allow_once` / `reject_once`, quick messages, voice messages ≤ 280 (P9.18), Freeze on | composer replaced by a quick-message picker (plus **🎙 Speak** when voice is available, same flow as the watch); approval sheet has only Allow once / Reject; no New, Console, Files, Changes, Settings edits |
| **View only** (`viewer`) | timeline, console (read-only), Files, Changes, Crypt list | Freeze on, notifications | no composer; approval sheet shows the request with "View only — approve from a Full device" and no buttons; no New, Take control, Move, Edit, Add machine/device |

**Global connection states (top bar):** `connecting` (grey "Connecting…") · `replaying` ("Catching up… 1,204 events") · `online` (hidden) · `degraded` (yellow: "Relay quota exceeded — bots keep working locally" / "Relay unreachable — approvals will time out") · `offline` (red) · every `frozen` room has a purple bar "Room frozen — no handoffs. [Unfreeze]"

**Timeline items (one component per `meta.type`):**

| Item | Content | States |
|---|---|---|
| UserBubble | text + @mentions chips + `by` icon (phone/desktop/watch/pane-unverified ⚠ in yellow) | sending → sent(seq) → failed(error) |
| BotBubble | streaming text (markdown, code block copy), footer: stopReason, time | streaming / done / cancelled |
| ToolCard | icon by kind, title, status chip, accordion: command/diff (react-diff-view) / output (≤ 32KB, "Open console" if truncated) | pending/in_progress/completed/failed |
| ApprovalCard (inline) | summary + button to open the sheet + countdown | open / resolved(option, by) / expired / rejected_late |
| HandoffCard / ReturnCard | from → to, mode ("asked" for `assign`, "handed to" for `transfer`), task, refs (PR links), label "delegated by cloud (untrusted)" when from=cloud | queued / waiting (node offline) / started / done |
| AskHumanCard | question + choices as buttons (answer = `user.message` replyTo) | open / answered |
| T0StatusCard | state + **"raw snapshot" label** + latest `terminal.read` text in a monospace box | – |
| PRCard | title, number, branch, CI chip (if any), "@app-v100 checkout & test" button (opens the composer with the text) | – |
| SystemCard | respawned / host moved / queue dropped / quota / revoked | – |

**Composer:** auto-grow `<textarea>` (plain text only, no rich text or `contenteditable`) — **multi-line rules (C68):** (1) grows with its content up to **6 lines on phone** and **10 lines or 40 % of the window height on desktop** (whichever is smaller), then scrolls inside; returns to one line after sending · (2) one line = pill (radius = half the height); from 2 lines the radius becomes **22px** and the padding stays 12px top/bottom · (3) 🎙 and Send/Stop are bottom-aligned (`align-items: flex-end`) so they stay next to the last line · (4) desktop: **Enter = send, Shift+Enter = new line**; Enter while an IME is composing (`event.isComposing` or keyCode 229) never sends; with the `/` or `@` picker open Enter inserts the choice; Enter sends only when the box is non-empty and **never stops a bot** (Stop stays on the button and Esc Esc); the placeholder says "Enter to send · Shift+Enter for a new line" · (5) phone: Return inserts a new line; only the Send button sends · (6) a counter "14,200 / 16,000" appears at ≥ 90 % of the `user.message` limit (16 000 chars), turns `--danger` above it, and Send is disabled while over (the limit is checked again before signing) · (7) a paste of more than 20 lines shows as a chip "Pasted 120 lines · 14,200 chars · Expand" in the composer container **above** the textarea (the textarea keeps only the typed text; the full text is sent as typed text + newline + pasted text; **Expand** moves it into the textarea, **✕** removes it) · (8) voice transcripts are inserted as plain text; fixed names are shown only in the strip above the composer (DECISION-55), never as styling inside the box · (9) phone: the composer sticks to the top of the on-screen keyboard using `visualViewport` (resize/scroll), and the timeline keeps the last message visible while the box grows · (10) message bubbles keep the user's line breaks (`white-space: pre-wrap`, long words wrap with `overflow-wrap: anywhere`) and collapse after **12 lines** with a fade + **Show more** / **Show less** (code blocks: 20 lines) · a11y: the counter is `aria-live="polite"`, the mic has `aria-pressed` and "Listening…" is announced, the recording level bar is `role="meter"`, Show more/less and the paste chip's ✕ are labelled `<button>`s · the draft per chat is kept in memory until sent (not in localStorage: it may hold secrets) · `@` autocomplete from bots in the room (shows node badge + state), **Esc to stop (desktop, DECISION-45):** Esc first closes whatever is open (`/` or `@` picker, sheet, modal, Files/Changes panel); with nothing open and a bot in this chat `working`, the first Esc shows a bar above the composer "Press Esc again to stop @bot" for 2s and the second Esc sends `bot.cancel{room}` for the session serving this chat · in a group with **one** working bot it stops that bot; with **several** the second Esc opens "Stop which bot?" (one row per working bot + **Stop all**; Esc closes it, nothing is stopped without a click/Enter) · the turn ends with `stopReason: cancelled` → "Stopped by you" card; queued prompts stay queued · not in Console with Take control (Esc goes to the terminal; read-only console ignores it) · phone has no shortcut, the ■ Stop send button does the same · hidden for Approve only / View only (no `bot.cancel`) · **`/` command picker** (DECISION-44: typing `/` as the first character in a bot chat, or right after `@bot ` in a group, opens a list above the composer in two sections: **Wraith** = `/cancel`, `/queue`, `/status`, and `/rewind` for bots that support it (run as Wraith actions on that bot, never sent to the agent) and **@bot's skills** = that bot's latest `bot.commands` (name, one-line description, kind badge Skill/Prompt/Command); typing filters (prefix first, then substring); ↑↓/Enter or tap inserts `/name ` plus the greyed `hint`; Esc closes · in a group, `/` with no `@bot` first shows "Pick a bot first: type @" · empty or `source:"none"` (T0, agent sends nothing) → "@bot has no commands to list" · hidden for Approve only/View only (no composer) · phone: sheet anchored above the keyboard, rows ≥ 44px; desktop: popover ≤ 360px tall), Enter = send on desktop / send button on mobile, **Autopilot** (DECISION-49): bot card / bot chat header menu → **Autopilot** sheet (duration 1 h / 2 h / 8 h / until I turn it off; "Allowed automatically: edits and commands inside <workspace> in turns you start"; "Still asks: <always-ask list>, anything after a handoff or cloud message") → signed `bot.configure`; the approval sheet may suggest it after 3 allows in 10 min (§7.2.1); while on, a purple **Autopilot · 1:42** badge sits in the header row and the Home row, and auto-approved cards are collapsed; hidden for Approve only / View only · **Rewind** (DECISION-47, bot chats of agents that support it: Claude via the SDK driver, Kiro if Q37 passes): long-press (phone) / hover menu (desktop) on one of your own messages → **Rewind to here**, or `/rewind` in the Wraith section of the `/` picker (shown only for bots that support Rewind) → a sheet lists your prompts in this session, newest first (text, time, "N files changed after") → choosing one shows only the restores that agent allows: **Restore code and conversation** · **Restore conversation** · **Restore code** (code options only when files changed after that point; Kiro offers only the first) · Cancel → a code restore first shows the files it will restore (`bot.rewindPreview`) and the warning "Files @bot edited go back to that point, including later changes anyone made to them. Files changed only by shell commands or subagents are not restored." → **Rewind** (signed `bot.rewind`) → "Rewound to …" card; later messages stay visible, greyed and marked rewound; after a conversation restore the prompt text returns to the composer · not offered in groups, while the bot works ("Stop @bot first"), or for Approve only / View only · Summarize options are not in v1 · **Send ⇄ Stop** (DECISION-46): while a bot serving this chat is `working` and the composer is empty, the send button becomes **■ Stop** (same size and place, aria-label "Stop @bot") and sends `bot.cancel{room}`; typing turns it back into Send (the message is queued) · in a group with several working bots Stop opens "Stop which bot?" (one row per working bot + Stop all; tap outside closes, nothing stops without a tap) · same on phone and desktop (desktop also has Esc Esc) · hidden for Approve only / View only · messages with no mention: show the hint "No bot mentioned — this will be posted to the room only"

**Bot rail item:** avatar (sprite variant chosen at creation, changeable), name, machine badge, Tier chip, state dot (§14.6), queue count, menu: Open console, Cancel current, Stop bot, Move host (cloud)

### 14.2 Approval sheet (PWA/desktop)

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ⚠ tester wants to run a command         │  ← title (from request.title)
│ on V100 · ~/code/app · expires in 28:14 │
│ ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐ │
│ │ $ npm install axios                 │ │  ← detail.command (monospace, wrap) or diff viewer
│ └─────────────────────────────────────┘ │
│ Signature: node V100 ✔ verified         │  ← failed = red bar "Unverified request — Allow disabled"
│ [ Reject ]              [ Allow once ]  │  ← buttons per options[].kind; allow_always hidden under "More"
│ More ▾  (Allow always · Reject always)  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
```
- `More` shows only the kinds present in `options[]`: it is hidden when the sprite dropped `allow_always` (DECISION-33) or the target path is outside the bot's workspace
- Button order: reject on the left, approve on the right · the Allow button takes **2 taps** counted from opening the sheet (opening the sheet = tap 1, Allow = tap 2, per G2); no nested confirm
- After tapping: button disabled + "Sending…" → `approval.resolved` arrives → "Allowed by this device" (and "waiting for # <other chat> to finish writing" while the tool is `waiting_write`) / if `E_ALREADY_RESOLVED` → show the actual result "Already rejected (timeout)"
- The sheet and its push/notification name the chat it belongs to ("in # release" or "in @app's chat"); in a group, member chips show the seat state: `starting` · `own` · `shared` · `waiting_write` · `closed`
- Several devices open at once: the sheet closes itself on resolved, with a toast
- `expiresAt` reached → buttons disappear, show "Expired — agent received reject"

### 14.3 Desktop (Tauri) tray / menu

| Icon state | Meaning |
|---|---|
| White sprite | node + relay online, no approvals |
| White sprite + red number badge | number of pending approvals (across all rooms) |
| Grey sprite | node offline / sidecar starting |
| Red sprite | sidecar dead or herdr not running |

Menu (top→bottom): `Open Wraith` · `Pending approvals (3)` → submenu listing "app-v100: npm install axios" (click = open sheet) · separator · `Rooms` → submenu · `Freeze all rooms` / `Unfreeze all` · separator · `Node: macbook-pro · online` (disabled) · `Relay: Local (localhost:7777)` / `Relay: Cloudflare (…workers.dev)` (disabled) · `Restart node` · separator · `Launch at login` (checkbox) · `Settings…` · `Quit Wraith`

- Native notification: title = `<bot> needs approval`, body = summary, actions (macOS): `Allow once` / `Reject` **only when the WebView is ready to sign** (clicking an action = open the window and let the WebView sign; Rust does not sign by itself without showing the request) — if Q24a uses the keychain path, the window must still open so the user sees the request first (SI-1 on the UX side)
- Global shortcut opens the window at the latest approval · single-instance: opening again = focus
- First run: "Welcome" page → check herdr/kiro-cli (`doctor`) → show results as a checklist with install commands → **choose**: **Start on this Mac** (default: create a Local Space → `/new` or the last opened chat) or **Join my Space** (DECISION-36) → a form takes the relay URL + ticket (pasting the whole `wraith-node pair …` command fills both) → the window shows the QR, the text pairing code, the key fingerprint and the ticket countdown (all read from the sidecar's stdout, §3.4 Join protocol), and waits → on success "Paired · this Mac is <name>" → open the Space. Errors: expired/used ticket (`E_TICKET_INVALID`, "ask for a new ticket"), relay unreachable, HMAC mismatch (`E_PAIR_HMAC_MISMATCH`, "pairing was tampered with, start again"). A machine joined this way runs **no** local relay

### 14.4 Watch (Amazfit Active 2 Square, 390 × 450)

**Layout requirements:** 16px safe area on all sides (corner radius 86) · font ≥ 28px for content, 24px for captions · buttons ≥ 72px tall · physical buttons: top button = back/Home, bottom button = scroll list (system default, not overridden) · every page scrolls vertically by swipe

| Page | Content (reference sizes in px) | Actions |
|---|---|---|
| **Home** | 56px header "Wraith" + status dot (green = synced < 60s, yellow = stale, red = no phone) · Pending approvals list (120px-tall card: bot name + 2-line summary + time remaining) · if empty: dimmed sprite image + "All quiet" · bottom buttons: `Bots` `Say` `Freeze` (72px each) | tap card → Approval |
| **Approval** | `summary` is **truncated** when the raw command contains a newline or is longer than 120 characters · title (bold 30px, ≤ 2 lines) · line "app-v100 · V100 · execute" · 26px monospace box showing `summary` (≤ 120 characters) · time remaining · `Reject` (dark red, 80px) and `Allow once` (dark green, 80px) buttons stacked vertically · if `detail.diff` is present or `summary` is truncated: replace the Allow button with an `Open on phone` button, which **sends no command**, only shows "Open Wraith on your iPhone to review this request" (the Web Push for that approval is already on the iPhone), and keep the `Reject` button · signature fails → no buttons + "Unverified" | Allow/Reject → Side Service signs `approval.respond` → show "Sent ✓" → wait for `approval.resolved` from the inbox → "Allowed" / "Already resolved" |
| **Bots** | bot list (96px card: avatar colored by state, name, machine, queue) | tap → details: state, last message 3 lines, `Say` button to this bot |
| **Say** (quick message) | **🎙 Speak** button at the top (P9.18: records ≤ 30s, `POST /api/v1/voice`, shows the text, **Send** sends `user.message{via:"voice"}`, ≤ 280; hidden if Q45b fails) · list of quick messages (defaults: "Go ahead", "Stop and summarize", "Wait for me", "Run the tests", "Open a PR", "Explain briefly") · pick a bot first (if entered from Home) | send `user.message{by:"watch", mentions:[bot]}` |
| **Freeze** | confirm: "Freeze all groups? Bots stop delegating." `Freeze` button | send `room.freeze{frozen:true}` for every group (bot chats are not frozen from the watch) · unfreeze only on the phone (show this message) |
| **Settings** (on the watch) | pair status, relay host, last sync, `Sync now` button, `Unpair` button | – |
| **Watch notification** (layer 3 if Q27b passes) | `notify({title:"app-v100 needs approval", content:"npm install axios", actions:[{text:"Allow once", file:"app-service/approve", param:"<approvalId>:<requestHash>"},{text:"Reject", file:"app-service/reject", param:"…"}], vibrate:2})` | App Service (≤600ms) sends message `{approvalId, requestHash, option}` to Side Service → Side Service signs (the request envelope must already be cached) → if not in time, Device App opens the Approval page to tap again |

**Settings App (in the Zepp app on iPhone):** Relay URL field (validate https) · "Generate pairing code" button → shows the `WRP1-` pairing code (§3.4) + Copy button (the user switches to the PWA and pastes it into Add device → `/pair/scan`) · status: "Waiting for owner…" → "Paired as Amazfit" · Unpair button · Sync interval (default 25s long-poll while the app is open) · Debug log (last 50 lines, through redaction)

**Side Service behaviour:** when the Device App opens → start the long-poll loop (`wait=25`) until it closes · when the Zepp app is in the background on iOS → treat it as stopped (do not rely on it) · cache the latest 20 `approval.request` envelopes (used to compute `requestHash` and to sign) · roster cache + verify the owner signature on every refresh

### 14.5 Notification copy (Web Push / native / watch)

| Event | title | body | action/tap |
|---|---|---|---|
| approval.request (not `autopilot:true`) | `<bot> needs approval` | `<summary>` (≤ 100) + ` · on <node> · in <chat>` | open that sheet · tag = approvalId (replaces itself) |
| approval resolved elsewhere | – (close the previous notification by tag) | – | – |
| ask_human | `<bot> asks you` | question ≤ 120 | open the room at the card |
| bot back from Terminal mode in `needs_resolve` | `<bot> needs you` | `Back from terminal · settings changed — choose before it runs again` (or `session changed`) | open the bot chat at the card |
| handoff reaches the user (return of work the user requested, all bots idle) | `Done: <bot>` (transfer: `Done: <target> · handed over by <sender>`) | first line of the summary | open the room |
| bot failed | `<bot> stopped` | `<reason>` | open the room |
| cloud host moved | `Kiro·Cloud moved to <node>` | `previous host <node> offline` | open /machines |
| queue dropped | `Undelivered work for <node>` | `<n> messages older than 7 days were dropped` | open the room |
| relay quota | `Relay quota exceeded` | `Bots keep working locally; approvals will wait` | open settings |
| security | `Rejected a forged command` | `Signature check failed from <member>` | open /machines |

Rules: no emoji in notification text · no diff/output content in push (may contain secrets); only the `summary` that passed the redactor · mute repeats within 10s per bot

### 14.6 Theming (Kiroween / tasteful sprite theme)

Theme setting: **System** (default; follows `prefers-color-scheme`), Crypt, or Daylight (DECISION-40).

| Token | Crypt (dark) | Daylight (light) |
|---|---|---|
| `--bg` | `#0e0b16` | `#f7f5fb` |
| `--surface` | `#17122a` | `#ffffff` |
| `--surface-2` | `#211a3a` | `#efeaf7` |
| `--text` | `#ece8f6` | `#1b1430` |
| `--text-muted` | `#9a92b4` | `#5e5679` |
| `--accent` (wisp violet) | `#a658eb` | `#4c21ae` |
| `--accent-2` (ectoplasm) | `#56e0c0` | `#0b7a6a` |
| `--warn` | `#f5b449` | `#8f5600` |
| `--danger` | `#ff6b7a` | `#c62839` |
| `--ok` | `#58d68d` | `#177a42` |
| `--border` | `rgba(255,255,255,.08)` | `rgba(0,0,0,.08)` |
| `--grad-a` (indigo dusk) | `#3d27ad` | `#3d27ad` |
| `--grad-b` (plum night) | `#6b16a5` | `#6b16a5` |
| `--tint` (page ground under cards) | `#1b1530` | `#f4f1fc` |
| `--shadow` | `rgba(0,0,0,.45)` | `rgba(76,33,174,.16)` |

- **State dots:** working = `--accent-2` + slow 2s pulse · blocked = `--warn` blinking 1s · idle = `--text-muted` and **avatar opacity 0.55 (dimmed sprite)** · offline = dashed outline · paused = dashed outline + ⏸ badge · terminal = `--warn` outline + terminal glyph · needs_resolve = `--warn` + ! badge · failed = `--danger`
- **Motif:** sprites — small glowing wisps (round body + two dot eyes) in one set of clean SVGs with 6 variants (different colors/faces), used as avatar/empty state/tray; built from basic shapes so they scale from 16px tray icons to the watch · no blood, no jump scares, no spooky sounds · faint fog only as a static gradient in the background
- **Motion:** ≤ 200ms for UI; the status pulse is the only looping animation · `prefers-reduced-motion` → disable all pulses and fades
- **Accessibility:** contrast ≥ 4.5:1 for every text/surface pair (checked by a token test) · tap targets ≥ 44px on touch hosts (`mobile`): a smaller icon (e.g. 24px header icons) keeps a ≥ 44px hit area; desktop pointer targets (32px pills, ⊐ Panel) may be 32px · clear focus ring · state does not rely on color alone (has icon/label)
- **Style (violet gradient, 2026-11-02):** brand gradient `linear-gradient(120deg, var(--grad-a), var(--grad-b))` on headers (curved bottom edge), the desktop sidebar, primary buttons, the send button and the user's own bubble · white cards (`--surface`) on `--tint`, separated by soft `--shadow` instead of borders · pill shapes everywhere (buttons, chips, inputs, toggles: fully rounded); cards 20–26px radius · selected chip/toggle = white pill with `--grad-a` text on gradient, or gradient pill on white · Allow = gradient pill, Reject = outlined `--danger` pill · watch stays AMOLED black with gradient pills
- **Fonts:** `Poppins` (400/500/600/700) for UI, then **`IBM Plex Sans Thai`** (400/500/600/700) for Thai text (Poppins has no Thai glyphs; the browser picks Plex per character), then `-apple-system` · stack: `font-family: Poppins, "IBM Plex Sans Thai", -apple-system, system-ui, sans-serif` (one token `--font-ui` in `packages/ui-kit`) · body line-height ≥ 1.5 (Thai above/below marks clip at tighter values); the token test renders a Thai+Latin string ("ช่วย commit ที่ผ่านแล้ว") and checks no glyph is clipped · both fonts are **bundled as woff2** in `packages/ui-kit` (PWA, desktop, demo build; no Google Fonts request at runtime), Thai subset only for Plex · the watch uses the Zepp OS system font (it has Thai) · `JetBrains Mono`/`ui-monospace` for code and terminals · no "horror" fonts
- **Themed feature names (allowed):** The Crypt, Resurrect, sprite, haunt ("Your agents, haunting one room.") · avoid the word kill in the UI (use "Stop bot"; the internal message name stays `bot.kill`)

## 15. Demo / Replay Mode (for the judges)

- `wraith-node fixture record --room <id> --out fixtures/<name>.jsonl` records real events + console frames through redaction (SI-8) → the agent checks with `tools/redact.ts --check` and **a human checks visually before publish**
- `VITE_MODE=demo` uses `DemoTransport`, which replays in real time (and answers `media.get` from fixture blobs that passed the same human check, so media cards open; C74) (can speed up ×1/×4); the Approve button works in UX terms (branches by fixture: `approve.jsonl` / `reject.jsonl`) · has a "Director bar" at the bottom of the screen: scene list (1 Problem, 2 Phone, 3 Handoff, 4 Approve, 5 Console, 6 Move host) + restart button
- deploy to Cloudflare Pages/Workers static with no login required (stays up until 2027-01-29) · build-time assertion: no `WsTransport` in the demo bundle (SI-14)
- Submission text: **"The real system runs on your own machines (herdr-core + kiro-cli); works instantly in Local mode, and you can enable remote/mobile with a relay in your own Cloudflare account."** with a Deploy button and README · the video shows the real system, including the scene **close the Mac lid and the cloud bot moves to V100** (auto failover if P8 is done in time; otherwise Move host)
