## 16. Tech Stack

| Part | Choice |
|---|---|
| Runtime (node, sprite, relay-node) | Node 22 LTS + TypeScript 5.x (pnpm workspaces, ESM) |
| relay-core | pure TypeScript, no platform-bound dependencies |
| Node adapter | `ws` + Fastify 5 + `better-sqlite3` |
| Cloudflare adapter | Workers + Durable Objects (SQLite) + WebSocket Hibernation ✅ · `wrangler` 4 · `compatibility_date ≥ 2026-04-07` |
| Web Push | a pure-WebCrypto library (e.g. `@block65/webcrypto-web-push`) 🧪 Q19 · fallback: own implementation per RFC 8291/8292 |
| Crypto | WebCrypto ECDSA P-256 + HMAC-SHA256 + SHA-256 · JCS implemented in-house in `packages/crypto` (cross-runtime byte-equality tests) · Argon2id for recovery (Node: `argon2`; CF: `@noble/hashes` argon2 🧪; if it exceeds the CPU limit, use PBKDF2-600k instead and record it) |
| ACP | `@agentclientprotocol/sdk` 🧪 Q10 or an in-house JSON-RPC client (`packages/acp-lite`) |
| MCP | `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` (Streamable HTTP server) |
| Validation | zod 3.x (`packages/protocol`) + JSON Schema codegen for docs/fixtures |
| IDs | ULID (`ulid` or in-house; monotonic within a process) |
| Web | React 19 + Vite 6 + Tailwind 4 + TanStack Virtual + `@xterm/xterm` + `react-diff-view` + `qr-scanner` + `qrcode` |
| Desktop | Tauri v2 (+ plugins: shell, notification, global-shortcut, autostart, single-instance, updater) · fallback Electron |
| Watch | Zepp OS Mini Program (Zeus CLI, ZML) · `@noble/curves` if Q26 requires it |
| Voice (P9.18) | `whisper.cpp` (`whisper-cli`, MIT) with `ggml-large-v3-q5_0` (OpenAI Whisper, MIT) and a ggml conversion of `biodatlab/distill-whisper-th-large-v3` (Thonburian Whisper, license checked in Q45) · `ffmpeg` for decoding (LGPL build, called as a process) · PWA: Web Speech API, `MediaRecorder`, `speechSynthesis` |
| Media (P9.19) | `ffmpeg` (same LGPL build as voice, child process) for thumbnails, posters, 720p previews and metadata stripping · `file-type` (MIT) for magic bytes · PWA: `<img>`/`<video>` from blob URLs, canvas re-encode for uploads |
| Tests | Vitest + fast-check (PBT) + Playwright + `@cloudflare/vitest-plugin` (the renamed `@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers`, which is deprecated and supports only Vitest 4; the plugin supports Vitest 4 and 5) + fakes in `packages/fakes` |
| Lint/format | Biome · typecheck `tsc -b` · commit hook `secret-scan` (gitleaks) |
| Packaging | esbuild → `wraith-node`, `wraith-sprite`, `wraith-relay` (`pnpm link --global` for development / bundled in the desktop app / release tarballs + Homebrew formula `wraith-node` / Docker, §4.6.1) · node and sprite are single files the same way as the sidecar (Node SEA by default, Q24b) · Cloudflare = `wrangler deploy` |
| CI | GitHub Actions: `ci.yml` (lint incl. `tools/check-platform.ts`, typecheck, unit/PBT, conformance, E2E on Linux), `desktop-release.yml` (dmg, AppImage/deb, node tarballs, tap pull request), `relay-docker.yml` |

## 17. Repository Layout

```
wraith/
├── .kiro/
│   ├── steering/   product.md tech.md structure.md security.md ux-theme.md agent-loop.md
│   ├── specs/      <24 specs in §20, each with requirements.md design.md tasks.md>
│   ├── hooks/      protocol-codegen · test-on-save · no-trust-all · no-auto-approve · fixture-redact-check · secret-scan
│   ├── agents/     architect.json implementer.json reviewer.json tester.json   (build agents for this repo: explicit allowlist —
│   │               read/grep; fs_write inside the repo; shell `pnpm` install/build/test/lint/typecheck, `git` (no force push), `wrangler dev`;
│   │               no wildcards; reviewer = read/grep only)
│   └── settings/mcp.json
├── apps/
│   ├── relay-cf/     # Cloudflare adapter: Worker + SpaceDO (wrangler.toml, Deploy button)
│   ├── relay-node/   # wraith-relay standalone (Fastify, Dockerfile)
│   ├── node/         # wraith-node (+ relay-embedded, migrations/, native/clock, src/voice/ for P9.18, src/media/ for P9.19)
│   ├── sprite/        # wraith-sprite
│   ├── web/          # PWA (mobile + desktop WebView + demo build)
│   ├── desktop/      # Tauri v2 (src-tauri/, binaries/wraith-node-<triple>)
│   └── watch-zepp/   # Zepp OS Mini Program (app/, app-side/, setting/, app-service/)
├── packages/
│   ├── protocol/     # zod schemas, message catalogue, templates, JSON Schema codegen
│   ├── crypto/       # JCS, sign/verify, HMAC, hashing, key import/export
│   ├── relay-core/   # relay logic + ports + migrations/ + conformance tests
│   ├── acp-lite/     # (if Q10 fails) JSON-RPC ACP client
│   ├── fakes/        # fake-acp-agent, fake-hc, fake-kiro-exec, fake-push-service, fake-tui, fake-github
│   ├── platform/     # every OS-specific call: IPC, file modes, login env, service unit, signals (darwin/linux/win32 stub, §4.10)
│   └── ui-kit/       # tokens (§14.6), sprite SVGs, shared components
├── tools/            record-fixture.ts redact.ts check-platform.ts spike/*.ts
├── fixtures/         *.jsonl (redacted)
├── spike/            scripts and raw results for §19 (not product code)
├── .github/workflows/ ci.yml desktop-release.yml relay-docker.yml
├── deploy/{launchd,systemd,docker,homebrew}/   (homebrew/ = formula/cask templates for the tap repo)
├── docs/  architecture.md (this document, the English master) kiro-usage.md demo-script.md self-host.md windows-wsl.md (P9.20) THIRD_PARTY.md
│          spikes/RESULTS.md DECISIONS-PENDING.md PHASE-STATUS.md
├── LICENSE (Apache-2.0 — see §24.2)
└── README.md
```

## 18. Phase Plan (build order to the final version, including the watch) 💡

> **Principle (user confirmed 2026-11-01, DECISION-11):** Do not cut scope because of the deadline · This table is the **build order**. Whatever is done before 2026-12-03 is what gets submitted (P6); the rest continues afterwards · Each phase is **usable/demoable on its own** and has a testable DoD · The agent loop updates `docs/PHASE-STATUS.md` every time a DoD item passes

### 18.0 Dependency graph and rules for picking the next phase

```
P0 ──► P1 ──► P2 ──► P3 (cloud bot)        ──► P6 (submission package, time-boxed)
        │       │                               ▲
        │       └──► P4 (remote relay + mobile) ─┤──► P7 (watch)      ──► P9 (extensions)
        └──────────► P5 (desktop)  ─────────────┘──► P8 (crypt + resilience) ─┘
```
- **Rule:** Work in numeric order · If the current phase is blocked by "needs a human" (§22), move to the next phase whose **entry criteria are met** (e.g. P3 waits for a Kiro Cloud session → do P4 first; P7 waits for the real watch → do P8 first), then come back
- **Swap in P2 (C66):** when `spikes/RESULTS.md` records Q33a as failed for Claude or Kiro, P9.17-D1…D3 are copied into `PHASE-STATUS.md` as applicable P2 DoD items (D4 is the Codex part); otherwise P2 lists them as n/a and they stay in P9.17
- **P6 is a special time-boxed phase:** entry = date **2026-11-26** (T-7) or P5 done, whichever comes first · During P6, do not start a new phase until all P6-D items pass (except small spikes)
- A new phase may start when **its entry criteria are met and at least 90% of the *applicable* DoD items of the previous phase have passed**. "Previous phase" = the nearest lower-numbered phase that is not `blocked-human`; DoD items that depend on a phase not yet done (e.g. P5-D7 needs P4) are not applicable and do not count (open items must be recorded in `PHASE-STATUS.md` with a reason, and must not be items tied to SI-\*)

### 18.1 P0 — Spikes & Scaffold (1–3 days)

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | Answer every Q that blocks P1, and lay out the repo/CI/steering so the loop can work |
| **Scope in** | monorepo (pnpm, TS, Biome, Vitest, CI) · all 6 `.kiro/steering/*` files · `packages/protocol` + `packages/crypto` skeleton (JCS, sign/verify, Envelope) · `packages/fakes` skeleton (fake-acp-agent, fake-hc) · `packages/platform` skeleton (`darwin`, `linux`, `win32` stub) + `tools/check-platform.ts` in CI (§4.10) · spike scripts for groups A, B (§19) · `docs/spikes/RESULTS.md`, `docs/PHASE-STATUS.md`, `docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md` · LICENSE + README skeleton + THIRD_PARTY (herdr-core, ACP SDK) · `.kiro/hooks` (`no-trust-all`, `no-auto-approve`, `secret-scan`) and `.kiro/agents` (§17) · GitHub secret scanning + push protection on **before the first push** (repo is public from the start, DECISION-19) |
| **Scope out** | Any product code other than protocol/crypto · UI · relay |
| **Entry** | Repo created (human: name/owner §24.2; if no answer, use the defaults) · Machine has herdr-core ≥ 0.1.18 (`hc`; the owner's machines run 0.1.16 on 2026-11-05, so run `hc update` first) and a logged-in `kiro-cli` (human) |
| **DoD** | **P0-D1** `pnpm -r test` passes on CI (Linux + macOS) **and** the P0-D2/P0-D3 suites exist and run (an empty test run does not count) · **P0-D2** JCS byte-equality test passes on Node and in the Workers runtime via `@cloudflare/vitest-plugin` · **P0-D3** sign→verify round-trip + mutate-any-byte→reject (PBT 1,000 runs) · **P0-D4** `RESULTS.md` has a pass/fail answer + evidence (log/JSON) for Q1, Q2, Q3, Q3b, Q4, Q6, Q7, Q10, Q11, Q12, Q13, Q23, Q30, Q31, Q32, Q33, Q35, Q43 and the branch selected (Q14 may be PARTIAL: it needs a human logout/login 🧑) · **P0-D5** `.kiro/steering` states the prohibitions from SI-1, SI-2, SI-6, SI-8 as plain sentences · **P0-D6** hooks `no-trust-all`, `no-auto-approve`, `secret-scan` work (tested with sample files that must be caught) · **P0-D7** `tools/check-platform.ts` fails on a sample file outside `packages/platform` that uses `process.platform` or `SIGHUP`, and passes on the real tree |
| **Deliverables** | repo + green CI · `RESULTS.md` · `packages/{protocol,crypto}` v0 |
| **Specs** | S-protocol, S-crypto (start), S-ops-packaging (start: CI) |
| **Human** | Log in to kiro-cli; if Q3 and Q3b both fail → **stop and notify** (§22.3) |
| **Next** | P1 |

picking the same folder again opens the same bot (no second pane); restarting the node and opening the folder resumes the same session (DECISION-24) · **P1-D13** rename `@app` to `@shop` → mentions resolve to the same bot; renaming another bot to `shop` → `E_NAME_TAKEN` with a suggestion (DECISION-27) · **P1-D14** `wraith-node roots add ~/elsewhere` and `node.roots` from the PWA work; `fs.list` rejects `..`, a symlink pointing outside the roots and `/code/app2` under root `/code/app`; after 10 minutes it returns to read-only · **P1-D9** kill `kiro-cli` under the sprite → respawn, epoch+1, old approvals `expired` · **P1-D10** integration suite with fake-acp-agent + fake-hc passes on CI (no Kiro needed) · **P1-D11** `wraith-node doctor` reports everything and `agents validate` rejects `--trust-all-tools` · **P1-D12** in a test `HC_SESSION`: `hc server restart` → the bot keeps working with no `restoring`; kill the pane's holder → bot `restoring`, the node respawns the sprite with ids only and the bot is back to `idle` within 120s · **P1-D15** change the approval timeout to 1 minute in Settings → `node.configured` arrives, a new request expires in 60s while a pending one keeps its `expiresAt`; ### 18.3 P2 — Group chat on one machine

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | Several bots hand work to each other via `@`/MCP and send results back, with loop-guard and Freeze |

| **Scope out** | cloud bot · cross-machine (can be tested with 2 node processes on one machine connected to the same **embedded Local relay**, which accepts a second paired node over `127.0.0.1` because relay-core has no mode-specific pairing rule (§3.4, DECISION-43); pairing QR belongs to P4 → use dev pairing = `wraith-node pair --dev --print-code`, the normal §3.4 flow with the QR payload pasted as text; no setup-token shortcut, SI-11 unchanged) |

| **DoD** | **P2-D1** in a group with `@app` and `@app-v100`: `@app ask @app-v100 to run tests` → app calls `wraith_mention` → app-v100 gets the prompt with the packet (resource block) → returns to app → handoff/return cards complete · **P2-D2** fake agents that keep forwarding to each other → stop at depth 4 / budget 12 / rate 6 per minute with an `E_LOOP_*` card, and Freeze stops everything · **P2-D3** PBT loop-guard: `forwardDepth/root` values supplied by the sender have no effect · **P2-D4** `wraith_room_context` returns only verified events (test injects fake events into history) · **P2-D5** handoff to T0 gets `E_TIER_UNSUPPORTED`; `requires:["gpu"]` to a bot without it → `E_CAPABILITY_MISSING` with suggested · **P2-D6** T0 `shell` bot shows a status card labelled raw snapshot that changes with the shell's OSC 7501 reports (a test script prints them), and "running" with no status for a program that never reports · **P2-D7** redactor strips AWS key/GitHub token in attachments and tool output (tested with fixtures) · **P2-D8** E2E: 3 bots (`app`, `reviewer`, a shell bot) in one group complete the "review then test" scenario within 1 hour with no unexpected errors in the log · **P2-D10** create group `# release` with `@app` and `@app-v100` → each gets a seat pane on its node; a message in the group reaches only the seats and the reply appears in the group, not in `@app`'s own chat; two sessions of `@app` asking to edit at once → both cards appear at once; after both are allowed, the second shows `waiting_write` until the first finishes; an unanswered card never blocks the other session; removing `@app-v100` closes its seat; adding `@app-cloud` → `E_CLOUD_NO_GROUPS`; Take control on a seat pane → `E_SEAT_READ_ONLY` and typing in that pane at the machine does nothing; with a live seat, write-tool `approval.request.options` contain no `allow_always`, a forged `allow_always` answer is refused (`E_OPTION_INVALID`), and after the last seat closes a new request offers it again; after 10 idle minutes `@app`'s own chat shows "From # release: …" · **P2-D11** in a bot chat a message without `@` reaches that bot; in a group a user `@reviewer` (not a member) → `E_NOT_MEMBER`, while a bot handoff to `@reviewer` works · **P2-D16** turn on Autopilot for `@app` (1 h) from the phone → write/execute calls in `@app`'s chat run with "auto-approved by Autopilot" cards and Changes attribution; `git push` and a write outside the workspace still ask; a handoff from `@reviewer` to `@app` asks for every call; Stop turns Autopilot off; after `until` it asks again; after 3 Allow once taps for `@app` within 10 min the 4th approval sheet shows the suggestion strip, "Not for this bot" hides it for good on that device, and no strip appears for a handoff request; a watch or viewer `bot.configure{autopilot}` → `E_CAP_DENIED`; a `wraith_spawn_request`, `sudo …`, `bash -c '…'` and a write in a group seat all still ask; revoking the enabling device turns it off; no push is sent for auto-approved calls; `until` beyond 24 h → `E_SCHEMA_INVALID`; turning off `git-push` in Settings lets `git push` run on Autopilot while `sudo` still asks · **P2-D18** (DECISION-53) in `# main` with `@app` and `@reviewer`: send 3 messages without mentions, then `@app summarise` → `@app`'s seat prompt holds exactly those 3 plus its own last reply, and a second `@app` prompt holds only what came after; 200 queued messages → the block stays ≤ 24 KB and says "N earlier messages"; `@app` calls `wraith_mention("reviewer", …, mode:"transfer")` → its turn ends with "handed this to @reviewer", `@reviewer`'s answer appears as the reply to the user and `@app` gets no return; with `mode:"assign"` `@app` gets the return; a chain `@app` → transfer `@tester` → transfer `@reviewer` ends with `@reviewer`'s answer as the reply to the user and no return to `@app` or `@tester` · **P2-D17** in `# main`, long-press `@reviewer` → Respond → `@reviewer` answers the last 3 messages in the group; the prompt is the Wraith template plus verified events only; a T0 member shows the option disabled; a viewer device has no option · **P2-D15** phone: in `@app`'s chat while it works the send button shows ■ Stop; tapping it stops the turn; typing a letter turns it back into Send; in a group with two working bots ■ opens "Stop which bot?"; the Freeze button shows ❄ and never ■ · **P2-D14** in a group with `@app` and `@app-v100` both working, Esc Esc opens "Stop which bot?"; Esc closes it and both keep working; choosing `@app` stops only `@app`'s seat in this group · **P2-D13** in a group type `@app /` → `@app`'s skills; `/` with no mention → "Pick a bot first"; a description containing `<script>` or a fake secret renders as plain, redacted text · **P2-D12** after `@app` edits two files and runs a formatter command, the Changes sheet in `@app`'s chat (button shows the count) lists all changed files with +/−, each attributed to the right tool call and approval (the command's files via the lock window); `View diff` from the tool card opens the diff; asking for `.env` or a path outside the workspace → `E_FILE_DENIED`; the relay stores none of it; a non-git workspace shows "Changes need git" · **P2-D9** app calls `wraith_spawn_request{workspace:"~/code/app/api", agent:"kiro"}` → approval card (node-signed) → Allow once from the PWA → the sheet shows the full instructions → `api` pane appears; reject/timeout → nothing spawned; an agent without `spawnableByBots` → `E_CAP_DENIED`; the 4th bot-requested bot in the requester's subtree → `E_LOOP_BUDGET`; a workspace outside the requester's subtree → `E_PATH_OUTSIDE_WORKSPACE` |
| **Deliverables** | MCP server, loop-guard, packet template, T0 cards |

|---|---|

| **DoD** | **P4-D0** (C71, CLI) on the Mac in Local mode with 2 bots: `wraith-node pair <cloudflare-url> <ticket>` (ticket from the iPhone owner) → the bots appear in the new Space with their own chats, `~/.wraith/relay-local/` and `spaces/<localId>/` are untouched, `history` rows of the two Spaces never collide (same `seq` in both) and no old `outbox` row reaches the new relay · `wraith-node unpair` → the Local chats are back, the Cloudflare roster shows the Mac offline · `pair` while a bot works → refused · · **P4-D1** `wrangler deploy` from a fresh repo → open the setup link on iPhone → create a Space → recovery code is shown · **P4-D2** `wraith-node pair` on Mac and V100 with ticket + QR scan → both online in /machines and `trust.json` pins the owner key · **P4-D3** from iPhone (mobile network) send `@app-v100` (on V100) → approve from iPhone → passes · **P4-D4** Web Push reaches a locked iPhone within 10s of `approval.request` (or in-app badge if Q19 fails, recorded in RESULTS) · **P4-D5** conformance suite passes on all 3 adapters, including the property "lease is not taken over before `last_seen + 45s`" under simulated hibernate/redeploy · **P4-D6** leave the DO idle for 10 minutes → no extra duration charge in the measurement (Q22) and the node stays online from the auto-response timestamp · **P4-D7** revoke a device from the owner → that device is cut off and its old commands are rejected by the node with `E_REVOKED` · **P4-D8** `docker run wraith-relay` + `tailscale serve` → iPhone works (manual test) · **P4-D9** disconnect the Mac from the network for 2 minutes → bots on the Mac `offline`, pending work stays in `outbox_relay`, after reconnect everything is sent with no duplicates · **P4-D10** quota: measure requests/rows/GB-s for a simulated usage day (load script with 200 prompts + 30 minutes of console) < 50% of the Free plan · **P4-D11** a desktop-browser owner pairs V100 by pasting the `WRP1-` code; the fingerprints on both screens match; an offer whose key differs from the code is never signed · **P4-D12** a View-only phone sees timelines, console, Files and Changes, but every write or approval it signs (forged through a fake client) gets `E_CAP_DENIED` from the relay **and** the node; PBT #16 runs for `viewer` · **P4-D13** opening a pairing link and the setup link in iOS Safari (UA-emulated in E2E, real iPhone by hand) creates no key and shows Add to Home Screen + Copy; the installed PWA completes pairing with the same, still-unused ticket |

|---|---|

reopen → single instance · **P5-D5** a `wraith-node` from launchd is already running → the app attaches to it and does not spawn another · **P5-D6** GitHub Actions creates a draft release with `.dmg` (universal) + AppImage from tag `v0.5.0-test`, and the dmg opens after `xattr -dr` · **P5-D7** remote mode: set the P4 relay URL → desktop becomes a paired device that can approve · **P5-D8** `.app` size < 60MB (if SEA), actual value recorded in RESULTS · **P5-D9** remote mode with the relay stopped (or network off): a bot on this Mac asks for approval → the sheet still appears in the desktop app → Allow once passes via `127.0.0.1` → when the relay returns, the phone shows the approval as resolved; the same `approval.respond` sent on both paths is applied once; a request from a process with no device key, or from another `Origin`, is rejected (`E_BAD_SIGNATURE` / 403) · **P5-D10** Join with "Also run bots on this Mac" on → one confirmation on the owner phone adds both a machine and a device; a relay that swaps either key or adds `cloud-host` makes both sides abort (`E_PAIR_HMAC_MISMATCH`); the secret appears in no relay log, `/local/v1/ws` frame or localhost HTTP request (test inspects them) · **P5-D11** remote mode: Run bots on this Mac off → bots show `paused` (not `offline`) on the phone after 60s; on → bots return without pairing; Local mode shows Pause all bots and the app stays connected · **P5-D12** downgrade: revoke the machine role → node stops, `node.key` deleted, bots `offline`, the app window still approves as a device · **P5-D13** (C71) Settings → Relay wizard in Wraith.app: Cloudflare URL → Check → "no Space yet" → **Create on my phone** (QR → iPhone creates the Space → Add machine → ticket → Join) → Switch; repeat on a fresh relay with **Create here** (recovery code, no QR) → Switch; **Go back to Local** shows the Local chats; a wrong URL → `E_RELAY_UNREACHABLE`; the cards on the iPhone PWA are read-only; Switch is disabled while a bot works · **P5-D14** (C76) tag `v0.5.0-test` → the release has the three node tarballs + `SHA256SUMS`; on a clean Apple Silicon Mac `brew install <owner>/wraith/wraith-node` (from the merged tap PR) → `wraith-node doctor` runs with no Gatekeeper prompt, `wraith-sprite` is on PATH, `brew uninstall` leaves `~/.wraith` alone; the same install + `doctor` with Homebrew on the Linux box (Homebrew may rewrite files in a Linux keg, which must not break the executables 🧪) · **P5-D15** headless: on the Linux box with no browser, follow §3.4 item 8 over SSH → the machine appears on the phone, a bot starts, and after a reboot it is back with nobody logged in (linger, Q14) · **P5-D16** `brew upgrade` to a second test tag + `wraith-node service restart` while a bot is idle → the bot keeps its pane and answers a prompt; `wraith-node status` shows the build mismatch for that sprite (logged, not refused); kill that pane's holder process (a lost pane, as in Q6 (b); not `hc kill`, which counts as a person's close) → the node respawns it from `<bin-dir>` → the new sprite runs and the mismatch clears |

|---|---|
| **Goal** | See approvals and tap Allow once / Reject from the watch, send quick messages, Freeze · Works even though iOS does not let the Side Service run in the background |
| **Scope in** | Full HTTP API (`inbox` long-poll, `cmd`, `pair`, `approvals/:id`, `bots`, `quick-messages`, `api_nonces`, rate limit) in relay-core + both adapters · cap `watch` checked at relay and node · Zepp Mini Program: Device App (Home, Approval, Bots, Say, Freeze, Settings), Side Service (signing, roster pin, inbox loop, cache), Settings App (relay URL, pairing code), App Service `approve/reject` (if Q27b passes) · PWA: Quick messages settings, Add device accepts a watch code (paste already exists from P4) and shows the `watch` role chip in /machines · Zepp Simulator tests |
| **Scope out** | Garmin/Wear OS/Apple Watch · Zepp App Store publishing (do once stable) |

| **DoD** | **P7-D1** HTTP API conformance: inbox/cmd/pair pass on 3 adapters, including replay nonce, skew, rate limit, cap watch (PBT cap matrix) · **P7-D2** pair the watch with a text code from the Settings App → appears in /machines as `watch` and the Side Service pins the owner key (wrong HMAC → abort) · **P7-D3** approval on Mac → open the Mini Program → card appears within 1 poll cycle (≤ 25s) → tap Allow once → tool runs and the PWA sees `resolved{by:watch}` · **P7-D4** watch sends `allow_always` (forged request from a fake) → relay and node respond `E_CAP_DENIED` · **P7-D5** diff/long → Allow button is not shown, only "Open on phone" · **P7-D6** Say "Run the tests" to app-v100 → prompt enters the queue with `by:"watch"` · **P7-D7** Freeze from the watch stops handoffs in every room; unfreeze from the watch has no button/is rejected · **P7-D8** iOS: lock the iPhone, send an approval → Web Push is mirrored to the watch → open the Mini Program from the notification → can approve (layer 1 must always pass) · **P7-D9** (only if Q27b passes) the Allow button in the watch notification works ≥ 8/10 times, and when it is too late it opens the Approval page instead (no false allow) · **P7-D10** every screen passes on the Simulator at 390×450 with no text overflow (screenshot test) |

### 18.9 P8 — The Crypt & Resilience

| | |
|---|---|
| **Goal** | Wake sessions that were closed, automatic cloud host failover, owner recovery, relay migration, survive reboot |
| **Scope in** | crypt-scanner + crypt-index + `/crypt` UI + Resurrect/Fork (Kiro local/cloud, Claude metadata) · auto failover (alarm, `migrating`) + the "close the Mac lid" scene · recovery code + `trust --rotate` + passkey/biometric unlock before signing an approval (WebAuthn user-verification gate 💡) · `relay migrate` + `wraith-relay export/import` · `service install` tested with a real reboot (Q14) · PAT in keychain · push subscription cleanup · Web Push retry · quota degrade path tested for real |
| **Scope out** | T2 Claude plugin, fan-in (P9) |
| **Entry** | P3-D ≥ 90% and P4-D ≥ 90% · Q7, Q18 answered |
| **DoD** | **P8-D1** close a bot on purpose → appears in the Crypt within 60s → Resurrect → comes back with the same `botId` and the same context (asking "what did we do last?" gets an answer) · **P8-D2** concurrent Resurrect from 2 devices at the same time → one gets the lease, the other `E_LEASE_HELD` · **P8-D3** kill the holder of a bot's pane (test `HC_SESSION`) → the node respawns the sprite, the bot does not show up in the Crypt during 120s and returns to `idle`; `hc server restart` → the bot keeps working with no `restoring` · **P8-D4** close the Mac lid (host of the cloud bot) → within 45–120s V100 gets the lease, resumes the session, the room shows a system card; open the Mac lid → old sprite exits with `lease.lost`, no duplicate prompt (marker checked) · **P8-D5** PBT lease timeout model (ping ≤ recv, fence 35, expiry 45, unbounded pong delay) passes · **P8-D6** recovery: simulate a lost owner → recovery code → new owner → rotate on 2 nodes → old roster no longer works (commands signed with the old owner are rejected) · **P8-D7** `relay migrate` from Local → Cloudflare, and 30 days of history and the roster are complete (checksum compared) · **P8-D8** reboot the Mac → herdr-core + node come back on their own and bots go `restoring→idle` · **P8-D9** passkey gate: no user verification → no approval is signed (E2E with a virtual authenticator) |
| **Deliverables** | Crypt, failover, recovery, migrate, service units |
| **Specs** | S-crypt, S-relay-lease (failover), S-pairing-trust (recovery), S-ops-packaging (service), S-pwa-shell (passkey) |
| **Human** | Real Mac lid-close scene, real reboot |
| **Next** | P9 |

## 19. Spike Plan (P0 and spikes for later phases) 💡

> **Result format:** Every Q has a row in `docs/spikes/RESULTS.md`: `Q | date | runner | result: PASS/FAIL/PARTIAL | evidence (path in spike/) | branch selected | affects`. Scripts live in `spike/<group>/<q>.ts` and must be re-runnable with `pnpm spike <q>`
> **Runner:** 🤖 = agent can do it alone · 🧑 = needs a human (login, real device, account) · 🤖+🧑 = agent writes the script and analyses; the human runs it or captures evidence
> **Ordered by how much they unblock:** A and B block P1 → do first · D blocks P4 · C blocks P3 · E blocks P5 · F blocks P7 · G blocks P9.1 · I blocks P1 bot creation · J (Q35, Q43 → P1; Q36–Q38 → P9.14/P9.15; Q39–Q42 → P9.16) · H blocks P9.12 (optional, last)

### Group A — herdr-core (blocks P1) 🤖

All Group A runs use a **test core**: `HC_SESSION=wsp` with `XDG_STATE_HOME`/`XDG_CONFIG_HOME` under a short path such as `/tmp/hcw/` (socket paths over 103 bytes are refused on macOS ✅), stopped and deleted afterwards; never `hc server stop`/`restart` on the owner's `default` core. ✅ marks what a pre-check on hc 0.1.16 already showed (2026-11-05); the spike still records evidence in RESULTS.

| Q | Question | Experiment | Passes when | If it fails → branch | Affects |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Q2** | Start a pane with argv, cwd, env and labels | `hc schema > spike/hc/schema.json` from the pinned version · `terminal.run` a stub that prints `$PWD`, `$WRAITH_X`, `$HC_TERMINAL_ID` · `terminal.list{labels}` · note whether the pane shows in Herdr.app · check that `terminal.get` with an address like `t7` resolves the address, never a terminal *named* `t7` · confirm `env -0` exists on macOS · compare the pane's `PATH` and agent variables with a login shell's, for a core started from a terminal and one started by the Herdr Core app | ✅ cwd, env and labels applied; `HC_TERMINAL_ID` is set in the child (so the sprite must strip it) · Herdr.app visibility recorded either way | – (pre-check passed) · if API panes don't show in Herdr.app: G3 at the machine uses `hc attach` | S-node-core pane-manager |
| **Q1** | Cost of `terminal.read` every 250 ms | on hc ≥ 0.1.17 (0.1.16 stalls ~25 s reading a full-screen program at a prompt, CHANGELOG 0.1.17) run `htop` in a pane, `terminal.read` every 250 ms for 5 minutes, measure core CPU and payload | core CPU increase < 5%, payload < 64 KB · ✅ text is plain (no ANSI colour) | high CPU → poll 1 s · large payload → send only line diffs (Q20) | S-console |
| **Q6** | Node respawn after a lost pane | stub sprite pane with Wraith labels · (a) `hc server restart` (b) kill the holder pid from `server.status` (c) `hc server stop` + start · a node stub subscribed to events | ✅ (a) same pid keeps running · ✅ (b) pane `finished` with reason `holder_lost` · ✅ `hc kill` of a sprite stub that exits 75 on SIGHUP → `killed` (code 75); `kill -TERM <pid>` → `exited` 75 · still to record: what Herdr.app's close button records (expected `killed`) · the node stub respawns the pane with the same ids within 10 s for (b) and (c), and never for `exited 0` or `killed` | node also polls `terminal.list{labels}` every 5 s · if Herdr.app's close records `exited 75`: document "close bots with `/exit`" and ask upstream to record `killed` | S-sprite-acp, S-crypt |
| **Q12** | Does herdr-core say who sent input to a pane? | subscribe to events, then `hc send` from another shell | an event names the pane and the sending client | **expected** (no such event in the 0.1.16 schema ✅): drop `herdr-guard`, keep the limitation in §12 | S-node-core |
| **Q13** | Do hc templates, `first_message` or `terminal.wait` reduce the sprite's work? | read the schema + try them | one replaces part of the sprite's ACP or queue work | use the sprite as designed (no impact; expected) | S-sprite-acp (note) |
| **Q14** 🧑 | Is herdr-core back after login and reboot without the app? | macOS (Herdr Core app, 0.1.18) and Linux (user unit); log out/in and reboot, then check the socket; Linux: also reboot **with nobody logging in** (linger on) | socket ready within 10 s after login (Linux with linger: after boot) · a respawned pane's `PATH` finds `kiro-cli` · record the reason a reboot leaves on a sprite pane (must not be `killed`) | the `wraith-node` unit runs `hc server start` before connecting (documented in `deploy/`) | S-ops-packaging |

### Group B — Kiro ACP local (blocks P1/P2) 🤖+🧑 (must be logged in)

| Q | Question | Experiment | Passes when | If it fails → branch | Affects |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Q3** | Does Kiro ACP really send `session/request_permission`? | `spike/acp-echo.ts`: spawn `kiro-cli acp --agent-engine v3 --agent spike` (profile does not trust `execute_bash`) → prompt "run `echo hi`" → log all JSON-RPC to `spike/kiro/q3.jsonl` (usable as a fixture) | `session/request_permission` is seen with `options[]`, and answering `selected` makes the tool run | **Q3b** | S-approval (main path) |
| **Q3b** | Does the `preToolUse` hook work in `kiro-cli acp`? | put a hook in `.kiro/agents/spike.json` that does `curl -X POST 127.0.0.1:7782/hook`, then run acp | hook fires before the tool and exit 2 can block; measure the hook timeout | **Both Q3 and Q3b fail = STOP, notify the human** (differentiator 2 is impossible for Kiro) | S-approval (fallback) |
| **Q10** | Does `@agentclientprotocol/sdk` work with Kiro? | acp-echo with the SDK vs raw JSON-RPC, compared | initialize/new/prompt/update/permission all work with no schema reject | `packages/acp-lite` (own JSON-RPC, loose zod validation) | S-sprite-acp |
| **Q11** | Does Kiro support MCP http in `session/new`? | check `mcpCapabilities` in the `initialize` response; send `mcpServers:[{type:"http"…}]` to an MCP echo server; have the agent call a tool | agent can call a tool over http | stdio shim `wraith-mcp-stdio` (proxy to 127.0.0.1) or put it in `.kiro/agents/*.json` | S-node-local-endpoints |
| **Q4** | Does Kiro call `terminal/create`? | declare `clientCapabilities.terminal:true`, then ask it to run a long command | get `terminal/create` + `terminal/output` | show output in the ToolCard (no side pane) | S-sprite-acp, S-console |
| **Q7** | Does `--list-sessions` have JSON? | `kiro-cli chat --list-sessions --help`; try `--format json` | JSON exists | use ACP `session/list` only | S-crypt |
| **Q23** | Does Kiro's ACP give stable message ids on replay? | `session/load` the same session twice, compare ids of `agent_message_chunk`/tool_call | ids are equal both times | dedupe with `hash(turnIndex, text)` | S-cloud-bot |
| Q8 (small remainder) | Can the contest credits be used on cloud? | check the usage dashboard after running 1 cloud session 🧑 | yes | not blocking (user has Pro) | – |

### Group D — Cloudflare (blocks P4) 🤖 (with `@cloudflare/vitest-plugin`/miniflare) + 🧑 (first real deploy for Q21/Q22)

| Q | Question | Experiment | Passes when | If it fails → branch | Affects |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Q22** | Do `setWebSocketAutoResponse` + `getWebSocketAutoResponseTimestamp` work without waking the DO, without counting requests, and are readable after hibernate · How quickly is a silently dead TCP detected? | deploy a test DO; client sends "ping" every 15s for 30 minutes; check duration/requests metrics; let the DO hibernate, then read the timestamp; cut the client's network without sending FIN and measure when `webSocketClose/Error` is called | duration ≈ 0 while idle, requests do not increase from pings, timestamp correct after hibernate, close called within ≤ 2 minutes | app-level heartbeat: `expiry = 3 × pingInterval`, `fence = expiry − 10s` (interval 60s → 180/170) and accept the extra duration | S-relay-adapter-cf, S-relay-lease |
| **Q19** | Does a Web Push lib on Workers really work with iOS PWA? | Worker sends push with `@block65/webcrypto-web-push` to a subscription from an iPhone (installed PWA) | notification appears on the locked iPhone | write it ourselves per RFC 8291/8292 with WebCrypto (1–2 days) · if that still fails → DECISION-8 in-app | S-web-push |
| **Q20** | Maximum WS message size on Workers/DO | send 64KB/256KB/1MB/2MB through the DO | actual value known | adjust limits in `packages/protocol/limits.ts` and split frames | S-relay-core, S-console |
| **Q21** | Is the Free plan enough for 1 day of real use? | load script (200 prompts, 30 minutes of console, 3 node pings) on a real deployment; read metrics | requests < 50k, rows < 50k, GB-s < 6.5k | recommend Workers Paid ($5/month) in docs/self-host; no architecture change | docs |

|---|---|

| Q33a fails for an agent | Q33c passes → `seats:"swap"` (one process switching sessions, boundary 5) · fails too → `seats:"none"` (`E_SEATS_UNSUPPORTED`) |
| Q3 fails, Q3b passes | Kiro approval via `preToolUse` + one-shot grant (§7.2) |
| Q3, Q3b fail | **STOP** → human decides (options: T2 Claude as the main bot that can be approved / wait for a Kiro release / change positioning) |
| Q5+Q5b pass | cloud bot = sprite ACP remote load (full stream) |
| Q5 fails, Q5c passes | Cloud Status Bot (exec mode) + `@` from stdout |
| Q5, Q5c fail | cloud bot = PR card only + notify the human (DECISION-9) |
| Q22 passes | presence per §4.2 (15s/35s/45s) |
| Q22 fails | app-level heartbeat 60s/170s/180s and accept the duration cost |
| Q19 passes / fails | Web Push / in-app + badge (DECISION-8) |
| Q24a passes / fails | WebView signs / Rust keychain signs after showing the request |
| Q24b passes / fails | single-file externalBin / resources folder / Electron |
| Q27a, Q27b pass | watch notifies by itself and Allow from the notification (layer 3) |
| Q35 passes / fails | `bot.commands{source:"acp"}` / folder scan (`source:"files"`), nothing found → `source:"none"` |
| Q36 passes (and H11 answered) | Claude bots via `--mode sdk` + Claude Rewind (P9.14) |
| Q36 fails | no Claude T1 bot (Claude only as T0, or T2 plugin P9.4); notify the human |
| Q37 passes / fails | Kiro Rewind (`restore:"both"` only) / no Kiro Rewind |
| Q38 ACP passes / hook passes / both fail | Grok T1 via ACP / T1 with hook-gated approvals / Grok stays T0 |
| Q39–Q41 pass (Claude) / Q42 passes (Kiro) | that agent gets Terminal mode (P9.16); Q39 fails but the fork variant works → fork-visit Terminal mode; Q40 fails → none for Claude |
| Q43 passes / fails | `acp --agent wraith-<botId>` / agent selected after `initialize` |
| Q47–Q49 pass / fail | P9.21 native Windows can start / stays blocked |
| Q50 passes / fails | P9.20 WSL2 as written / logon-task start or blocked |

---
## 20. Spec Decomposition (`.kiro/specs/<name>/`) 💡

> Every spec has `requirements.md` (EARS, in English, because it is the part judges and machines read), `design.md` (refers to § of this document, does not copy it), `tasks.md` (subtasks mapped to DoD) · The requirements below are the **minimum** set: each spec must also carry, in English, every contract it implements from this document (§6, §10, §11, §12.2, §14, §18 DoD), so `.kiro/specs/` stays complete · "SHALL NOT" statements that cite SI-\* must not be removed or edited by the loop (§22)
> EARS patterns: **Ubiquitous** "The X shall …" · **Event** "WHEN … the X shall …" · **State** "WHILE … the X shall …" · **Unwanted** "IF … THEN the X shall …" · **Optional** "WHERE … the X shall …"

| # | Spec | Phase | Depends on | Key design notes (→ §) | Representative EARS requirements |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **S-protocol** | P0–P1 | – | §6.1, §6.2, §6.9 · zod is the source of truth; codegen JSON Schema + TS types; `limits.ts`; templates | R1 The protocol package shall export one zod schema per `meta.type` and a `SIGNER` map. · R2 WHEN an envelope fails schema validation, the receiver shall reject it with `E_UNSUPPORTED_VERSION` or `E_PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE` before any other processing. · R3 The protocol package shall not depend on any runtime-specific module. · R4 WHEN a schema changes, the `protocol-codegen` hook shall regenerate JSON Schema and fixtures in the same commit. |
| 2 | **S-crypto** | P0–P1 | 1 | §6.1 · JCS, ES256, HMAC, SHA-256, key import/export JWK; identical bytes across browser/Worker/Node | R1 The crypto package shall produce byte-identical JCS output for the same JSON value on Node, Workers and browsers. · R2 The signature scope shall be `{v, meta, body}` and shall exclude `sig` and `relay`. · R3 IF any byte of `meta` or `body` of a signed envelope is modified, THEN `verify()` shall return false. · R4 The crypto package shall expose `requestHash(envelope)` = base64url(SHA-256(JCS({v,meta,body}))). |
| 3 | **S-relay-core** | P1–P2 | 1, 2 | §4.2, §6.8, §10.1, §10.3 · ports only; no in-memory truth; conformance suite | R1 The relay shall assign `relay.seq` only to envelopes with `meta.persist = true`. · R2 WHEN a duplicate `meta.id` is received, the relay shall not persist it again and shall reply `ack` with the original `seq`. · R3 WHEN a node reconnects with `hello{lastAckedId}`, the relay shall deliver all queued envelopes newer than that id exactly once. · R4 The relay shall compute `root` and `forwardDepth` from its own `prompts` table and shall ignore any such values supplied by the sender. · R5 IF forwardDepth would exceed 4, or root budget 12, or room rate 6/min, THEN the relay shall reject with the corresponding `E_LOOP_*` and emit a `system.card`. · R6 The relay shall not contain any code path that creates an `approval.respond` envelope (SI-1). · R7 WHILE a room is frozen, the relay shall reject `room.handoff` with `E_ROOM_FROZEN` and shall still accept `room.return` (C66). · R8 WHEN presence is evaluated, the relay shall compute `lastSeen` from storage and socket auto-response timestamps and shall never default a missing value to "now". |
| 4 | **S-relay-adapter-node** | P1 (embedded), P4 (standalone) | 3 | §3.2, §11.2, §11.3 · Fastify + ws + better-sqlite3; embedded in wraith-node; `wraith-relay` CLI; Docker | R1 The Node adapter shall implement `Storage`, `Sockets`, `Timers`, `Http` and pass the conformance suite. · R2 WHEN started in Local mode, the embedded relay shall bind only to `127.0.0.1`. · R3 WHEN `wraith-relay init` runs, it shall generate `SETUP_TOKEN` and VAPID keys and write them with mode 0600. · R4 The standalone relay shall serve the PWA build from the same origin as `/ws`. |
| 5 | **S-relay-adapter-cf** | P4 | 3 | §3.1, §3.6, §4.1 · Worker + SpaceDO; Hibernation; auto-response; single alarm multiplex; static assets; Deploy button | R1 The Space DO shall accept WebSockets with `ctx.acceptWebSocket` tagged by `memberId`. · R2 WHEN `webSocketClose` or `webSocketError` fires, the DO shall persist `members.last_seen` before returning. · R3 The DO shall maintain at most one alarm, set to the minimum `fire_at` of the `timers` table. · R4 The DO shall not perform application heartbeats that wake the object (SI-13 budget). · R5 WHEN the DO constructor runs, migrations shall be applied under `blockConcurrencyWhile`. |
| 6 | **S-relay-lease** | P3 (manual move), P8 (auto failover) | 3 | §7.4.2, §7.7 · fencing, revoke→release, timeout rule, self-fencing contract with node clock | R1 The relay shall grant at most one `active` lease per `session_key`. · R2 WHEN a lease changes holder, the relay shall increment `lease_epoch` and shall discard envelopes carrying an older `leaseEpoch`. · R3 The relay shall not transfer a cloud-host lease by timeout before `last_seen + 45s`. · R4 WHEN `cloud.move` is received, the relay shall send `lease.revoke` and shall grant the new lease only after `lease.release` or the timeout rule. · R5 The sprite shall not submit work to Kiro WHILE `now − freshAt ≥ 35s`. · R6 IF the node's monotonic clock helper is unavailable, THEN the node shall disable its `cloud-host` capability. |
| 7 | **S-pairing-trust** | P1 (local setup), P4 (QR/ticket/revoke), P7 (watch code), P8 (recovery/rotate) | 2, 3 | §3.4, §12.2 · setup token, owner key, tickets, HMAC secret out-of-band, roster versions, revoke, recovery, rotate | R1 `POST /space` shall succeed only once per relay and only with a valid `SETUP_TOKEN`. · R2 WHEN a new member (node, device, or both in a Join) completes pairing, it shall pin the owner public key only after verifying `HMAC(secret, ownerPub‖spaceId‖rosterVersion‖SHA-256(JCS(rosterEntries sorted by memberId)))` and that its own entry's `signPub` equals its own key with the expected kind and caps. · R2b The owner device shall build a new member's roster entry only from the out-of-band QR/code content, never from `pair.offer` fields. · R3 The node shall reject any roster whose version is lower than the pinned version. · R4 WHEN a member is revoked, the relay shall close its sockets and nodes shall permanently reject its signatures. · R5 Every pairing payload (QR or text code, including the watch) shall use the `WRP1-` format carrying the full public key(s) and the 128-bit secret; no fingerprint-only code shall exist. · R7 A ticket shall be consumed when an offer binds to it, not when a page containing it loads, and shall bind at most one offer of 1–2 members. · R8 WHERE Wraith.app joins, the secret shall pass from the node to the app only through the sidecar's stdout pipe, never through the relay, the local channel or a localhost HTTP endpoint. · R9 WHERE the new member is a device, its caps shall be the owner's choice; `caps` in the offer shall be ignored. · R6 WHEN the owner rotates, every node shall require a new out-of-band secret before trusting the new owner key. · R10 The node shall create `node.key` only when it is absent and shall reuse it for every pairing. · R11 The node shall pin each Space's owner in `spaces/<spaceId>/trust.json` and the verifier shall read only the Space selected by `node.json relay.spaceId`. · R12 IF a pairing or unpair is requested while §3.4 item 7's Switch rule does not hold, THEN the node shall refuse with `busy{reasons[]}` and change nothing. · R13 The node shall accept relay changes only through `control.sock` from the same OS user or the CLI writing files with no node running; no protocol message, HTTP endpoint or `/local/v1/ws` call shall change `node.json relay`. |
| 8 | **S-node-core** | P1 | 1, 2, 3 | §4.3, §6.3, §6.5, §10.2, §11.1 · relay-link, outbox, verifier, herdr-client (herdr-core), pane-manager, sprite-hub, agents, redactor, doctor | R1 The node shall verify every signed command against the pinned owner key and roster before acting (SI-3). · R2 The node shall record `meta.id` of accepted commands for 8 days and reject repeats with `E_REPLAY`. · R3 WHEN the relay is unreachable, the node shall keep operating in local-only mode and buffer outgoing persisted events on disk up to 50MB. · R4 WHEN a sprite reports `prompt.started` and then disconnects, the node shall not resend that prompt and shall emit `E_PROMPT_PARTIAL`. · R5 The node shall run the redactor on every outbound body (SI-8). · R6 IF an agent launch config contains `--trust-all-tools` or `--trust-tools=*`, THEN the node shall refuse to spawn it (SI-2). · R7 WHEN herdr-core sends `missed`, the node shall rebuild its pane view from the terminals in that notification and drop events with `seq` up to its `seq`; WHEN a pane slot ends without a recorded close intent and not by `killed`, `spawn_pending` or `server_stop`, the node shall adopt a live pane carrying the slot's `spawn_token` or else respawn its sprite once (compare-and-set on `bot_sessions.pane_id`) with ids only, backing off; a 4th respawn within 10 minutes is not made and the slot gets intent `failed`, and shall act on a stored pane address only while the connected core's id equals the stored `core_id` (§7.6). · R8 The sprite shall strip `HC_*` and `HERDR_*` variables from the environment of the agent it spawns, and the node shall never call herdr-core's `machine.*`, `file.*`, `machine.exec` or `doc.*` methods. · R9 WHEN a verified `node.configure` arrives, the node shall persist it atomically, send `config` to every running sprite, and announce `node.configured`; it shall also announce `node.configured` on every `hello`. · R10 WHEN the node pauses, it shall send `node.paused{true}` before stopping; WHEN its own machine key is revoked, it shall stop sprites, delete `node.key` and exit. · R11 The node shall send, replay and query only rows whose `space_id` equals the active Space (`role='main'` sessions excepted), and on a relay switch shall turn Autopilot off for every bot and delete all `grants`. |
| 9 | **S-sprite-acp** | P1 | 1, 8 | §4.4, §6.5, §7.1, §7.6, §9 · ACP client, renderer, prompt queue, ring buffer, lifecycle, input modes, OSC 7501 status | R1 The sprite shall process prompts strictly one at a time in FIFO order. · R2 WHEN the agent exits with non-zero status, the sprite shall restart it with backoff, increment `epoch` and call `session/load`; after 3 failures in 10 minutes it shall report `failed`. · R3 WHEN the sprite or agent restarts, all open approvals of the previous epoch shall be resolved as `expired`. · R4 The sprite shall accept approval answers only from the node over `sprite.sock` and shall provide no in-pane approve command (DECISION-2). · R5 Prompts typed in the pane shall be tagged `by:"pane-unverified"`. · R6 The sprite shall report its state to herdr-core only as OSC 7501 on its own stdout, and shall write none while a Terminal-mode TUI owns the pane. · R7 The sprite shall request engine v3 and shall check `agentCapabilities.loadSession` before calling `session/load`. · R8 The sprite shall exit with status 0 only after the node acked its `closing` report or told it to stop, and with status 75 on SIGTERM, SIGHUP, a TTY read error or `E_SPRITE_DUPLICATE` (§7.6). · R9 The sprite's line reader shall strip bracketed-paste markers (`\e[200~`, `\e[201~`) that `terminal.send` adds when the program enabled bracketed paste, and shall treat the text between them as one input with newlines kept as text, never parsing a `/command` from inside a paste. |
| 10 | **S-approval** | P1 | 3, 8, 9 | §7.2, §14.2 · ULID approvalId, epoch, requestHash, timeout at sprite only, hook fallback | R1 An approval shall resolve to an allow-kind option only upon a verified `approval.respond` from a device or watch, or a node Autopilot decision that satisfies every rule of §7.2.1 (SI-1). · R7 The node shall never answer `allow_always`, `reject_*`, a spawn request, a group-seat request, or a call it cannot classify, on its own. · R2 WHEN no verified answer arrives within the timeout (default 30 min), the sprite shall answer `reject_once` (or `cancelled`) and mark `by:"timeout"`. · R3 WHEN a response arrives after resolution or with a stale epoch, the node shall emit `approval.rejected_late` with the reason. · R4 The node shall reject a response whose `requestHash` differs from the hash of the request it signed. · R5 The UI state of an approval shall equal what the agent actually received, under all interleavings of answers, timeouts and disconnects. · R6 WHERE the hook fallback is active, the hook shall return "block" when no verified approval exists at hook timeout and shall never return "allow" on timeout. |
| 11 | **S-handoff-groupchat** | P2 | 3, 8, 9 | §6.4, §6.6, §7.3 · packet, MCP tools, context-builder, capability tags, cards | R1 `wraith_mention` shall produce a signed `room.handoff` whose `replyTo` is the prompt the calling bot is currently executing. · R2 WHEN a bot's turn ends without `wraith_reply`, the sprite shall send the final message as `room.return`, EXCEPT for prompts of kind `return` or `summary` and prompts marked transferred. · R3 `wraith_room_context` and the context-builder shall include only events with valid signatures (SI-6). · R4 IF the target bot is T0, THEN the relay shall reject the handoff with `E_TIER_UNSUPPORTED`. · R5 Attachments shall total ≤ 8KB after redaction and shall set `truncated` when cut. · R6 WHEN a bot calls `wraith_spawn_request`, the node shall spawn a bot only after a device-signed `allow_once` for the matching node-signed `approval.request{requestKind:"spawn"}`, and a timeout shall spawn nothing. · R7 WHEN a bot is added to a group, the node shall start a seat bound to that room (a seat process, or for `seats:"swap"` a session in the bot's one process), seeded with a user-visible summary unless native fork was chosen for that group. · R8 The node shall grant the workspace write lock to at most one session of a bot at a time, only after an `allow_*` answer, and shall release it on completion, failure, pane exit, epoch bump, cancel, kill, transfer of the holder's prompt (`prompt.transferred`), seat close or expiry, in both ACP mode and hook mode (§7.2 step 5). · R9 Events of a prompt shall carry that prompt's `meta.room`, and replies shall be posted only to that chat. · R10 IF a user mentions a non-member in a group, THEN the relay shall reject it with `E_NOT_MEMBER`; IF a cloud bot is added to a group, THEN it shall reject with `E_CLOUD_NO_GROUPS`. · R11 WHERE an agent's `seats` is `swap`, the sprite shall switch to the prompt's own session before each prompt and no prompt of one chat shall run in another chat's session; WHERE it is `none`, adding the bot to a group shall fail with `E_SEATS_UNSUPPORTED`. (Swap is built in P2 only if Q33a failed for Claude or Kiro, else P9.17.) · R12 Seat summaries shall be posted as node-signed `bot.summary` and labelled as agent output in the main session's context. |

| 19 | **S-desktop-tauri** | P5 | 18, 8 | §4.6, §14.3 · sidecar, tray, notifications, shortcut, remote mode, release workflow | R1 WHEN the app starts and no healthy node answers on 127.0.0.1:7777, it shall spawn the sidecar; otherwise it shall attach to the existing node. · R2 WHEN the app quits, it shall terminate the sidecar it spawned within 5 seconds. · R3 Native notification actions shall open the request in the window before any signing occurs. · R4 The tray badge shall equal the number of open approvals across rooms. · R5 Release artifacts shall be built for macOS universal and Linux without requiring signing secrets. · R6 WHILE the relay is unreachable, the app shall receive approval requests for bots on its own machine and deliver signed responses directly to the local node over 127.0.0.1, which shall verify them exactly as relay-delivered commands. · R7 WHEN joining a Space, the app shall obtain the pairing payload only from the sidecar's stdout and shall verify its own roster entry before pinning the owner key. |

**P9 specs** (created when reached): S-tier1-adapters, S-mobile-capacitor, S-watch-apple, S-tier2-claude, S-hc-console, S-watch-others, S-windows

**Steering referenced by every spec (`.kiro/steering/`):** `product.md` (differentiators 1–7, G1–G11) · `tech.md` (stack §16; prohibitions: do not parse ANSI for chat, do not auto-approve outside Autopilot (§7.2.1), do not trust-all, do not declare schemas outside protocol) · `structure.md` (§17) · `security.md` (SI-1…SI-14 as plain sentences, same meaning as §12.1) · `ux-theme.md` (§14.6) · `agent-loop.md` (§22 condensed: decision authority, stop conditions, PHASE-STATUS/RESULTS/DECISIONS-PENDING formats)

---
## 21. Test Strategy per phase 💡

### 21.1 Property-based tests (fast-check; keep all of v3.5 + additions)

| # | Property | Module | Phase |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `hello{lastSeq}` + any replay yields every persisted event for every seq with no duplicates | relay hub/sequencer | P1 |
| 2 | node outbox + any disconnect/reconnect: every event reaches the relay completely with no duplicates (dedupe by `id`) | relay-link | P1 |
| 3 | ring buffer replayed any number of times gives the same result | sprite-hub | P1 |
| 4 | for any sequence of handoff/return, depth does not exceed the limit and `return` does not automatically create a new prompt | loop-guard | P2 |
| 5 | an approval without a **correctly signed** answer from a device/watch (or a valid Autopilot decision, #23) can **never** end with an allow-kind option (SI-1), in every sprite mode including `--mode sdk` with permissive Claude settings files | verifier + sprite | P1 (sdk: P9.14) |
| 6 | modify any byte in `meta`/`body` of a signed command → always rejected · modify `relay.*` → no effect · resend the same `meta.id` (even after 7 days) → executed once · revoked key → always rejected · `requestHash` mismatch → always rejected | verifier | P0–P1 |
| 7 | answers arriving concurrently over several paths give one result, and an old-epoch answer can **never** reach the agent | sprite | P1 |
| 8 | **the `approval.resolved` option the UI sees = the decision committed at the sprite, always; the agent receives exactly that option, or (crash/cancel while `waiting_write`) receives `cancelled` and the request is re-issued under a new epoch** (including timeout racing an answer, and link drops) | relay approvals + sprite | P1 |
| 9 | however ping, disconnects, DO hibernate/restart and clocks interleave, **a session has at most one active lease holder** · envelopes with an old `leaseEpoch` are not accepted · **there is no window where the old host passes self-fence after the relay grants a new lease** (model: ping sent ≤ relay receives, fence 35s, expiry 45s, unbounded pong delay), for both the timeout and revoke→release cases · handoffs from cloud created by two hosts from the same message get the same id | lease-authority + relay-link | P3 (revoke), P8 (timeout) |
| 10 | for any socket close/hibernate/redeploy, the lease is not taken over before `last_seen + 45s` | presence + lease-authority | P4 |
| 11 | context sent to an agent contains only events with valid signatures (SI-6) | context-builder | P2 |
| 12 | for any cloud-host failover, a prompt whose marker is already in history is not resent | cloud-host + sprite | P3 |
| 13 | **Adapter conformance:** all relay-related properties pass on the in-memory, Node and Cloudflare adapters (including loss of in-memory state) | relay adapters | P1 (2 adapters), P4 (3) |
| 14 | zod round-trip of every event type, and JCS gives the same bytes on browser, Worker, Node | protocol + crypto | P0 |
| 15 | loop-guard: whatever `forwardDepth`/`root` the sender supplies has no effect | loop-guard | P2 |
| 16 🆕 | **Cap matrix:** random (cap, command, option) triples, caps including `viewer` → the check result at relay and node always matches §12.2, a watch can never get `allow_always` (SI-9), and a viewer can never sign a write or an approval | verifier + relay roster | P4 (device/viewer/owner); P7 adds the watch HTTP path |
| 17 🆕 | **HTTP API nonce/skew:** a request with a repeated nonce or `ts` out of range → always rejected; a valid request within range → accepted once | relay api | P7 |
| 18 🆕 | **Migrations:** migrating from any version k → latest gives a schema equal to fresh, and fixture data is still readable | relay-core, node | P1+ |
| 19 🆕 | **Redactor:** for secrets produced by the generator (AWS, GitHub, Slack, JWT, PEM) embedded at any position in the body → output contains no substring of the secret | redactor | P2 |
| 20 🆕 | **Deterministic promptId:** same message → same id; different lines → different ids; id matches the ULID regex | cloud-bot | P3 |
| 21 | write lock: under any interleaving of acquire/release/crash/epoch bump/cancel/timeout there is at most one holder per workspace and every lock is eventually released | node write-lock | P2 |
| 22 🆕 | **Pairing payload:** for random keys/secrets, `WRP1-` encode→decode round-trips; a corrupted code fails its CRC; an offer whose `signPub` (either member) differs from the payload is never signed; an HMAC whose entries have their contents swapped (keys, caps or any other field exchanged between entries, or changed) never verifies, while the order in which entries are listed has no effect (they are sorted by `memberId`, §3.4) | pairing | P4 |
| 23 🆕 | **Autopilot:** for random (autopilot on/off/expired/turned off by cancel/freeze/revoke, room kind, turn origin, command segments incl. `sh -c`/pipes/scripts/abs paths, owner patterns, `autopilotOff`, paths, `requestKind`, options) → an automatic allow happens only when every §7.2.1 rule holds; never for group seats, non-`user` origins, spawn requests, hard-floor or enabled default-rule matches, paths outside the write area, unknown shapes, or `allow_always` | node autopilot | P2 |
| 24 🆕 | **Terminal mode:** for random interleavings of `bot.terminal` enter/exit from several devices, driver/TUI exits and crashes, node and herdr-core restarts, lost panes, prompts and approvals → at most one driver process per session at any time, no `approval.request` exists for `(bot, mainSessionId)` between `entering` and the driver's resume and the resume epoch is greater than the entry epoch (approvals of a seat turn already running at entry are unaffected; its writes stay `waiting_write` until the lock is released at the end of the return path or the turn is cancelled), the write lock is held from `entering` until the return path ends (driver resume or `needs_resolve`) and no driver or TUI process exists for that session while it is `needs_resolve`, no seat of that bot starts a new prompt between `entering` and the end of the return path (with a pending `settingsChanged`: until `bot.terminalResolve`), and every queued prompt is delivered exactly once, in order, after the return | node terminal + sprite | P9 |
| 25 🆕 | **Seat watermark** (DECISION-53): for random interleavings of group events, seat prompts, restarts and Crypt resumes → every allowlisted signed event reaches the seat exactly once and in `relay.seq` order, or is covered by the summary line with a count; no thinking/tool commentary is ever included; `delivered_seq` never decreases; two prompts queued while the seat is busy never share an event; a resend reuses the stored block; the triggering event is never inside its own block; a Crypt resume of the same session keeps `delivered_seq` | node context-builder | P2 |
| 26 🆕 | **Media transfer** (P9.19): for random files, chunkings, loss, duplication and reordering of chunks and acks, and one flipped byte → the receiver accepts exactly the bytes whose SHA-256 equals the signed hash and nothing else; the stripper is idempotent (strip(strip(x)) = strip(x)) and leaves no metadata chunk; uploads never write outside `.wraith/media/in/` for any `Basename` |

### 21.2 Integration tests with fakes (`packages/fakes`, run on CI without real Kiro/herdr-core)

| Fake | What it simulates | Required capabilities |
|---|---|---|
| **`fake-acp-agent`** | an agent speaking ACP over stdio | scripted with JSON (`scenario.json`): answers `initialize` (capabilities configurable, including `loadSession`, `mcpCapabilities.http`), `session/new/load/list` (several sessions in one process, for swap mode), sends `agent_message_chunk` on a schedule, `tool_call`+`tool_call_update` (including diff), `session/request_permission` then waits for the answer, `terminal/create`, crashes on command (`exit 1` at turn n), replays history on load (stable/unstable ids switchable), calls a given MCP tool (used to test handoff), a "cloud" mode that replies with lines `@app-v100 …` and `DONE:` |
| **`fake-hc`** | herdr-core socket, JSON-RPC 2.0 NDJSON | `hello` (generation 1; its `methods` come from `spike/hc/schema.json`), `terminal.run/list/get/read/send/keys/kill/rm`, `events.subscribe` with snapshot, `created`/`finished{reason}`/`status`/`labels`, `missed` + snapshot and `heartbeat`; parses OSC 7501 from the fake program's output into `status`; `send` refuses `blocked` without `force`; simulated core restart (socket drops, panes keep running) and holder loss (every pane `finished` with `holder_lost`) · a contract test fails if the node calls a method or param that `spike/hc/schema.json` lacks |
| **`fake-kiro-exec`** | `kiro-cli chat --no-interactive --resume-id` | scripted stdout, exit code, delays, simulated "active in another process" lock |
| **`fake-tui`** | an agent's own TUI in Terminal mode (DECISION-52) | prints a first screen, echoes typed text, exits on `/exit` or `/quit`, appends turns to a session JSONL, can write `.claude/settings.local.json` (allow rule, hook, defaultMode), calls the guard hooks (`/hook/terminal`), can crash mid-turn |
| **`fake-push-service`** | Web Push endpoint | receives POST, checks the VAPID header + decrypts RFC 8291 with the simulated subscription's key; replies 201/404/410 per script |
| **`fake-github`** | GitHub REST (PR list) | returns PRs by branch naming |
| **In-memory relay adapter** | a relay that can `hibernate()`/`redeploy()`/`dropSocket()`/`skewClock()` | used in PBT and integration |

**Scenario suites per phase** (`apps/*/test/integration/*.test.ts`):

| Phase | Main scenarios |
|---|---|
| P1 | spawn→prompt→stream→done · approval allow/reject/timeout/late/stale-epoch · sprite crash mid-prompt (`E_PROMPT_PARTIAL`) · agent crash→respawn→expired approvals · hc `missed` → snapshot · core restart (panes keep running, no respawn) · holder loss → exactly one respawn with ids only (finished event + `missed` snapshot + node restart racing) · `killed` / `close` → Crypt, `pause` → paused then respawned on unpause, `moved` → neither · node killed between spawn and store → next start adopts by `spawn_token` (no second pane) · startup crash loop → `failed` on the 4th respawn in 10 min · `failed` report → no respawn, lease released · `E_SPRITE_DUPLICATE` → the refused sprite exits 75 · every finished pane is `rm`ed · `spawn_pending` → wait, then kill after 3 checks · stale address after a state wipe (new `core.id`) → never read or killed · second sprite `hello` → `E_SPRITE_DUPLICATE` · console poll + take control expiry · redaction on tool output |
| P2 | seat crash releases its lock and expires only its approvals · timeout/reject never creates a `write_locks` row and `lock.acquire` happens only after `allow_*` · (when swap is built in P2) swap-mode seats switch sessions before every prompt and never run one chat's prompt in another's session (fake-acp-agent with `session/load`) · transfer: a relay error leaves the turn running; an ack sends `prompt.transferred`, the turn is cancelled, no auto-return, the return goes to `P1.replyTo` (or only the ReturnCard when it is null) · non-member handoff runs in main session and posts "Worked for…" card · mention→handoff→return · fan-out 2 targets · loop until `E_LOOP_*` · freeze/unfreeze · T0 status cards · `requires` mismatch · ask_human roundtrip · 2 node processes on one relay (dev pairing via `pair --dev --print-code`) |
| P3 | cloud exec/acp modes · `@` parse (including the fake line `@notabot`) · PR detection · Move host revoke→release · force move · stale leaseEpoch dropped · marker dedupe · clock helper missing → cap off · Kiro lock → `E_LEASE_HELD_EXTERNAL` |
| P4 | conformance 3 adapters · pairing ticket/QR/HMAC (including wrong HMAC) · revoke · push subscribe/send/cleanup · quota error → local-only · DO redeploy → grace · rate limits · Origin denied |
| P5 | sidecar spawn/attach/quit · tray badge · notification click → sheet (Playwright + tauri-driver 🧪; if not possible → test the Rust side with unit tests and the WebView with plain Playwright) |
| P7 | every HTTP API endpoint · cap watch · Side Service logic as a pure TS module tested with Vitest (signing, roster verify, inbox merge) · Device App layout functions |
| P8 | crypt list/resurrect/double-resurrect · auto failover timeline (fake clock) · recovery+rotate · migrate export/import checksum · service units (shell test in CI macOS/Linux) |

## 22. Agent-loop Guardrails 💡

### 22.1 Decision authority

| The loop may decide alone | Must record in `DECISIONS-PENDING.md`, then continue with the default | Must stop and wait for a human (blocks only that task) |
|---|---|---|
| Code structure, file/function names, libraries not in §16 if the license allows it (MIT/Apache/BSD/ISC) and < 1MB | Change numeric values in §3.6/§4.2 (timeouts, limits) within ±50%, with a reason from a spike · **excluded:** lease/self-fence timing (15/35/45 s), replay windows (7/8 days), the **default** approval timeout (30 min) and its 1–240 range — these are human-gated (the user changing the per-machine value in Settings, DECISION-41, is not a code change) | Everything in §24.2 that has no usable default |
| Pick a branch per §19.1 when the spike result is clear | UX copy/layout that differs slightly from §14 (record a screenshot) | A spike whose result is "STOP" (Q3+Q3b fail; Q5+Q5c fail affects DECISION-9) |
| Add a new error code in §6.9 (must also update the document) | Add/edit a migration | **Any change that touches SI-1…SI-14, the §12.2 cap matrix, the §6.1 signature scope, the §7.4.2 lease timing** |
| Add PBTs/tests | Move work between phases without changing a DoD | Spending money (Workers Paid, Apple Developer, domain), deploying to a real account, publishing to a public store/npm/Docker Hub/Homebrew tap/winget/Scoop, submitting on Devpost, posting on social |
| Write docs/README | Add a native dependency (needs a build) | Deleting or rewriting the user's files outside the repo (`~/.kiro`, `~/.claude`, `~/.config/herdr-core`, `~/.local/state/herdr-core`) — **exceptions:** (1) product code may create/update/delete only `~/.kiro/agents/wraith-<botId>.json` (DECISION-26); (2) on a device-signed `bot.terminalResolve`, exactly the entries listed in `bot.terminalReturn.settingsChanged`/`model`, in the Claude settings files SI-2 checks, with a backup in `~/.wraith/backups/` first (DECISION-52); tests do both in a temp `HOME` · Running real agents using more than 200 credits/day |
| Run spikes that use only the loop's machine and fakes | Adjust phase order within P7/P8/P9 according to blocks | Changing locked decisions (§24.1) — **forbidden even if it looks better**; write a proposal in DECISIONS-PENDING |

### 22.2 Stop conditions (stop the loop and summarise for the human)

1. A spike gives a STOP result per §19.1
2. Any DoD item of the current phase fails **3 times in a row** after fixes
3. A test tied to SI-\* fails and the fix would require loosening the invariant
4. A credential/account/device is needed that is not available, and there is no other phase with met entry criteria to work on instead
5. This document is found to contradict itself at a point that affects security or a DoD (record §/line and propose a fix; do not guess)
6. Work will exceed the budget: credits > 200/day, Cloudflare spend > US$2 in one day (owner is on Workers Paid; P4-D10 still measures Free-plan-equivalent usage), or any GitHub Actions spend
7. The date reaches 2026-11-26 and P6 has not started → force entry into P6
8. A secret leaks in a commit/log (stop, revoke, notify)

### 22.3 Re-routing when a spike fails

| Fails | Do immediately | Phase affected | Notify human? |
|---|---|---|---|
| Q2 | (pre-check passed) if API panes don't show in Herdr.app, document `hc attach` for G3 at the machine | P1 (S-node-core) | No |
| Q33a | set `seats:"swap"` (Q33c passed) or `"none"` for that agent | P2 (S-handoff-groupchat; swap is then built in P2, not P9.17) | Yes (member chip shows "swap", or the bot is disabled in the group picker); record the result in `spikes/RESULTS.md` and copy P9.17-D1…D3 into P2 in `PHASE-STATUS.md` |
| Q1 | poll 1s / plain text | P1 (S-console) | No (record) |
| Q3 passes, Q3b not needed | – | – | – |
| Q3 fails, Q3b passes | enable hook fallback in S-approval; add hook-endpoint to P1 scope | P1 | Notify (not blocking) |
| Q3 + Q3b fail | **STOP** | all of P1 | ✅ block |
| Q35 | folder-scan fallback for `bot.commands` | P1 | No (record) |
| Q36 | no Claude T1 bot; keep Kiro as the only T1 agent | P9.14 | ✅ Notify |
| Q37 | no Kiro Rewind | P9.14 | Notify (not blocking) |
| Q38 | Grok stays T0 | P9.15 | Notify (not blocking) |
| Q39–Q42 | no Terminal mode for that agent (fork-visit variant if only Q39 fails) | P9.16 | Notify (not blocking) |
| Q43 | launch Kiro without `--agent`, select the agent after `initialize` | P1 | No (record) |
| Q44 | Codex stays T0; P9.17 builds only the swap part | P9.17 | Notify (not blocking) |
| Q45 | (a) swap default/fallback or device-only voice · (b) no watch dictation | P9.18 | Notify (not blocking) |
| Q46 | lower the size caps; video download instead of inline play | P9.19 | Notify (not blocking) |
| Q47, Q48, Q49 | native Windows stays blocked (WSL2 remains the Windows path); Q49 alone → offer only the agents that passed | P9.21 | Notify (not blocking) |
| Q50 | Windows logon task that runs `wsl --exec` instead of systemd; mirrored networking if localhost fails; else P9.20 stays blocked | P9.20 | Notify (not blocking) |
| Q6 | node respawns itself | P1, P8 | No |
| Q10 | build `packages/acp-lite` (+1–2 days) | P1 | No |
| Q11 | stdio shim | P2 | No |
| Q5/Q5b | exec mode | P3 | Notify |
| Q5 + Q5c (final message unreadable) | PR card only | P3; demo scene | ✅ blocks the positioning decision (DECISION-9), but P4/P5 can continue |
| Q18 | retry 60s; demo scene uses Move host | P3/P8 | Notify |
| Q19 | write push ourselves first (≤ 2 days) → if that still fails, in-app | P4 | Notify |
| Q20 | adjust limits | P4 | No |
| Q21 | docs recommend Paid | – | Notify (the cost belongs to the human) |
| Q22 | heartbeat 60/170/180 | P4, P8 (PBT timing changes per the formula) | Notify |
| Q24a | keychain path (Rust signs after showing the request) | P5 | No |
| Q24b both SEA and bun | resources folder; if that still fails → propose Electron | P5 | ✅ before switching to Electron |
| Q25/Q25b | long-poll with the value that passes | P7 | No |
| Q26 | noble + settingsStorage (check bundle size) | P7 | No |
| Q27a/Q27b | layer 1 only (the planned default) | P7 (drop P7-D9) | Notify (not blocking) |
| Q28 | layout unit tests + human checks on the real device | P7 | No |
| Q9 | T0 local-llm | P9.1 | No |
| Q29 | YubiKey/FIDO2 or park P9.12 | P9.12 | ✅ (needs a YubiKey or buying an Apple Developer ID) |

### 22.4 Security invariants (summary; details in §12.1)
Never loosen SI-1…SI-14, whether for tests, demos or speed · There is no flag/env that disables signature checks, enables auto-approve (Autopilot is a device-signed per-bot setting with the §7.2.1 rules, never a flag/env), accepts `--trust-all-tools`, skips the redactor or trusts all origins · Allowed "dev" values: self-signed cert for test HTTPS, relay on localhost, fakes · If the loop finds that a DoD cannot be met without loosening an SI → stop condition 3

### 22.5 Loop status file formats
- `docs/PHASE-STATUS.md`: table `phase | DoD id | status (todo/pass/fail/blocked-human) | evidence (test name/commit) | date`
- `docs/spikes/RESULTS.md`: §19 format
- `docs/DECISIONS-PENDING.md`: `id | date | question | default taken | impact if changed | resolved?`
- commit message prefix: `[P<n>][S-<spec>] …` · every PR cites a DoD id

## 23. Traceability: differentiator → Component → Phase

| Differentiator | Components | Phase |
|---|---|---|
| 1 Kiro Cloud as a bot + `@` delegation | §5.1, sprite remote load / Cloud Status Bot, cloud-host lease (§7.4.2), `@` parser (§7.4.1), Handoff Packet + git refs | P3 (+P8 failover) |
| 2 Per-command approval from phone/watch | sprite requestPermission / hook fallback, signed approval + requestHash + node-signed request + verifier, relay approvals, Web Push, HTTP API + cap watch | P1, P4, P7 |
| 3 Group chat | router + loop-guard, Handoff Packet, MCP, context-builder | P2 |
| 4 Multiple vendors | Tier T0/T1/T2, Claude Agent SDK driver, Grok (T0/T1) | P1, P2, P9 |
| 5 terminal = herdr pane | console S3, sprite renderer, take control | P1 |
| 6 Crypt | crypt-scanner/index, lease-authority, node respawn | P8 |
| G5/G8/G10 no single point of failure + standalone + self-run remote + desktop | relay-core + 3 adapters, outbox, pairing, Deploy button, Tauri | P1, P4, P5 |
