## 24. Decisions

### 24.1 Settled (do not reopen; from v3.5.4 §20 + new facts 2026-11-01)

| # | Topic | Agreed |
|---|---|---|
| DECISION-1 | Scope of differentiator 1 | Cloud bot + bots on every machine are full members |
| DECISION-2 | Approval from the pane | Disabled (only exception: Terminal mode, DECISION-52, where the agent's own TUI asks) |
| DECISION-3 | ACP engine | v3 for every bot |
| DECISION-4 | primary | No primary; all nodes are equal |
| DECISION-5 | If Q5 fails | Cloud Status Bot |
| DECISION-6 | E2E | Not done (signatures remain) |
| DECISION-7 | Public hosted relay | Not done |
| DECISION-8 | Web Push | May degrade to in-app if Q19 fails |
| DECISION-9 | Cloud `@` delegation | Headline feature; try for real first; may fall back only after notifying the user |
| DECISION-10 | relay | `relay-core` + adapters, Local is the default |
| DECISION-11 | Scope vs deadline | Do not cut scope; use the P0–P9 order |
| DECISION-12 | Desktop | Tauri v2 + sidecar; Electron as the fallback |
| DECISION-13 | Desktop distribution | Build from source + unsigned release; signed later |
| DECISION-14 | Mobile & Wearables | PWA → Capacitor; first watch is Amazfit Active 2 (Square) via Zepp Mini Program + HTTP API + cap watch |
| DECISION-15 🆕 | Phone paired with the watch | **iPhone** (user confirmed) → design around iOS limits; layer 1 (mirror notification → open Mini Program) is always the main path |
| DECISION-16 🆕 | Contest Kiro credits | The user **has already submitted the request form**; not the loop's job; the loop only monitors usage |
| DECISION-17 🆕 | Language of the repo's UI/specs/docs | English (Kiroween rules) · **This English document is the master** (v4.1); the Thai v4.0 is archived outside the repo |
| DECISION-18 🆕 | Approval path when the relay is down (Fable's proposal, 2026-11-02) | **No passphrase in the pane** (a script with shell access can steal the code, and can force the "relay down" condition to downgrade security) · Use a **Touch ID approver as a device in the roster** instead → SI-1 needs no change, DECISION-2 (approval from the pane disabled) stays · **optional, done as the very last item (P9.12)** |
| DECISION-19 🆕 | Repo visibility | **Public from the first push** (2026-11-02) · gated by secret scanning/push protection + the `secret-scan` hook (SI-12) |
| DECISION-20 🆕 | Bots creating bots | A bot may **ask** to spawn a bot with `wraith_spawn_request`; the node turns it into a node-signed approval card; only a device-signed `allow_once` spawns it (watch may approve) · only agents marked `spawnableByBots` (Settings) · counts toward the requester's loop-guard root budget · ≤ 3 bot-requested bots alive per requester subtree · SI-1/SI-2/SI-7/SI-10 unchanged (P2) |
| DECISION-21 🆕 | herdr in the desktop app | **Not bundled.** The desktop app bundles `wraith-node` (+ relay-core Local) and `wraith-sprite`; first-run checks herdr-core and kiro-cli and offers a one-click install of herdr-core (runs its official installer after the user confirms; C75) |
| DECISION-22 🆕 | Approvals when the relay is down, at the machine | **Desktop direct local channel** over `127.0.0.1` in P5 (2026-11-02): device-signed `approval.respond`/`room.freeze` for this machine's bots, same verifier, no SI change · the Touch ID approver (P9.12) stays optional on top of it |
| DECISION-23 🆕 | Name for the per-bot pane process and the avatar motif | **sprite** (2026-11-02): `wraith-sprite` in code; small glowing-wisp sprites in the UI · no mascot name for now |
| DECISION-24 🆕 | Bot identity | **Workspace-centric** (2026-11-02): one bot per folder (`nodeId:realpath`), deterministic botId, session resumed on reopen, folder tree = bot hierarchy, parent may write anywhere below it, rooms stay independent, first-run New-bot flow picks the folder (§4.8) |
| DECISION-25 🆕 | Bot kinds and write scope | Project (any folder incl. a root; leading is derived from the tree, no separate orchestrator kind — revised by DECISION-29) / Project assistant (`<project>/.wraith/assistants/<name>`, git-excluded) / Standalone assistant (`~/.wraith/scratch/<name>`) · read anywhere under roots, write only in own workspace (parents also below them) · bots may request new bots only inside their own subtree; non-lead requesters are flagged and the lead is told · Move to folder keeps the botId (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-26 🆕 | Profiles | **Removed** (2026-11-02). A bot = workspace + agent (detected CLI) + optional instructions (empty by default, editable later). No preset roles. Wraith manages launch configs and never lets instructions widen tool access (SI-2) |
| DECISION-27 🆕 | Bot names | Name ≠ identity (2026-11-02): prefilled from the folder, editable at creation and later, unique in the Space, `@mention` uses it, path always shown under it |
| DECISION-28 🆕 | Chats | Each bot has its own chat (created with it); groups are created separately by picking existing bots and a bot can be in many groups; replies go back to the chat the prompt came from (sessions per DECISION-31/32); no room choice when creating a bot (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-29 🆕 | Roots and leading | `workspaceRoots` is a list proposed at first start (never `~` by default); projects elsewhere are added as their own root (by path, desktop picker, or `wraith-node open`). No orchestrator kind: any project bot leads the bots in its sub-folders, including existing ones (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-30 🆕 | One UI, many hosts | Same PWA build everywhere; host detected once (`tauri`/`browser-local`/`browser-remote`/`mobile`); host-only features gated by `can()` with documented fallbacks, never broken or silently disabled (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-31 🆕 | Who represents a bot in a group | **A group seat** started by Wraith (not the agent's own subagent feature): separate process + session in the same workspace per group, seeded with a summary (native fork when available), one write lock per workspace across the bot's sessions, summaries back to its own chat; swap-mode fallback (one process switching sessions, DECISION-54) if the agent can't run two processes at once; **Kiro Cloud bots don't join groups** (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-32 🆕 | Seats and handoffs refined | Lock taken **after Allow**; seat seed = summary the user can see/edit (native fork opt-in per group); **bot handoffs run in the target's main session** (colleague memory) with a "Worked for…" card in its own chat — no per-handoff sessions, also for cloud handoffs; **SI-10 covers text from any bot**, cloud only adds a label (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-33 🆕 | Allow always vs the write lock | While a bot has ≥ 2 live sessions, `allow_always` is not offered for write-kind tools, so every write passes the lock; single-session bots keep it. Group chat stays **mention-driven** (no listen-to-everything mode) (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-34 🆕 | Typing into a group seat | Not allowed: seat panes are view-only (console and at the machine); messages to a group come only from the group chat (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-35 🆕 | Files and Changes | Read-only views per bot workspace, opened from small icon buttons in the **bot chat** header (not tabs, not in groups; C42), viewable from any device through the relay (option A) with device signatures, no persistence, deny-list, redaction and 256 KB caps; Changes in P2 (with per-tool-call attribution), Files in P9.13; edits only by asking the bot (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-36 🆕 | Add machine UX | Ticket screen with a copyable command; QR + text pairing code from the node (paste on a desktop owner, scan on a phone; the code never goes through Wraith); Wraith.app shows the QR itself; Local mode cannot add machines and guides the switch to an external relay, where the machine creates or joins a Space (§3.4 item 7; 2026-11-02, C72) |
| DECISION-37 🆕 | Add device | Pairing link (ticket) → device shows QR + code + fingerprint → owner chooses Full / Approve only / View only (`viewer` cap) → Pair; no password login; Wraith.app join pairs machine + app together; watch code from the Zepp Settings page (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-38 🆕 | Machine vs device | A computer is a machine only while its node runs with a roster key; Wraith.app Join has "Also run bots on this Mac" (default from doctor); upgrade = Run bots on this Mac (same Add machine confirmation), downgrade = revoke the machine role only; `/machines` grouped by computer (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-39 🆕 | Phone home | Telegram-style chat list as `/`, sorted by latest event with waiting chats first; Search / New / Settings icons top right, no bottom tab bar; Back on chat, group and settings returns Home; Machines & devices inside Settings (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-40 🆕 | Settings | Theme default System; notification groups Needs you / Bot finished / Bot stopped, security alerts always on; approval timeout per machine; quick messages ≤ 8; Touch ID waits for P8; Release notes instead of an updater; tray icon cannot be hidden; Run bots on this Mac = pause, not revoke (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-41 🆕 | Approval timeout setting | Changed from the owner device or a Full device with signed `node.configure` (1–240 min, per machine), applies to new approval requests only (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-42 🆕 | Pairing payload and Join | One `WRP1-` payload (full public key(s) + 128-bit secret) for every member, no fingerprint-only code; Wraith.app Join = one ticket, one offer of two members, one HMAC; the node generates the secret and hands it to the app only over the sidecar stdout pipe (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-43 🆕 | Local mode limits | "Cannot add machines" is a UI rule from reachability only; relay-core accepts any paired member, so a second node on the same machine works (P2 tests); in Local mode "Run bots on this Mac" becomes **Pause all bots** so the relay keeps running; pause is announced with node-signed `node.paused` (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-44 🆕 | `/` command picker | Agents advertise their own skills/commands (ACP `available_commands_update` → node-signed `bot.commands`); the composer shows Wraith actions and the bot's skills; in groups `@bot /`; picking only inserts text, sending is a normal signed `user.message`, tools still need approval (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-45 🆕 | Esc to stop | Desktop only: Esc closes popups first; then Esc Esc within 2s sends `bot.cancel` (ACP `session/cancel`, same for every agent); in a group with several working bots a "Stop which bot?" chooser, never stop-all without a click; Console Take control passes Esc to the terminal (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-46 🆕 | Stop vs Freeze | Interrupt lives in the send button (■ Stop while a bot works, Send again once you type; "Stop which bot?" in groups); Freeze (stop handoffs, not the current turn) uses a ❄ icon so it is never mistaken for Stop (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-47 🆕 | Rewind and the Claude driver | Rewind uses each agent's own rewind only (no fork/summary fallback; agents without one get no Rewind): Claude via the **Claude Agent SDK**, which replaces the ACP adapter for Claude bots, and Kiro via `/checkpoint` if it works over ACP (Q37); options = restore code and conversation / conversation / code as the agent allows; bot chats only (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-48 🆕 | Grok Build | Supported as T0 now; T1 (`grok agent stdio`) only if Q38 shows approvals cannot be skipped (ACP permission requests or a blocking `PreToolUse` hook); the node refuses to launch it with always-approve/auto/yolo; P9.15 (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-49 🆕 | Autopilot (bypass for AI loops) | The user changed "never auto-approve": a bot may be put on Autopilot by a device-signed `bot.configure`; the agent still asks for every call and the **node** answers `allow_once` only for turns the user started, never after handoffs/cloud/pane text, never for the always-ask list or outside the write area, optionally time-limited; agent-native bypass flags stay forbidden (SI-2) (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-50 🆕 | Autopilot scope and classifier | Autopilot works **only in a bot's own chat** (never groups); a fail-closed classifier decides; a hard floor always asks (sudo, `sh -c`, scripts, outside the write area, secret files, spawn requests, `hc`/`herdr` commands); default rules (git push, destructive git, publish, `rm -r`, pipe-to-shell, network-out, remote installs, system) are on but the owner may switch each off; it turns off on Stop/Freeze/pause/device revoke; ≤ 24 h (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-51 🆕 | Respond (nudge) in groups | Long-press a member chip (desktop: hover button) or the bot card's **Respond to the chat** sends a signed `room.nudge`; the bot reads the group's verified events since its watermark (DECISION-53) and answers there; never Autopilot-eligible (2026-11-02) |
| DECISION-52 🆕 | Terminal mode | The owner or a Full device can hand a T1 local bot's main session to the agent's own TUI in the same pane and back (`bot.terminal`); one driver at a time; guarded launch (manual mode, no auto/bypass); write lock held; messages queue; Autopilot off; return card with imported turns (unverified), git diff, settings and session-id checks. TUI-only `/` commands, unknown names and unknown agent updates point to it. Approvals inside the TUI are the one exception to DECISION-2 (SI-1, boundary 8). Per agent after Q39–Q42; P9.16 (2026-11-03) |
| DECISION-53 🆕 | Group context and handoff modes (adopted from OpenBot's Channels) | Each group seat keeps a delivery watermark (`delivered_seq`) and gets only the signed group events it has not seen, bounded with a summary line; `wraith_room_context` pages back; handoffs are `assign` (result returns) or `transfer` (whole task handed over, result answers the original requester); thinking and tool commentary never enter shared context. Not adopted: LLM routing of unmentioned messages (Wraith stays mention-driven + Respond) and resources declared by the model (the node's write lock stays the only guard) · C64: transfer ends the turn hard after the relay ack; the watermark moves when the node builds the prompt; summary prompts carry the block (2026-11-03/04) |
| DECISION-54 🆕 | How seats run when an agent can't run two processes in one folder | `agents.json` `seats: process | swap | none` per agent from Q33a–c; `swap` = one process, one session per chat, switched before every prompt; **no shared-session mode**; Claude and Kiro are `process` until Q33a runs at P1 entry; the swap protocol is specified now and built in P2 only if Q33a fails for Claude or Kiro, otherwise as P9.17 with the Codex T1 driver (C65); seats queue during Terminal mode (2026-11-04) |
| DECISION-55 🆕 | Voice input | Dictation only: speech becomes an editable draft in the composer (or a confirmed watch message ≤ 280), never sent automatically and never an approval; engines: **Browser speech** (Web Speech API, may use the browser vendor's servers) or the node's whisper.cpp; per device **Automatic** / Wraith node / Browser speech (Whisper large-v3, Thonburian distill fallback), cloud only by owner opt-in; spoken names fixed to `@handles`; audio never stored; a realtime call mode is not planned. P9.18, spike Q45 (2026-11-04) |
| DECISION-56 🆕 | Switching the relay | Only on a machine (Wraith.app driving its own node, or `wraith-node`), never by a remote signed command; URL-first wizard that detects Create vs Join from `/healthz`; on Create it asks who holds the owner key (**Create on my phone** default, or **Create here**); going back to Local restores the Local Space; per-Space storage (`spaces/<spaceId>/`, `space_id` rows, one `node.key`, window keys per Space); CLI in P4, wizard in P5 (C70/C71, 2026-11-04) |
| DECISION-57 🆕 | Images and video | Bots generate (their own tools or the owner's GPU pipeline in a bot's instructions); Wraith only moves media: `wraith_share_file` → node-signed `media.shared` card with a ≤ 32 KB thumbnail in history; full image (≤ 20 MB) or 720p video preview (≤ 50 MB) streamed from the node on demand, chunks unsigned but checked against a node-signed SHA-256; relay never stores media; metadata stripped; devices upload images/video to a bot's `.wraith/media/in/`; originals of video open only on the machine (Wraith.app); `wraith_share_file` asks like `wraith_post`; uploads in bot chats only; no push or watch item for media. P9.19, spike Q46 (C73/C74, 2026-11-05) |
| DECISION-58 🆕 | herdr-core instead of the herdr TUI (C75) | Panes run in **herdr-core** (`hc` ≥ 0.1.18, protocol generation 1), the runtime under the owner's Herdr.app; the herdr TUI server is not supported (it does not share terminals with herdr-core). The node uses only `hello`, `server.status`, `terminal.run/list/get/read/send/keys/kill/rm` and `events.subscribe` over the local socket (and `terminal.attach{observe}` from P9.5), and never `machine.*`, `file.*`, `machine.exec`, `doc.*`, templates or `on`: Wraith reaches other machines only through its own signed, approved paths. Panes are found by labels (with a `wraith.spawn` token for adoption) but identified by `core.id` + address only. Status out = OSC 7501 from the sprite. No resume argv: herdr-core keeps programs through core restarts and never re-runs a lost one, so the node respawns sprites with ids only (§7.6). Wraith uses the `default` hc session so its panes stay visible to `hc ls` and Herdr.app |
| DECISION-59 🆕 | Install channels and supported systems (C76) | macOS and Linux now; Windows through WSL2 (P9.20), native later (P9.21); every OS-specific call in `packages/platform`, checked in CI · release tarballs + Homebrew tap with formula `wraith-node` (P5, no app, no signing needed) and cask `wraith` (P9.9, signed app only) · headless machines join through an external relay and `wraith-node service install` · sprite protocol additive across one release (2026-11-09) |

### 24.2 Open decisions for the human (short; every item has a default so the loop can continue)

| # | Question | Default if no answer | Must answer before |
|---|---|---|---|
| H1 | Repo name, owner, visibility | ✅ **Answered 2026-11-02:** `github.com/<user>/wraith` where `<user>` = the GitHub account logged in to `gh` on the loop's machine · **public from the first push** (free macOS CI, visible build history) · before that push: secret scanning + push protection on, `secret-scan` hook installed | P0 |
| H2 | License | ✅ **Default confirmed 2026-11-02** · Apache-2.0 (compatible with herdr-core and the contest rules) | P0 |
| H3 | Node name of each machine (used in UI/roster) and `hostPriority` | ✅ **Answered 2026-11-02:** do not hard-code machine names · `nodeId`/`label` default from the hostname as a slug (e.g. `macbook-pro`); on collision append `-2` · changeable with `wraith-node init --name <n>` or in /machines · `hostPriority` default = 1 for every node; the user adjusts it in /machines · ties are broken by the lowest `nodeId` (deterministic) | P1 |
| H4 | Cloudflare account: use `workers.dev` or your own domain | ✅ **Answered 2026-11-02:** `workers.dev` · the user already has **Workers Paid** (Q21 does not block our work, but P4-D10 still measures against the Free plan for other users) | P4 |
| H5 | Apple Developer ID ($99/year) for signed desktop and Capacitor/Apple Watch | ✅ **Answered 2026-11-02: not buying yet** (level 2 unsigned) · revisit when P9.2 starts | P9 |
| H6 | GitHub repo for testing the cloud bot (must be connected to Kiro Cloud) and PAT | ✅ **Default confirmed 2026-11-02** · create test repo `<user>/wraith-playground` (private) and a fine-grained read-only PAT for that repo only (the human must create the PAT) | P3 |
| H7 | Order after P6: P7 (watch) first, or P8 (Crypt/failover) first | ✅ **Default confirmed 2026-11-02** · **P7 first** if the watch is ready and Q25–Q27 are answered, otherwise P8 | After P6 |
| H8 | Bonus: Blog, Social Blitz, "Ghost of Kiroween Past" | ✅ **Answered 2026-11-02:** the loop drafts the blog/posts in P6; the human decides whether to post · **Not eligible for Ghost of Kiroween Past** (never registered in 2025) | P6 |
| H9 | Kiro Crew bridge (a separate `wraith-crew-app` that relays a Crew thread into a Wraith room) | ✅ **Default confirmed 2026-11-02** · **Not before P9.11** · No architecture impact | P9 |
| H10 | Default quick messages for the watch, and approval timeout | ✅ **Default confirmed 2026-11-02** · List in §14.4 · 30 minutes | P7 / P1 |
| H11 | **Claude Agent SDK** (DECISION-47): accept its license (Anthropic terms, not MIT/Apache/BSD/ISC, so the loop may not decide it, §22.1), how it authenticates (your own Claude login if the terms allow personal use, else an API key / LiteLLM), and whether it is bundled in Wraith.app | **Not bundled**: `doctor` offers to install it on the machine when a Claude bot is first created (like `kiro-cli`); uses the user's existing Claude login if Q36 confirms the terms allow it, else asks for an API key; listed in `docs/THIRD_PARTY.md` | P9.14 (Q36) · ✅ **Answered 2026-11-02:** use the default (not bundled; `doctor` installs; own Claude login if Q36 confirms the terms allow it, else API key) |
| H12 | Windows code signing for P9.21 (e.g. Azure Trusted Signing, monthly fee) | **Not buying yet**: no native Windows build is published before P9.21; revisit when Q47–Q49 pass | P9.21 |
